任务背景: 目标是把已有的强预训练非循环扩散 Transformer 转为在深度方向复用同一权重块的循环模型，从而在不重新预训练的前提下获得可调循环数与更低推理开销。

Motivation: 现有循环扩散 Transformer 均从零训练，重复了完整的预训练成本，而高质量预训练非循环扩散模型已存在，这一成本浪费未被解决。

Method: 学生块由教师块初始化，将蒸馏建模为向教师收敛的不动点迭代：每一次循环的输出都被训练逼近教师输出，且每次循环视为深度方向上 ODE 的一步。因此单一模型在测试时可运行任意循环数而无需重训，并可沿深度维套用高阶 ODE 求解器；同时利用不同循环数输出构造 loop guidance。

实验效果: 在 SD3-medium 上，约 37% 参数量的循环学生用 2–4 次循环即在 GenEval、ImageReward、PickScore、HPSv2.1 平均上匹配教师，2 次循环约用教师 58% 计算；四步 SD3-medium 上 loop guidance 将四项指标平均从 88.2 提升至 99.2。

Abstract: Looped transformers reuse one block of shared weights along the depth of the network. Recent looped diffusion transformers are trained from scratch, which repeats the full pretraining cost although strong pretrained non-looped diffusion transformers exist. We propose Distill to Loop (Dooop), which distills a pretrained non-looped diffusion transformer into a looped student whose blocks are initialized from the teacher's. We cast distillation as a fixed-point iteration toward the teacher, in which the output after every loop is trained toward the teacher, and each loop is one step of an ordinary differential equation in depth. As a result, one model runs any number of loops at test time without retraining, and the higher-order ODE solvers of diffusion model sampling can be applied along the depth dimension for better results. Meanwhile, because a looped model produces an output after every loop, we also construct loop guidance between the outputs of different loop counts, which improves the generated samples at little extra compute. On SD3-medium, a looped student with about 37% of the teacher's parameters matches the teacher on average over GenEval, ImageReward, PickScore and HPSv2.1 with two to four loops, and with two loops it uses about 58% of the teacher's compute. And on a four-step SD3-medium, loop guidance raises the average of the four metrics from 88.2 to 99.2.

</details>


### [71] [WaveSR: Physics-Inspired Wave Evolution Attention for Efficient Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31984)
*Xiaoyan Lei,Biao Luo,Wenlong Zhang,Tingwen Huang,Chunhua Yang,Weihua Gui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出受物理启发的波演化注意力框架 WaveSR，利用二维波动方程的闭式频时解耦解实现高效图像超分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是图像超分辨率重建，即由低分辨率输入恢复高分辨率图像，目标是在保持重建质量的同时提升计算效率。

Motivation: 现有方法缺乏兼具可解释性与高效率的注意力机制；而波动方程的闭式频时解耦解具有线性复杂度、各频率分量可独立演化的特性，尚未被用于高效超分辨率，这构成研究缺口。

Method: 框架含两个模块：波演化注意力（WEA）在FFT域用二维波动方程解析解完成特征交互，各频率分量独立演化，波速c与阻尼系数α可学习；频率自适应前馈网络（FA-FFN）通过可学习谱调制实现自适应频率特征选择。训练上采用结合空域像素损失与频域损失的两阶段混合目标策略。

实验效果: 摘要未报告任何实验数值、基准或对比结果，仅提出该工作为受物理启发神经网络在高效图像超分辨率上的初步探索；理论层面的依据是波动方程闭式解带来的线性复杂度与频率分量独立演化特性。

Abstract: Recently, physics-inspired neural networks have gained widespread attention due to their interpretability. The closed-form, frequency-time decoupled solution of the wave equation offers linear complexity and allows different frequency components to evolve independently, making it a potential alternative solution for efficient image super-resolution. Therefore, a physics-inspired wave evolution attention framework is proposed, namely WaveSR. The WaveSR framework involves two key modules, i.e., wave evolution attention (WEA) and Frequency-Adaptive Feed-Forward Network (FA-FFN). The WEA module achieves feature interactions through the analytical solution of the 2D wave equation in the Fast Fourier Transform (FFT) domain, enabling independent evolution of different frequency components with learnable wave speed \(c\) and damping coefficient \(α\). The FA-FFN module realizes adaptive frequency feature selection through learnable spectral modulation, effectively enhancing feature representation capability. Subsequently, we employ a two-stage training strategy with a hybrid objective that combines a spatial-domain pixel loss and a frequency-domain loss to improve reconstruction quality. Our method provides a preliminary exploration of physics-inspired neural networks for efficient image super-resolution.

</details>


### [72] [InCue: Making Better Use of What Multimodal LLMs Already See at Test Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31987)
*Shuwei Wu,Siyue Yu,Longin Jan Latecki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 InCue，一种在推理阶段仅通过优化已有视觉 token 的偏移、利用模型内部线索（视觉注意力与预测熵梯度）引导注意力指向相关区域的测试时框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 面向多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的视觉问答等任务，输入为图像与问题，模型需给出正确答案；论文目标是提升模型在无需额外视觉输入条件下利用内部证据的能力。

Motivation: 现有主流补救方式是将线索选中的区域放大裁剪后重新输入，但作者发现该增益很大部分并非来自新细节：使用已见过的低分辨率输入裁剪时增益基本保留，甚至仅复制该区域缓存 token 时也部分保留，说明增益部分源于对模型已持有证据的重新关注，而重呈现方法未针对这一点设计。

Method: InCue 将视觉注意力与预测熵梯度视为内部线索，二者耦合形成空间目标区域；随后仅优化现有视觉 token 上的一个偏移量，以引导注意力指向该目标，全程不引入额外视觉输入。

实验效果: 在视觉 token 预算匹配条件下，InCue 取得最优性能，稳定优于近期测试时方法，在四个 MLLM 上于 V$^*$ 提升最高 8.9%、在其困难样本上提升最高 23.4%，增益并可迁移至八个通用基准。

Abstract: Even when multimodal large language models (MLLMs) answer incorrectly, their internal cues often point to the relevant region. A widely used remedy re-presents the cue-selected region as an enlarged crop to supply finer detail. However, we find that much of its gain requires no new detail: it largely survives when the crop comes from the already-seen low-resolution input, and partly even when the region's cached tokens are merely copied. Part of re-presentation's gain thus comes from drawing attention to evidence the model already holds. We propose InCue, a test-time framework that makes better use of this evidence without additional visual input. InCue treats visual attention and predictive-entropy gradients as Internal Cues, couples them into a spatial target, and optimizes only an offset on the existing visual tokens to steer attention toward it. Under matched visual-token budgets, InCue achieves state-of-the-art performance, consistently outperforming recent test-time methods and improving four MLLMs by up to 8.9% on V$^*$ and 23.4% on its hard samples. Its gains carry over to eight general benchmarks. Code will be available at https://github.com/paul-swu/InCue.

</details>


### [73] [Describe, Don't Decide: Correcting Answer Bias in Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31988)
*Shuwei Wu,Longin Jan Latecki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的 D$^3$（Describe, Don't Decide）框架，通过让 MLLM 单独描述实体并在无图条件下做决策，纠正图像比较题中的答案偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是让多模态大模型（MLLM）对图像中两个实体进行比较并给出答案，输入为图像与比较问题，输出为比较判断。实践中此类比较题错误率偏高，目标是提升回答准确率。

Motivation: 现有观察表明，MLLM 在比较实体时容易答错，且偏差并非发生在“看图像”阶段，而是发生在“带图决策”阶段——模型自身对两个实体的描述往往已包含正确答案，说明问题出在决策环节而非感知环节。

Method: D$^3$ 分三步：先让模型分别单独描述每个实体；再在无图像条件下仅依据这些描述做出决策；最后将该决策与模型直接看图给出的答案进行融合。整个过程无需任何训练，直接针对“带图决策”引入的偏差。

实验效果: 无需训练即在 10 个基准、8 个 7-9B MLLM 的全部 79 个模型-基准组合上均有提升；在留出 GQA 比较题上，平均准确率由 72.4% 升至 83.2%（单模型最高 +21.5%），超过 Qwen2.5-VL-32B 直接作答的 82.8%。

Abstract: Comparing two entities in an image, multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) often answer wrongly. We find that the answer bias enters not when the model sees, but when it decides with the image in view: the model's own descriptions of the two entities often already hold the right answer. We therefore propose D$^3$ (Describe, Don't Decide): the model describes each entity alone, decides from these descriptions without the image, and merges this decision with its direct answer. Without any training, D$^3$ improves all 79 model-benchmark pairs across ten benchmarks and eight 7-9B MLLMs. On held-out GQA comparison questions, the mean accuracy of these models rises from 72.4% to 83.2% (up to +21.5% per model), higher than that of Qwen2.5-VL-32B answering directly (82.8%). Code will be available at https://github.com/paul-swu/describe-dont-decide.

</details>


### [74] [Reconstructing Natural Images from Brain Activity during Natural Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31989)
*Tejasvi Kothapalli,Ryan Ressmeyer,Declan Rowley,Gregory D. Horwitz,Jude Mitchell,Alexander C. Huk,Bruno Olshausen,Jacob Yates*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个在灵长类动物自由观看静态自然场景时，从神经活动中重建完整图像的框架，并结合线性方法与基于Transformer与扩散模型的深度学习解码器，刷新了跨模态的图像重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是由大脑神经活动重建所见图像，既服务于脑机接口构建，也用于检验大脑对自然图像的先验。本文以五只猴子的LGN与V1高密度电极阵列记录和眼动数据为输入，输出完整自然场景图像。

Motivation: 既有图像重建研究多要求被试保持眼球不动，属不自然条件；而灵长类在真实观看中持续眼动，且记录的感受野仅覆盖图像的一小部分，因此需要能在自由观看条件下跨眼动、试次与session整合证据的重建框架。

Method: 先在自由观看条件下采集LGN与V1神经活动及眼动，将跨眼动、试次和session的神经活动合并以覆盖全图；给出带1/f先验的简单线性方法，再将其扩展为深度学习方案：对脉冲活动与实测注视位置进行token化，输入Transformer与扩散模型训练解码器重建完整场景。

实验效果: 摘要指出重建质量取决于三点：随观看时间累积的神经证据量、眼动追踪精度和图像先验的选择；作者称该方法在fMRI、EEG、MEG和电生理等多种记录模态上均达到从脑活动重建自然图像的最先进性能，但未给出具体数值指标。

Abstract: Reconstructing images from brain activity has two broad goals. The first is to build brain-computer interfaces. The second is to understand how the brain sees. The eyes deliver incomplete, noisy input, so the brain must fill in the gaps with its expectations of what natural scenes look like. This filling-in relies on the brain's prior over natural images. Image reconstruction lets us test which natural image prior best matches the brain's natural image prior. However, most previous work on image reconstruction relies on an unnatural condition: subjects must hold their eyes still. In reality, primates constantly move their eyes to see the world. We introduce a framework for reconstructing images from neural activity while a primate freely views a static natural scene. We record from five monkeys with high-density electrode arrays in the lateral geniculate nucleus (LGN) and primary visual cortex (V1). The recorded receptive fields cover only a small part of the image. Thus, we combine neural activity across eye movements, trials, and sessions to reconstruct the full image. We first present a simple linear method with a 1/f prior. We then scale this approach up with modern deep learning. We train a decoder by tokenizing spiking activity and measured gaze; these tokens are fed into a transformer and diffusion model to reconstruct the full scene. Three factors determine reconstruction quality: the amount of neural evidence combined over viewing time, the accuracy of eye tracking, and the choice of image prior. Our method sets a new state-of-the-art performance in reconstructing natural images from brain activity, across brain recording modalities: fMRI, EEG, MEG and electrophysiology.

</details>


### [75] [Change the Base, Change the Game: Rethinking Pan-sharpening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32002)
*Jeonghyeok Do,Geunhyuk Youk,Munchurl Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 PanBase：一种无需网络训练、基于传感器物理特性的锐利多光谱起始基，用于替代全色锐化网络中的 4 倍插值残差跳跃基，从而在多个骨干网络上提升精度并加速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 全色锐化（PS）任务旨在融合低分辨率多光谱（LRMS）与全色（PAN）图像，重建高分辨率多光谱（HRMS）图像；本文目标是构造一个更锐利的残差学习起始基以改进 HRMS 重建。

Motivation: 现有 PS 网络通常以 4 倍插值的模糊 LRMS 作为全局残差跳跃基，PAN 空间细节仅通过残差修正引入，起始基本身缺乏高频信息，限制了残差学习的效果。

Method: PanBase 依据传感器物理特性，将 PAN 空间细节与 LRMS 光谱信息结合，并在传感器特定模糊下约束与 LRMS 观测的一致性；不依赖网络训练，仅通过闭式标定为每个光谱波段估计 9 个系数。集成到现有骨干只需两处改动：以 PanBase 替换跳跃基，并将其作为额外输入拼接。

实验效果: 在原训练配方与匹配训练预算下，PanBase 在 WorldView-3、QuickBird、GaoFeen-2 上提升多数降分辨率与全分辨率指标，平均训练收敛加速 4.4 倍；在 WorldView-2 零样本评估中，独立 PanBase 无需目标传感器拟合或神经精修即可获得有竞争力的重建结果。

Abstract: Many pan-sharpening (PS) networks reconstruct high-resolution multispectral (HRMS) images by adding learned residuals to 4$\times$-interpolated low-resolution multispectral (LRMS) inputs, incorporating panchromatic (PAN) detail only through these residual corrections. This raises a simple question: Why start with a blurry base when sharp spatial detail is already available? We propose PanBase, a sharper multispectral starting point designed for more effective residual learning. Guided by physical sensor characteristics, PanBase combines PAN spatial detail with LRMS spectral information while enforcing consistency with the LRMS observations under sensor-specific blur. We construct this base without neural network training, estimating only nine coefficients per spectral band through closed-form calibration. Our PanBase can be easily integrated into existing backbones with a global residual skip through two simple changes: (i) replacing the skip base with PanBase and (ii) concatenating it as an additional input. Using each backbone's original training recipe and matched training budgets, PanBase improves most reduced- and full-resolution metrics on WorldView-3, QuickBird, and GaoFen-2, while achieving 4.4$\times$ faster training convergence on average. In zero-shot evaluation on WorldView-2, standalone PanBase achieves competitive reconstruction without target-sensor fitting or neural refinement.

</details>


### [76] [Distractors are the Real Enemy: Visual Distraction Adversarial Perturbations on SAM2-Based Video Tracking and Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32011)
*Adonay Demewez Gebremedhin,Mohamad Alansari,Wessam Shehieb,Muzammal Naseer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 DistractAttack，一种利用背景干扰物对 SAM2 类可提示视频跟踪器实施持续性对抗攻击的白盒方法，通过反事实干扰物搜索显著提升攻击效果并可黑盒迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 可提示视频跟踪器（如 SAM2）凭借强时序一致性在视频理解与跟踪下游任务中被广泛用作基础模型；本文研究其对抗鲁棒性，即在视频级与单帧扰动下破坏跟踪输出的输入-输出攻击问题。

Motivation: 现有针对 SAM2 的攻击多聚焦局部掩码退化与短期时序特征扰动，对长程动态演化与竞争性干扰物（Distractors）的利用不足，难以实现持续性的跟踪破坏。

Method: 观察到 SAM2 在跟踪不确定时会自身暴露有用的干扰物线索；据此提出 DistractAttack，核心为反事实干扰物搜索（CDS）：并行传播干净与受攻击轨迹以发现合理竞争区域，并筛选在扰动下愈发有效的区域；再将该证据在时间上累积，削弱目标支持度并促成循环替代预测或目标消失预测。

实验效果: 在视觉目标跟踪与视频目标分割上开展视频级与单帧扰动实验，视频扰动下 DRE 提升至最近基线的 4.4 倍、NRE 提升至 7.3 倍，并在黑盒设置下有效迁移至不同模型规模与跟踪器变体。

Abstract: Promptable video trackers such as SAM2 achieve accurate and robust performance through strong temporal consistency, making them important foundations for downstream video understanding and tracking applications. As their use continues to expand, understanding their robustness to adversarial perturbations becomes increasingly important. Existing attacks on SAM2 largely focus on local mask degradation and short-term temporal feature disruption, leaving its longer-term dynamics and competing object interferences (Distractors) insufficiently explored. We observe that SAM2 itself exposes useful distractor cues when tracking becomes uncertain. Based on these observations, we propose DistractAttack, a white-box attack that uses background distractors to disrupt target tracking persistently. Its core procedure, Counterfactual Distractor Search (CDS), propagates clean and attacked trajectories in parallel to discover plausible competing regions and identify those that become increasingly effective under perturbation. The discovered evidence is accumulated over time to weaken target support and promote recurrent alternatives or disappearance predictions. Extensive experiments under video-level and one-shot perturbations across Visual Object Tracking and Video Object Segmentation demonstrate strong attack effectiveness, raising DRE to $4.4\times$ and NRE to $7.3\times$ their nearest baselines under video perturbation, while transferring effectively across model scales and tracker variants in black-box settings. Project page: https://adonaydem.github.io/sam2-da-page/

</details>


### [77] [DynaCue: Comparing Motion and Timing Between Groups of Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32014)
*Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DynaCue is a two-sample permutation test that fuses optical-flow dynamics, frame continuity, and appearance scores to detect group-level differences in video motion, outperforming single-score metrics.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: The task is to compare groups of videos (e.g., generated clips) to detect differences in motion quality and temporal consistency, in the context of evaluating and benchmarking video generation models against human judgments. Inputs are video groups; the output is a statistical detection of whether the groups differ.

Motivation: Single aggregate scores conflate evaluation criteria: VideoScore's temporal-consistency score separated differently rated Pika videos in 95% of comparisons but failed in all 200 comparisons of 41 VideoGen-RewardBench pairs, because such scores can miss differences tied to motion preference. Selecting one cue in advance is therefore unreliable.

Method: DynaCue performs a two-sample permutation test combining complementary cues without pre-selecting one: local optical-flow changes measured both before and after normalizing for each clip's usual activity level, interruption cues from frame differences and freeze counts, plus separate motion and appearance scores.

实验效果: DynaCue detected group differences across five controlled interventions (playback continuity, displacement, rhythm), and in real-world evaluations hit at least 80% detection on resampled comparisons in all three datasets at smaller sample sizes, while every other method scored 60% or lower on at least one dataset.

Abstract: A score that distinguishes videos with different temporal-consistency ratings can miss differences associated with motion preference. VideoScore's temporal consistency score distinguished higher- from lower-rated Pika videos in 95% of comparisons with 15 videos per group, yet could not detect the difference between preferred videos and their same-prompt alternatives in any of the 200 comparisons of 41 VideoGen-RewardBench pairs. We introduce DynaCue, a two-sample permutation test that combines complementary measurements of motion, frame continuity, and appearance to compare groups of videos without choosing a single cue in advance. DynaCue measures local changes in optical flow both before and after accounting for each clip's usual activity, captures interruptions through frame differences and freeze counts, and incorporates scores for motion and appearance. In controlled experiments, DynaCue detected group differences across five interventions affecting playback continuity, displacement, or rhythm. In real-world evaluations of generated videos where viewers rated temporal consistency or chose which of two videos they preferred for its motion, DynaCue detected group differences in at least 80% of resampled comparisons on each of the three datasets at the smaller sample sizes studied. Every other method evaluated on all three had a detection rate of 60% or less on at least one dataset.

</details>


### [78] [Learning Agent States for Latent Action Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32017)
*Shaoan Xie,Fan Feng,Peiyuan Zhu,Peter Spirtes,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Agent-State LAM，通过联合学习紧凑的智能体状态表示与隐动作，并用空间匹配时序判别（SMTD）引导该状态聚焦于智能体自身物理状态的演化，从而提升隐动作恢复、下游控制与世界模型预测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 隐动作模型（LAMs）从无标注视频中学习类动作隐变量：输入为帧对，通过帧对推断与隐条件预测的耦合训练，输出可用于策略与世界模型预训练的隐动作表征。

Motivation: 隐动作需要解释什么取决于动作推断与转移动预测所用的表征空间；当该空间把智能体不断变化的物理状态与背景、持续呈现的智能体外观等视觉上下文纠缠在一起时，推断出的隐动作会编码与动作无关的信息，导致隐动作不纯。

Method: 联合学习一个紧凑的智能体状态表示及其转移的隐动作建模：该紧凑状态将稠密视觉特征聚合到同一共享空间，供动作推断与前向预测共用；并提出空间匹配时序判别（SMTD），在冻结的局部视觉特征上做空间灵活匹配，用每帧最强的局部特征匹配得分（而非全局帧表示比较）来区分源帧与邻近帧，使背景与持续外观因位移后仍可匹配而信息量下降，从而促使表征聚焦智能体演化的物理状态。

实验效果: 在含干扰的控制任务与六个真实机器人视频数据集上，实验显示在动作恢复、下游控制与世界模型预测方面均有提升；摘要未给出具体数值。

Abstract: Latent action models (LAMs) learn action-like latents from unlabeled video by coupling frame-pair inference with latent-conditioned prediction, enabling policy and world-model pretraining. However, what the latent must explain depends on the representation space used for action inference and transition prediction. When this space entangles the agent's evolving physical state with visual context, such as background and persistent agent appearance, inferred latents can encode information unrelated to the agent's actions. To address this challenge, we propose Agent-State LAM, which jointly learns a compact agent-state representation and latent actions modeling its transitions. The compact state aggregates dense visual features into a shared space for both action inference and forward prediction. To guide what this state retains, we introduce spatially matched temporal discrimination (SMTD), which trains the state to distinguish its source frame from nearby frames through spatially flexible matching with frozen local visual features. Rather than comparing global frame representations, SMTD scores each frame by emphasizing its strongest local feature matches across spatial locations. This scoring allows background and persistent agent appearance to remain matched across frames despite displacement, making these shared cues less informative for identifying the source frame and encouraging the representation to capture the agent's evolving physical state. Extensive experiments on distracting control tasks and six real robot video datasets demonstrate improved action recovery, downstream control, and world-model prediction. Code and checkpoints are available at https://github.com/Mid-Push/Agent-State-LAM.

</details>


### [79] [HuluOPD: Unifying Heterogeneous Medical Specialists into One Lightweight Model with Unlabeled Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32021)
*Yuncheng Zhang,Songtao Jiang,Yitian Tang,Zijie Meng,Zihan Xu,Xuxiang Ren,Jian Wu,Hualiang Wang,Zuozhu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HuluOPD 提出一种基于在线策略蒸馏的方法，仅用无标注提示即可将多个异构医学专科视觉语言模型统一为一个轻量级 VLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 本文任务是构建统一的医学视觉语言模型：输入为无标注的医学影像提示（覆盖超声、病理、OCT、眼底、牙科五种模态），输出为由单一轻量模型生成的问答回答；目标是降低分别部署多个专科大模型的成本。

Motivation: 现有专科 VLM 虽在各自领域表现强，但训练数据多为私有、各专科模型由不同团队独立开发、架构与分词器不统一，难以合并；分别部署多个大模型代价高昂，且缺乏可复用的统一方案。

Method: 采用 on-policy 蒸馏：轻量学生模型对每个提示生成回答，由对应模态的专科教师模型对该回答进行监督，从而无需参考答案与教师训练数据；针对架构与分词器不一致问题，设计 continuation-aligned 跨分词器损失以对齐教师与学生的 token 序列。研究构建 HuluOPDCorpus（914k 无标注提示）用于蒸馏，并基于 90 余个公开数据集构建 HuluOPDBench（47k 留出测试项），在 132k 测试项（含第三方基准）上以统一协议评测。

实验效果: 学生模型以五个专科模型总参数量的 10% 保留了 94% 的问答性能，以 2% 参数量仍保留 89%；据作者所知，这是首次在无需教师训练数据与共享架构的前提下统一异构医学专科 VLM。

Abstract: Specialist medical vision-language models (VLMs) are strong within their specialties but hard to unify: their training data are often private, and independently developed specialists cannot be assumed to share an architecture. Deploying these large models separately is costly. We present HuluOPD, an on-policy distillation recipe that unifies heterogeneous specialists into one lightweight VLM using only unlabeled prompts, without requiring reference answers or the specialists' training data. The lightweight model answers each prompt, and the specialist of the prompt's modality supervises its response; a continuation-aligned cross-tokenizer loss makes this possible even when they differ in architecture and tokenizer. We apply HuluOPD to five specialists, for ultrasound, pathology, optical coherence tomography, fundus photography, and dental imaging. To distill and evaluate them, we build HuluOPDCorpus, 914k unlabeled prompts, and HuluOPDBench, 47k held-out test items, from over 90 public datasets, and score all models under one protocol on 132k test items, including third-party benchmarks. A single model with 10% of the five specialists' combined parameters retains 94% of their question-answering performance, and one with 2% retains 89%. To our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of unifying heterogeneous medical specialist VLMs without requiring their training data or a shared architecture.

</details>


### [80] [Beyond Before-and-After Images: Evaluating Temporal Grounding in a Lightweight VLM for Disaster Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32022)
*Shubh Garg,Pratham Arora,Debabrata Ghosh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 作者通过受控替换前时相影像的对照实验，发现轻量VLM在灾害双时相变化问答中并未真正利用真实的前事件影像内容，且对时序顺序不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 双时相卫星影像（前后事件各一景）是灾害评估的核心输入，需要比较事件前后场景来判断受损情况。本文目标是检验视觉语言模型（VLM）在变化问答中是否真正依赖前事件影像与时序信息，而非仅凭后事件影像或提示中的时间框架作答。

Motivation: 现有VLM可能"看似"回答了变化问题，实则主要依赖后事件影像或提示的时间表述，即缺乏可验证的时间性接地（temporal grounding）；而现有时间性基准多只报告答案正确率，无法说明答案是否真的依赖预期证据。

Method: 构建受控诊断协议并在 DisasterM3 上对 InternVL3-2B 施测：数据含41个事件、9类灾害的1,659对光学与SAR影像（按事件不重叠划分）及9,031个推理问题；设置五种输入条件——仅后事件影像、真实前/后配对、后事件影像重复两次、影像顺序反转、无关（他事件）前事件影像，并以配对bootstrap置信区间比较准确率差异。

实验效果: 真实配对仅比"后图重复"对照高0.5个百分点（95% CI [-0.2, +1.2]），顺序反转改变-0.2个百分点（[-0.8, +0.5]），即真实前事件影像内容无可测增益、对时序顺序也几乎不敏感；但真实配对比他事件配对高2.5个百分点（[+1.6, +3.4]），说明模型并非对首图视而不见；状态转移与多跳问题仍困难，光学与SAR呈现不同剖面。

Abstract: Bi-temporal satellite imagery is central to disaster assessment: deciding what was damaged requires comparing a pre-event scene with its post-event counterpart. A vision-language model (VLM) can nonetheless appear to answer change questions while relying mainly on the post-event image or on the prompt's temporal framing. We present a controlled diagnostic of temporal grounding and apply it to one lightweight VLM, InternVL3-2B, on DisasterM3: 1,659 optical and SAR image pairs from 41 events and nine disaster types, in event-disjoint partitions, with 9,031 reasoning questions. We compare post-only input, the true pre/post pair, the post image repeated twice, reversed image order, and an unrelated pre-event image. The true pair scores $+0.5$ accuracy points above the repeated-post control (95\% paired bootstrap CI $[-0.2, +1.2]$), and reversing the order changes accuracy by $-0.2$ points ($[-0.8, +0.5]$). Under this protocol we therefore find no measurable benefit from the content of the true pre-event image and little sensitivity to temporal order. The model is not blind to the first image, however: the true pair scores $2.5$ points above a pair whose pre-event image comes from another event ($[+1.6, +3.4]$). State-transition and multi-hop questions remain hard, and optical and SAR inputs show different profiles. We outline temporal scene graphs as a testable next step, not a validated solution, and argue that temporal benchmarks for Earth observation should report whether answers depend on the intended evidence, not only how often they are right.

</details>


### [81] [View Management with Gaussian Placement and Opacity Regularization for Online 3DGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32025)
*Dong Hwi Kim,Park Sangwon,Chaehyeon Song,Ayoung Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文从"增加有效视图监督"与"控制地图模型复杂度"两方面改进3DGS-SLAM在线建图，在实时预算内同时提升外观与几何质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是将3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）与SLAM耦合，实现需要在线建图的实时应用中的真实感渲染；系统需输出相机位姿与由高斯构成的在线地图，目标是提升地图的外观与几何质量。

Motivation: 现有3DGS-SLAM地图仍沿用传统SLAM设计：仅在为定位挑选的关键帧上训练，并用不确定的前端深度做几何正则；这忽视了3DGS地图的高模型复杂度（数百万参数而每区域视图极少），导致地图对稀疏输入过拟合，且每新增视图与高斯都争夺同一实时预算，难以直接修正。

Method: 双侧改进入手：一是当关键帧维持地图结构时，随建图更新逐步接纳非关键帧图像，并按各帧组提供的监督量分配有限更新次数；二是用边缘引导初始化，仅在图像细节需要处放置新高斯以控制模型复杂度；三是提出自参考不透明度正则（SOR），利用累积渲染证据（无额外渲染开销）抑制持续出现在地图导出表面参考前方的低透明高斯。

实验效果: 摘要未报告具体实验数据、基准或对比数值，仅定性说明上述设计在实时预算内同时改善了在线地图的外观与几何。

Abstract: The realistic rendering of 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has drawn interest from real-time applications that require maps to be built online. This led to coupling 3DGS with SLAM, providing camera poses and initial Gaussians for the map. However, the map is still optimized with designs inherited from conventional SLAM, which train only on keyframes selected for localization and regularize geometry with uncertain frontend depth. These designs overlook the model complexity of a 3DGS map: with millions of parameters but few views of each region, the map overfits to sparse inputs. Resolving this is challenging, as every view and Gaussian added to the map competes for the same real-time budget. We close this gap from both sides. To add views while keyframes preserve the structure of the map, we progressively admit non-keyframe images as mapping updates complete and allocate the limited updates across different groups of frames according to the supervision each group provides. To control the model complexity of the map, edge-guided initialization places new Gaussians where image detail requires them. To complement the uncertain frontend depth, Self-Referenced Opacity Regularization (SOR) uses accumulated rendering evidence, at no extra rendering cost, to suppress the opacity of Gaussians that persistently contribute in front of a map-derived surface reference. Together, these designs improve both the appearance and the geometry of online maps within the real-time budget.

</details>


### [82] [Do Occupancy World Models Really Follow Actions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32029)
*Jongseong Bae,Ha Young Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReCast 提出一种以自车轨迹引导的 Transformer 占用世界模型，通过对齐相对变换下的几何对应关系提升反事实动作下的预测可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 占用世界模型以自车动作为条件预测未来 3D 场景，输入为历史观测与指定轨迹，输出未来占用预测，目标是使预测忠实跟随给定轨迹（包括反事实轨迹）。

Motivation: 驾驶数据对同一场景的替代轨迹覆盖稀疏，现有模型只能从"场景—轨迹"组合中隐式学习自车运动的空间效应，易将动作效应与场景—轨迹相关性纠缠，导致反事实动作下预测不忠实于指定轨迹。

Method: ReCast 将历史编码与未来解码解耦，利用相对自车变换对齐可变形交互，把未来预测建立在动作诱导的跨自车帧几何对应之上，同时保留非刚性场景演化的灵活性；并构建覆盖分布内、尾部与分布外轨迹的反事实动作忠实性基准。

实验效果: 在 Occ3D-nuScenes 上，ReCast 取得有竞争力的标准预测性能，同时提升反事实预测，且在分布外轨迹下增益尤为明显（摘要未报告具体数值）。

Abstract: Occupancy world models predict future 3D scenes conditioned on ego actions, yet their predictions may not faithfully follow the specified trajectory under counterfactual actions. Because driving datasets provide sparse coverage of alternative trajectories for each scene, existing models learn the spatial effect of ego motion implicitly from observed scene--trajectory combinations, which may entangle action effects with scene--trajectory correlations. We propose ReCast, an ego-trajectory-guided transformer that grounds future prediction in action-induced geometric correspondence across ego-centric frames. ReCast decouples historical encoding from future decoding and aligns deformable interactions using relative ego transformations, while retaining flexibility for non-rigid scene evolution. We also introduce a counterfactual action-faithfulness benchmark covering in-distribution, tail, and out-of-distribution trajectories. On Occ3D-nuScenes, ReCast achieves competitive standard forecasting performance while improving counterfactual forecasting, with particularly clear gains under out-of-distribution trajectories.

</details>


### [83] [BodyWeave: Native Body-Conditioned 3D Garment Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32034)
*Junyi Zhang,Yuanchuan Wang,Qichao Wang,Wenzhe Qian,Shizhuo Cheng,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BodyWeave 以 3D 人体为原生空间条件，将服装几何生成与贴合统一在一个生成框架中，并进一步条件化材质合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是在保留参考设计的前提下，为数字角色生成贴合目标人体形状与姿态的 3D 服装；输入为参考设计与目标人体，输出为可直接渲染的服装几何及基于物理的材质属性。

Motivation: 缝纫纸样方法难以复现陌生素材设计与细节，图像到 3D 生成器虽有丰富形状先验却缺乏显式 3D 人体上下文，因此需要一种在生成全程以人体为空间参照的统一方案。

Method: 基于 3D 生成先验，在几何生成全过程引入人体条件：Global Body Adapter 在共享坐标系中引导服装的放置与范围，Local Body Attention 借助邻近人体几何细化表面，Surface Penetration Loss 对解码表面施加监督以减少穿插；所得几何再条件化材质合成以产生 PBR 属性。为支持跨设计与跨穿着者学习，构建 GarmentWeave3D 数据集（293K 对齐的服装—人体观测，含 114K 同设计跨人体配对）。

实验效果: 在域外 Artist-Created Garments 上取得较高几何保真度与较低人体穿插；跨人体评估显示对新穿着者的适配准确、服装—人体间距一致，外观比较显示面料复现忠实。

Abstract: Generating 3D garments for digital characters requires preserving a reference design while adapting the garment to the target body's shape and pose. Sewing-pattern methods can struggle to reproduce unfamiliar designs and fine details, while image-to-3D generators offer rich shape priors but typically lack explicit 3D body context. We present \textbf{BodyWeave}, a framework that unifies garment generation and fitting through native 3D body conditioning. Building on 3D generative priors, BodyWeave uses the body as a spatial reference throughout geometry generation: a \textbf{Global Body Adapter} guides garment placement and extent in the shared coordinate frame, while \textbf{Local Body Attention} guides surface refinement using nearby body geometry. A \textbf{Surface Penetration Loss} supervises decoded surfaces to reduce body intersections, and the resulting geometry conditions material synthesis to produce garments with physically based rendering attributes. To support learning across designs and wearers, we construct \textbf{GarmentWeave3D} with 293K aligned garment--body observations, including 114K same-design cross-body pairs. Evaluations on out-of-domain \textbf{Artist-Created Garments} demonstrate high geometric fidelity and low body penetration. Cross-body evaluations demonstrate accurate adaptation to new wearers with consistent garment--body spacing, while appearance comparisons show faithful fabric reproduction. Additional results and resources are available at https://gavin-jy.github.io/bodyweave-page/

</details>


### [84] [DeCo-Attention: Decompose to Compensate for Low-Precision Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32035)
*Yuhang He,Harrison Zhang,Fanrong Li,Chang Liu,RuQing G. Xu,Perkz Zheng,Zhenhua Wang,Rajeev Rao,Mengdi Wang,Sharan Chetlur,Jun Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的低精度注意力 DeCo-Attention，用主方向误差补偿使 QK 可在 FP4 中计算，从而大幅加速视频扩散 Transformer 的生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 视频扩散 Transformer 的生成时间主要由数万 token 的自注意力支配，其代价随序列长度平方增长；目标是在低精度（FP4/FP8）下加速注意力并保持生成质量。

Motivation: 现代 GPU 的 FP4 矩阵乘法可加速注意力，但 Q、K 的 4-bit 舍入误差较大，且 softmax 中的指数运算会放大 QK 乘积误差，使该误差成为注意力误差的主导来源，因此直接 FP4 化不可行。

Method: 算法与系统协同设计：通过逐头特征分解找出若干主方向，对 Q、K 的舍入误差进行免训练补偿，并将轻量补偿项作为额外通道拼接到 Q、K 上，使 FP4 乘积在计算注意力分数时自校正舍入误差；同时针对 softmax 成为 kernel 瓶颈的问题进行优化，使 FP4 计算收益转化为实际加速。

实验效果: QK 用 FP4、其余部分在 GB300/VR200 上用 FP8、在 RTX PRO 6000 上全 FP4 实现；Wan2.2 上 kernel 分别比 BF16 注意力快 2.13x/2.49x/2.82x，整段生成快 1.55x/1.68x/1.80x；四个视频生成模型上 FP8 配置 LPIPS 与已有 8-bit QK 方法相当，全 FP4 配置 LPIPS 低于已有全 4-bit kernel。

Abstract: Self-attention over tens of thousands of tokens dominates the generation time of video diffusion transformers, because its cost grows quadratically with sequence length. The FP4 matrix products of modern GPUs can speed up attention, but the 4-bit rounding of the queries Q and the keys K introduces large errors. We show that the exponential in softmax amplifies the error of their product QK, which therefore dominates the attention error. We propose DeCo-Attention, a co-design of algorithm and system for low-precision attention. DeCo-Attention is training-free and compensates the rounding error of Q and K along a few principal directions found by a per-head eigendecomposition. The lightweight compensation terms are appended to Q and K as extra channels, so the FP4 matrix product corrects its own rounding error while it computes the attention scores. Once the products run in FP4, the softmax bounds the kernel, and we optimize the softmax so that the gain of FP4 computation translates into realized speedup. With QK in FP4, we implement DeCo-Attention with the rest of attention in FP8 on GB300 and VR200 and in FP4 on RTX PRO 6000. On Wan2.2, these kernels run 2.13x, 2.49x, and 2.82x faster than BF16 attention, and the whole generation runs 1.55x, 1.68x, and 1.80x faster. On four video generation models, the FP8 configuration achieves LPIPS comparable to prior 8-bit QK methods, and the fully FP4 configuration lower LPIPS than prior fully 4-bit kernels.

</details>


### [85] [Instant LiDAR: Feed-Forward Gaussian Reconstruction for LiDAR Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32042)
*Xin Kang,Jiahui Huang,Jiawei Ren,Sipeng Zhang,Yu Chen,Weihua Zhang,Junjie Lai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Instant LiDAR 提出一种前馈式流水线，无需逐场景优化即可在数分钟内将多帧 LiDAR 扫描重建为可复用的高斯资产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 该任务面向闭环自动驾驶仿真所需的 LiDAR 场景重建：输入为一段驾驶日志中的多帧 LiDAR 扫描，输出为可复用的高斯场景资产，目标是低成本、快速地构建与维护大规模资产库。

Motivation: 现有重建方法依赖每场景数小时的逐场景优化，导致大规模资产库构建与维护成本过高；而直接用 LiDAR 测量初始化高斯因采样非均匀与视场受限而重建质量差——稀疏回波造成表面空洞，多帧拼接虽扩大覆盖却引入冗余基元。

Method: Instant LiDAR 采用三项设计：测量锚定的高斯预测，在记录回波上锚定并细化高斯属性；采样感知的高斯尺度，使空间支撑范围适应 LiDAR 采样分布；多帧整合，将重叠预测合并为覆盖整段驾驶日志的紧凑资产。

实验效果: 在 PAI 数据集上比 NuRec 快约 46 倍、在 Waymo 上快约 70 倍，并在 Waymo 上取得可比的下游 3D 检测精度。

Abstract: LiDAR reconstruction supports closed-loop autonomous driving simulation, but hours of per-scene optimization make large asset libraries costly to build and maintain. We present Instant LiDAR, a feed-forward pipeline that reconstructs multiple LiDAR scans as reusable Gaussian assets in minutes without per-scene optimization. LiDAR measurements provide strong geometric priors, but directly initializing Gaussians from them yields poor reconstruction due to nonuniform sampling and a limited field of view. Sparse returns leave surface gaps, while combining scans expands coverage but introduces redundant primitives. To address these challenges, Instant LiDAR combines measurement-anchored Gaussian prediction, sampling-aware Gaussian scales, and multi-frame consolidation. The predictor refines attributes anchored to recorded returns, while the scales adapt spatial support to LiDAR sampling. Multi-frame consolidation merges overlapping predictions into a compact asset spanning the driving log. Compared with NuRec, Instant LiDAR reconstructs scenes approximately 46 times faster on Physical AI (PAI) and 70 times faster on Waymo. It also achieves comparable downstream 3D detection accuracy on Waymo.

</details>


### [86] [SegPFN: Training-Free In-Context Segmentation with Tabular Prior-Data Fitted Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32045)
*Jiajian Ma,Yihao Qiao,Ke Chen,Kaiyue Feng,Wushuang Rui,Abhishek Jha,Aarti Darji,Yiqiu Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SegPFN 将上下文分割重构为基于冻结视觉特征的表格分类，借助冻结的表格 PFN 实现免训练、可泛化的上下文分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 上下文分割（ICS）以一张或少量带标注的参考图像指定目标图像中需分割的对象，输入为参考图像及其标注与目标图像，输出为目标图像的分割结果，目标是在无分割专用训练下灵活利用参考信息。

Motivation: 现有免训练 ICS 方法依赖人工设计的推理流程，参考信息的组合方式受限、灵活性不足；而通过分割专用训练获得的学习式上下文推理虽更具自适应性，却可能损害对未见域的泛化能力。

Method: 提出免训练框架 SegPFN：将 ICS 重构为对冻结视觉特征的表格分类问题，用冻结的表格先验数据拟合网络（PFN）以带标注的参考图像图块作为上下文，对目标图像的图块进行分类；并采用难负样本参考图块与联合条件化以提升细粒度判别和多参考利用。

实验效果: 在涵盖语义、部件与个性化分割的 11 个数据集上，SegPFN 相比最强整体基线平均 mIoU 提升 4.7 个点，并在五种任务-样本设置中的四种取得最高平均 mIoU。

Abstract: In-context segmentation (ICS) uses one or a few annotated reference images to specify what to segment in a target image. Existing training-free ICS approaches typically rely on manually designed inference procedures, which may limit how flexibly reference information is combined. Learned in-context inference offers an adaptive alternative, but acquiring this capability through segmentation-specific training can compromise generalization to unseen domains. We therefore ask: can a pretrained in-context learner infer what to segment from annotated references without segmentation-specific training? We introduce SegPFN, a training-free framework that reformulates ICS as tabular classification over frozen visual features. A frozen tabular prior-data fitted network (PFN) uses labeled reference patches as context to classify patches in the target image. We comprehensively benchmark SegPFN against major existing ICS methods across 11 datasets spanning semantic, part, and personalized segmentation. SegPFN improves overall mean intersection-over-union (mIoU) by 4.7 points over the strongest overall baseline and achieves the highest average mIoU in four of five task-shot settings. Further analyses show that hard-negative reference patches improve fine-grained target discrimination, while joint conditioning enables SegPFN to use multiple references effectively. These results show that SegPFN offers a simple and effective paradigm for training-free in-context segmentation.

</details>


### [87] [SurgGenesis: A Generative Surgical World Model for Future-Aware Surgical Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32054)
*Qirui Zhong,Quande Liu,Yang Chen,Cheng Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SurgGenesis 是一个生成式外科世界模型，通过学习潜空间中的未来手术状态转移来充当可扩展的手术数据引擎，并以三阶段渐进式 LoRA 适配视频扩散模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 手术场景理解受限于数据稀缺与长尾类别不平衡：手术视频标注依赖稀缺的临床专业知识，罕见手术事件样本不足。本文以视觉观测和文本化的手术流程描述为输入，目标是生成未来手术场景片段，用于扩充与再平衡手术数据并提升下游理解任务。

Motivation: 现有工作仅以视觉保真度评价生成质量，无法区分"世界模型"与"通用生成器"，也未验证生成数据是否忠实执行指定动作、能否真正缓解长尾事件不足并带来下游增益；同时手术领域数据稀缺，直接微调大规模视频扩散模型易发生灾难性遗忘。

Method: 提出 SurgGenesis，在潜空间中从视觉观测与文本流程描述学习未来手术状态转移。为在数据稀缺领域适配大规模视频扩散模型而不灾难性遗忘，设计三阶段渐进式 LoRA 策略，依次注入内镜视觉先验、流程语义与空间运动线索。评估沿两条轴展开：(i) 未来感知性，用 GT 潜空间预测差距与时间跨度分析对比预测未来与真实未来；(ii) 动作忠实的可用性，检验生成片段是否包含指定手术三元组，并将其用于长尾事件再平衡及驱动分割、三元组识别、3D 重建等下游任务，考察动作特异性增益。

实验效果: 摘要未报告具体实验数值结果；仅给出评价框架与定性结论：基于条件生成的前景可与传统长尾采样互补，且生成片段在再平衡长尾事件与驱动下游理解任务时表现出动作特异性增益。

Abstract: Surgical scene understanding is constrained by data scarcity and long-tail class imbalance, as annotating surgical videos demands scarce clinical expertise while rare procedural events remain underrepresented. We tackle this through data generation and propose SurgGenesis, a generative surgical world model that learns future surgical state transitions in a latent space from visual observations and textual procedural descriptions, serving as a scalable surgical data engine. To adapt a large-scale video diffusion model to the data-scarce surgical domain without catastrophic forgetting, we introduce a three-stage progressive LoRA strategy that incrementally injects endoscopic visual priors, procedural semantics, and spatial motion cues. Rather than judging generation by visual fidelity alone, we validate SurgGenesis along two axes that separate a world model from a generic generator: (i) future-awareness, by comparing predicted futures against the real future through a GT-latent prediction gap and temporal-horizon analysis, and (ii) action-faithful usefulness, by checking that generated clips contain the commanded surgical triplet and that using them to rebalance long-tail events and drive downstream understanding (segmentation, triplet recognition, 3D reconstruction) yields action-specific gains. Conditioned futures therefore complement conventional long-tail sampling. Code: https://zh-qr.github.io/SurgGenesis

</details>


### [88] [LoopDVR: Looped Transformer for Efficient Dense Visual Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32067)
*Sirui Liang,Xuanlong Yu,Longfei Liu,Xiaodong Cun,Xi Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoopDVR 通过在 ViT 主干与 Transformer 解码器中跨层复用模块，将预训练密集视觉识别模型转为参数高效的循环网络，在保持精度竞争力的同时显著减少参数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 该任务是将预训练的 Transformer 密集视觉识别模型（检测、分割等）压缩为参数高效网络，输入为预训练模型及其视觉表示，输出为保留原始计算结构的轻量化循环模型，目标是在精度与参数量之间取得更优权衡。

Motivation: 直接缩减网络宽度或深度会改变原有计算结构；而朴素地共享参数又会破坏不同深度层所学习到的差异化变换，因此需要一种既能复用参数又能保留深度特异表示的压缩方案。

Method: 不改宽度或深度，而是在主干与解码器中跨层复用 Transformer 模块：依据层间表示差异度划分合适的共享组，保留最后一个主干模块独立以构建多尺度特征；每个共享模块用其组内末端层的预训练权重初始化以保真已学表示；每层设置独立 Loop Adapter 保留深度特异变换；再将视觉表示蒸馏进循环主干，并与循环解码器结合做任务训练。

实验效果: 实验显示 LoopDVR 在多种密集识别任务上取得最佳精度—参数权衡，相比参考模型参数量减少约 28% 且精度保有竞争力；LoopDet-S 仅用 7.0M 参数即达到 50.9 box AP。

Abstract: We propose LoopDVR, a framework for converting pretrained Transformer-based dense visual recognition models into parameter-efficient looped networks. Rather than reducing the network width or depth directly, LoopDVR reuses Transformer blocks across layers in both the ViT backbone and Transformer decoder, reducing the number of parameters while retaining the original computational structure. However, naively sharing parameters can disrupt the distinct transformations learned at different depths. To address this challenge, we identify suitable sharing groups based on inter-layer representation dissimilarity and keep the final backbone block independent for multi-scale feature construction. We then initialize each shared backbone block with the pretrained weights of its group's terminal layer to better preserve the learned representations. An independent Loop Adapter at each layer further helps retain depth-specific transformations within shared blocks. Subsequently, we distill visual representations into the looped backbone and integrate it with a looped decoder for task-specific training. Experiments demonstrate that LoopDVR achieves the best accuracy--parameter trade-offs across dense recognition tasks. Compared with the reference models, it reduces parameters by approximately 28% while maintaining competitive accuracy. Notably, LoopDet-S achieves 50.9 box AP with only 7.0M parameters, highlighting its effectiveness in compact models.

</details>


### [89] [Not the Scalar, but Its Evolution: Robust Source Attribution for Generated Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32074)
*Chao Wang,Zijin Yang,Yaofei Wang,Yuang Qi,Weiming Zhang,Kejiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于"谱收缩(Spectral Contraction)"现象的 SpCon 框架，利用重建残差能量的频率分辨演化实现潜生成模型生成图像的来源归属，并在多种后处理与平台场景下显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是潜生成模型(LGM)生成图像的来源归属，用于版权审计与数据治理；仅可访问目标模型的 VAE，输入为图像，输出为来源判定，要求在常见后处理及平台处理下仍保持可靠。

Motivation: 现有基于重建的归属方法将图像对重建的响应压缩为单一全局标量（如重建误差或两次连续误差之比），丢弃了通道—频率结构，使图像内容与后处理扰动掩盖来源证据，导致图像传播后归属困难。

Method: 先在重复施加同一重建算子下发现更稳定的来源判别线索，即残差能量随频率分辨的演化规律（谱收缩）。SpCon 通过两次确定性重建获得相邻残差，基于其频率分辨的相对能量变化构建谱收缩表示，并对每个来源条件对在有限个拟合参考分量上聚合 Student-t 响应密度；再按非归属参考的可用性支持 Target-Only、External-Reference、Known-Reference 三种设置。

实验效果: 在 7 个 LGM、35 种条件下，TO/ER/KR 的平均 AUROC/AP 分别为 0.8786/0.6344、0.9404/0.7886、0.9652/0.8626，均优于最强基线(0.8154/0.5277)；在三个社交平台上分别为 0.8581/0.5507、0.9369/0.7549、0.9734/0.8773。

Abstract: The widespread deployment of latent generative models (LGMs) makes generated image source attribution essential for copyright auditing and data governance, yet practical attribution should remain reliable under common post-processing and platform-induced processing. Existing reconstruction-based attribution methods summarize an image's reconstruction response to a single global scalar, such as the reconstruction error or the ratio of two consecutive errors. These summaries discard channel-frequency structure, allowing image content and post-processing variation to obscure source evidence and making attribution after spreading difficult. We instead uncover a more stable source-discriminative cue under post-processing: the frequency-resolved evolution of residual energy across repeated applications of the same reconstruction operator. Motivated by this insight, we characterize a phenomenon we term Spectral Contraction and use it to build SpCon, a source-attribution framework that accesses only the target model's VAE. SpCon obtains adjacent residuals through two deterministic reconstructions, builds a spectral-contraction representation from their frequency-resolved relative energy change, and aggregates Student-t response densities for each source-condition pair over a finite set of fitted reference components. The same representation supports three settings according to non-belonging reference availability: none (Target-Only, TO; belonging references only), external out-of-domain sources (External-Reference, ER), or known in-domain competitors (Known-Reference, KR). Across seven LGMs, TO, ER, and KR attain average AUROC/AP of 0.8786/0.6344, 0.9404/0.7886, and 0.9652/0.8626 over the 35 conditions, all outperforming the strongest baseline (0.8154/0.5277), and achieve 0.8581/0.5507, 0.9369/0.7549, and 0.9734/0.8773 on the three social platforms.

</details>


### [90] [Clinical3D-Bench: Evaluating and Improving Spatial Intelligence of Multimodal LLMs in Hospital Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32094)
*Yang Bai,Yingzhi Xia,Ling Lin,Jun Cheng,Lin Fu,Huazhu Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Clinical3D-Bench 医院空间推理基准及 GeoJeV 方法，使多模态大模型通过显式 3D 计算显著提升空间推理表现并缩小与医生的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是在模拟医院的自我中心漫游视频上进行空间推理，识别外观相似的设备、理解患者相对方位、判断通道宽度等；输入为逼真视频（及可选 3D 地图），输出为八类空间问题的答案。目标是对标医生水平评测并提升多模态大模型的医院空间理解能力。

Motivation: 现有基准对这些空间能力的覆盖有限；且实验显示模型难以有效利用 3D 地图输入（替换为其他视频的地图几乎不影响准确率），说明缺乏让模型真正"用 3D 计算"的机制。

Method: 构建 Clinical3D-Bench：基于 373 个符合医院设计规范的病房布局生成 3,368 道八类问题，答案由 3D 场景计算得出，并通过配对场景状态检验模型是否随证据变化而改变答案，场景经十名医师审核；同时提供 44,750 题的训练集 Clinical3D-45K 用于微调。提出 GeoJeV：从每段视频重建场景记忆，依据模型的问题状态选择 3D 操作及其参数，将其证据融入选项打分。

实验效果: 35 个 MLLM 中最佳开源/闭源模型宏平均准确率为 45.7%/53.6%，答案先验为 40.8%，医生子集为 79.0%，32 个开源模型中 27 个在双向患者相对方位上低于 40%；Clinical3D-45K 微调使两个模型超出最佳闭源模型 16–20 个点。GeoJeV 取得所评测模型中的最高准确率，进一步缩小与医生的差距。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning in hospitals requires tracking look-alike equipment, interpreting patient-relative directions, and judging passage widths, yet existing benchmarks provide limited coverage of these abilities. We introduce Clinical3D-Bench, a simulated benchmark with 3,368 questions of eight types on egocentric walkthroughs of 373 hospital-room layouts. Layouts follow hospital design guidelines, answers are computed from the 3D scenes, and paired scene states test whether models change their answers when relevant evidence changes. Videos are photorealistic, and ten physicians screened the layouts. Among 35 multimodal large language models (MLLMs), the best open and proprietary models reach 45.7% and 53.6% macro accuracy, against 40.8% for an answer prior and 79.0% for physicians on a subset, and 27 of 32 open models score below 40% on two-way patient-relative directions. Fine-tuning on Clinical3D-45K, our 44,750-question training set, lifts two MLLMs 16-20 points above the best proprietary model. 3D maps given to the fine-tuned MLLM as input go largely unused: replacing them with another video's, even when they are ground truth, barely changes its accuracy. The proposed GeoJeV instead lets the MLLM compute with 3D: it reconstructs a scene memory from each video, selects 3D operations and their arguments from the MLLM's question state, and adds their evidence to its option scores. It achieves the highest accuracy of all evaluated models, narrowing the gap to physicians.

</details>


### [91] [Rethinking Post-Training Quantization for Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32096)
*Minchan Kang,Kyeonghye Park,Seoyoung Cho,Junhee Lee,Daeshik Kim,Yucheol Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出低比特PTQ性能下降源于权重与激活共享量化范围中的离群值，并提出结合局部Hadamard变换、分组量化与高效分组执行的Rotate-and-Refine Quantization（RRQ）基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）的训练后量化（PTQ），即在小型校准集上确定量化参数而无需重训练，以实现低比特权重-激活量化的压缩部署，目标是在低比特下尽量保持全精度性能。

Motivation: 已有方法不断改进校准策略，但在低比特权重-激活量化下仍出现显著性能退化，说明仅靠校准精细化无法解决根本问题；作者通过定位量化误差集中位置并分析对应权重与激活分布，发现少数离群值主导了逐通道权重与逐token激活共享的量化范围，导致大部分数值分辨率不足、大量被映射为零。

Method: 基于上述发现，作者将关注点从校准优化转向量化表示本身，提出RRQ：以局部Hadamard变换抑制离群值、缓解共享范围被少数极值占据的问题，结合分组量化提升有效分辨率，并设计高效的分组感知执行方式以保证推理效率。

实验效果: 在8个多模态任务及多种量化设置下，RRQ显著优于基线，同时降低推理时间与显存占用；W4A4设置下，8B模型保留超过96%全精度性能、32B模型超过99%，而基线不足8%。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) compresses large vision-language models (LVLMs) by calibrating quantization parameters on a small dataset without retraining. However, despite increasingly sophisticated calibration strategies, prior methods suffer substantial performance degradation under low-bit weight--activation quantization. To understand this degradation, we locate where quantization error is concentrated and examine the corresponding weight and activation distributions. We observe that a few outliers dominate the ranges shared by per-channel (PC) weights and per-token (PT) activations, leaving insufficient resolution for most values and mapping many of them to zero. These findings motivate a shift in focus from calibration refinement to the underlying quantization representation. We therefore present Rotate-and-Refine Quantization (RRQ) as a simple, effective baseline for LVLM PTQ, combining local Hadamard transforms with group quantization and efficient group-aware execution. Across 8 multimodal tasks and diverse quantization settings, RRQ substantially outperforms baselines while reducing inference time and memory usage. At W4A4, it retains over 96\% of full-precision performance on 8B models and over 99\% on a 32B model, compared with less than 8\% for these baselines. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/kmc3661/RRQ

</details>


### [92] [GUI-PRISM: Efficient Action Decoding for GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32097)
*Enqi Liu,Zhi Gao,Liu Liu,Lifeng Fan,Liyuan Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GUI-PRISM 提出一种混合动作解码器，将 GUI 智能体的动作类型与结构化参数改为直接预测，在保持开放式文本生成的同时显著降低推理延迟并提升完整动作准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: GUI 智能体需将屏幕截图与用户指令转化为可执行动作，输入为视觉与指令信息，输出为动作类型及其参数（含开放式文本），目标是准确且高效地完成完整 GUI 动作预测。

Motivation: 现有方法多依赖视觉语言模型以文本自回归方式生成动作，解码过程导致显著推理延迟；而已有空间解码方法主要只解决目标定位这一子问题，未覆盖动作类型与参数的完整预测。

Method: GUI-PRISM 采用字段专用查询从共享多模态特征库中提取与动作相关的语义与空间信息，并通过条件查询建模各动作组件之间的依赖关系，再用门控残差交叉注意力借助细粒度多模态信息精化预测；由此实现对动作类型与结构化参数的直接预测，开放式文本仍保留语言生成。

实验效果: 在 ScreenSpot-v2 上达到 95.1% 准确率，较最强基于 head 的空间解码方法高 2.0 个百分点，相较基线实现 3.8× 推理加速；在 AndroidControl-v2 与 MM-Mind2Web-v2 上，完整动作准确率分别提升 4.8 与 0.7 个百分点，并分别实现 2.7× 与 3.9× 推理加速。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) agents translate screenshots and user instructions into executable actions. Most existing approaches rely on vision-language models to generate actions as text, often leading to substantial inference latency due to autoregressive decoding. To address this inefficiency, we propose GUI-PRISM, a hybrid action decoder that directly predicts action types and structured arguments while retaining language generation for open-ended text. Unlike existing spatial decoding methods that primarily focus on target localization, GUI-PRISM extends direct prediction to action types and their associated arguments, enabling complete GUI action prediction. Specifically, GUI-PRISM employs field-specific queries to extract action-relevant semantic and spatial information from a shared multimodal feature bank. It further models dependencies among action components through conditional queries. Gated residual cross-attention refines these predictions using fine-grained multimodal information. GUI-PRISM achieves 95.1\% accuracy on ScreenSpot-v2, outperforming the strongest head-based spatial decoding method by 2.0 percentage points while delivering a 3.8$\times$ inference speedup over the baseline. On AndroidControl-v2 and MM-Mind2Web-v2, it improves complete-action accuracy by 4.8 and 0.7 percentage points while achieving 2.7$\times$ and 3.9$\times$ inference speedups over the baseline, respectively. Our code is available on our project page.

</details>


### [93] [PreferenceScene: Personalized Indoor Scene Synthesis from Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32099)
*Seongrae Noh,RyunHa Lee,HyeongYeop Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PreferenceScene，在大语言模型中显式维护并逐轮修正全部设计属性的隐藏用户偏好估计，配合场景实例化模块，实现个性化室内三维场景合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是从简短指令生成带家具的三维房间，但用户偏好往往难以用文字完整表达，需要多轮交互：系统展示三维场景，用户只看渲染图并给出简短自由反馈与满意度评分，系统据此将整个房间逐步调整到用户偏好上。

Motivation: 现有文本到场景方法依赖一次性语言描述，难以捕捉只在看到具体房间时才浮现的偏好；且偏好不止针对单个物体，还涉及表面、光照、织物、家具及其整体布局所营造的氛围，因此系统必须能改动房间的每一部分，而反馈通常只涉及局部。

Method: 用大语言模型对房间每个设计属性维护显式偏好估计，每轮根据反馈反推其背后的品味，并修订包括未被反馈提及部分在内的整体估计；场景实例化模块将每个估计值构建为房间中各自独立的部分，使各部分都能跟随偏好变化。

实验效果: 在含十类房间、320 个实例的基准测试集上，以模拟用户和基于渲染的评估器评测：PreferenceScene 对隐藏偏好的匹配优于五个经相同交互适配的已发表文本到场景基线，且匹配度随轮次递增；即使直接向基线提供偏好，本方法仍优于它们，视觉语言模型也将其场景评为最真实、最实用。

Abstract: Text-to-scene methods turn a short instruction into a furnished 3D room, but the room that a particular person wants rarely comes across fully in words: many preferences surface only when the person sees a concrete room and can point to what they like and dislike in it. We therefore study personalized indoor scene synthesis under hidden user preferences: over several turns, a system shows a 3D scene, and the user, who sees only rendered images of the scene, replies with brief free-form feedback and a satisfaction score. The system must therefore bring the whole room toward the user's preferences from feedback that comments on only part of it at a time. It must also be able to change every part of the room, because a preference concerns not only single objects but the look and atmosphere that surfaces, lighting, textiles, furniture and their arrangement create together. We present PreferenceScene, in which a large language model keeps an explicit estimate of the user's preferences for every design attribute of the room and, after each turn, infers the taste behind the feedback and revises the whole estimate, including the parts that the feedback does not mention. A scene instantiation module builds every estimated value as its own part of the room, so that each of these parts can follow the user's preferences. We evaluate on the test set of a benchmark with 320 instances in ten room types, with a simulated user and a render-based evaluator. PreferenceScene matches the hidden preferences better than five published text-to-scene baselines adapted to the same interaction, and its match rises with every turn. It even surpasses them when they are given the preferences directly, and a vision-language model rates its scenes as the most realistic and functional.

</details>


### [94] [RISE: Reasoning with Implicit State Embeddings for GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32102)
*Enqi Liu,Zhi Gao,Liu Liu,Lifeng Fan,Liyuan Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出隐式框架 RISE，将动作相关推理融入隐状态表示以提升 GUI 动作预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: GUI 智能体需将用户指令与截图转化为可执行动作；输入为指令和界面截图，输出为下一步可执行动作，目标是准确预测动作。

Motivation: 现有方法在训练中依赖显式推理，导致模型过度依赖特定推理模式，而非学到可泛化的、与动作相关的信息。

Method: RISE 以三种互补监督目标将动作相关推理编码进隐状态：(1) 全 CoT 状态对齐，保留整个推理过程的信息；(2) 动作分布对齐，编码动作偏好；(3) 推理到动作的边界对齐，捕获动作生成前一刻的表示；从而获得信息量更大的隐表示，提升下一动作 token 预测准确率。

实验效果: 在 OSWorld-G 上达到 70.6% 准确率，在对比的 8B 模型中为最优，较其骨干提升 4.3 个百分点；在两个不同骨干与六个基准上的结果进一步验证其在 GUI 定位与动作预测上的有效性。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) agents translate user instructions and screenshots into executable actions. To learn executable actions, existing approaches often rely on explicit reasoning during training, which encourage over-reliance on specific reasoning patterns rather than generalizable action-relevant information. In this paper, we propose an implicit framework, RISE, which integrates action-relevant reasoning into implicit state representations to guide GUI action prediction. To this end, action-relevant reasoning is captured through three complementary supervision objectives: full-CoT state alignment preserves information across the reasoning process, action-distribution alignment encodes action preferences, and reasoning-to-action boundary alignment captures the representation immediately before action generation. In this case, our RISE effectively learns informative implicit representations, thereby improving the accuracy of next-action token prediction. RISE achieves 70.6\% accuracy on OSWorld-G, establishing state-of-the-art performance among compared 8B models with a 4.3-point gain over its backbone. Results across two distinct backbones and six diverse benchmarks further demonstrate its effectiveness in GUI grounding and action prediction. Our code is available on our project page.

</details>


### [95] [An Automated Framework Integrating Deep Learning and Anatomical Reasoning for Cardiac Phenotyping From CCTA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32114)
*Hien Minh Tran,Thi-Thu-Hien Pham,Le Duy Tan,Hieu Xuan Nguyen,Tuong-Anh Mai-Phan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种结合深度学习与解剖推理的自动化框架，对CCTA/非增强CT进行管腔分割与冠状动脉钙化、PCAT、EAT的定量表型分析，并公开代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是面向心脏CT队列的定量表型分析：输入为CCTA与非增强CT影像，输出为管腔分割、冠脉钙化量化、血管特异性PCAT采样及EAT替代腔室的量化测量，目标是形成可标准化处理队列的流水线。

Motivation: 现有工作仅做分割，缺乏解剖一致性约束，单独分割无法保证测量结果在解剖上自洽；且EAT标注通常依赖专家心包轮廓，成本高，难以规模化。

Method: 框架将深度学习与解剖推理结合：解剖测量模块利用图几何、分支身份与周围结构定义血管特异性PCAT采样区和EAT替代腔室，并借此无需专家心包轮廓即可自动生成训练目标；配套模块在非增强CT上量化冠脉钙化；采用显式解剖规则使测量可解释、可核查，并做分组件基准评测。

实验效果: 在ImageCAS-X Test-160上，经验证集选定后处理的管腔分割Dice为0.9133、clDice为0.940，超过已发表CAS-Net点估计；四折ImageCAS评估中四模型集成优于单一ResEnc-L；800对配对扫描显示全血管与管腔参考下PCAT衰减测量存在差异；外部EAT评估显示体积相关性强、空间重叠良好。

Abstract: Coronary computed tomography angiography (CCTA) depicts coronary anatomy and cardiac fat, but segmentation alone does not ensure anatomically consistent measurements. We propose an automated framework that combines deep learning with anatomical reasoning for quantitative cardiac phenotyping. Its anatomical measurement module uses graph geometry, branch identity, and surrounding structures to define vessel-specific pericoronary adipose tissue (PCAT) sampling and a surrogate epicardial adipose tissue (EAT) compartment. The latter also generates training targets without requiring expert pericardium contours. A companion module quantifies coronary calcium on non-contrast CT. Component-wise benchmarking was conducted on five public datasets: ImageCAS, ImageCAS-X, COCA, Visual Lab, and MultiD4CAD. On ImageCAS-X Test-160, lumen segmentation with validation-selected post-processing achieved a Dice score of 0.9133 and a clDice score of 0.940, exceeding the corresponding published CAS-Net point estimates. In the four-split ImageCAS evaluation, the four-member ensemble outperformed the single ResEnc-L model. Analysis of 800 paired scans showed that PCAT attenuation measurements differed between whole-vessel and lumen references. External EAT evaluation showed strong volume correlation and good spatial overlap. Explicit anatomical rules make the resulting measurements interpretable and inspectable. In practice, the framework provides a standardized pipeline for processing cardiac CT cohorts and quantifying coronary calcium, PCAT, and EAT for population-level analysis and comparison across studies. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/HarryTRAN207/CardiacCT-Research

</details>


### [96] [The Old Scene Lives in the Cache: Diagnosing and Shedding Stale History in Streaming Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32116)
*Hao Zhou,Ziyang Xu,Chenhan Jin,Hao Qin,Shengze Xu,Tieyong Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文分析：流式视频生成中"换场景但保留原主体"的冲突，可通过 Molt Forcing 从冷启动目标场景重建历史并选择性保留主体 token 来解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是流式视频生成中的场景切换：在保持切换前原主体身份的前提下更换到新场景，输入为流式视频帧序列与文本条件等，输出为切换后的生成视频，目标是既清除旧场景物体又保持主体外观一致。

Motivation: 现有缓存机制存在内在矛盾：缓存历史既维持主体一致性，也持续保留本应消失的旧场景物体；作者通过干预实验表明，仅做 KV flush 与注意力 sink 重计算仍无法去除旧场景物体，而用目标场景历史替换原历史虽能去除物体却改变主体、降低人脸相似度。

Method: 先通过控制变量干预（文本条件、去噪步、局部窗口、注意力 sink）定位缓存视觉历史保留过时内容的机制；再提出免训练的 Molt Forcing：以冷启动的目标场景重建场景历史，同时从原始缓存中选择性保留主体 token。

实验效果: 在 16 类场景族上，Molt Forcing 去除旧场景物体的可靠性与 Echo-Forcing 硬切相当（至少不弱），且生成主体与切换前外观的接近程度明显更好。

Abstract: Switching scenes while preserving the original subject in streaming video generation exposes a conflict: cached history helps maintain the subject but also sustains old-scene objects that should disappear. Through controlled interventions on text conditioning, denoising steps, the local window, and the attention sink, we identify how cached visual history keeps this obsolete content. When the original frames remain in the cache, KV flush and sink recomputation still leave old-scene objects in the generated videos. Replacing this history with target-scene history largely removes those objects, but also changes the subject, reducing face similarity to the pre-switch subject. We then propose Molt Forcing, a training-free method that rebuilds scene history from a cold-start target scene while selectively retaining subject tokens from the original cache. Across 16 scene families, Molt Forcing removes old-scene objects at least as reliably as an Echo-Forcing hard cut while keeping the subject much closer to its pre-switch appearance.

</details>


### [97] [EditText: Structure-Aware Generation and Selective Visual Transport for Fine-Grained Text Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32134)
*Zhaobin Chu,Guandong Li,Xin Lv,Zhiwei Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EditText 提出统一框架，将目标字符生成与源图像视觉迁移结合，并用 Attention Transport 与 Glyph-aware Structural Loss 实现细粒度文本编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 细粒度文本编辑要求在指定区域准确替换文字，同时保留源文本的字体、颜色、纹理以及未编辑区域的视觉一致性。模型输入为掩码图像、源文本风格参考与目标文本，输出为编辑后的目标图像。

Motivation: 现有工作难以在生成新字符的同时精确迁移源图的细节外观，源细节的保留与空间聚合的准确性缺乏显式建模与刻画。

Method: 采用双条件 Fill 输入（掩码图像与源文本风格参考，均与目标图像共享空间参考）。提出 Attention Transport 框架，用 Source Weight 表示分配给某条件的注意力强度，用 Spatial Aggregation Kernel 表示其内部空间注意力分布；对选定的非编辑查询，保持分配给掩码图像的总注意力权重不变，而将其注意力重新分配到空间对应邻域，实现选择性视觉迁移。结合多阶段训练与面向局部目标字符结构的 Glyph-aware Structural Loss，使模型兼顾字符准确生成、外观保留与未编辑细节保持。另构建 TBench 基准，覆盖字母文字、表意文字与阿拉伯数字。

实验效果: 在 TBench 上，EditText 取得最优的非编辑区域 SSIM（0.9769）与全图 LPIPS（0.0460）。

Abstract: Fine-grained text editing aims to accurately replace text in a specified region while preserving the source text's font, color, and texture, as well as visual consistency in unedited regions. We present EditText, a unified framework for fine-grained text editing that combines target character generation with visual transport from the source image. The model uses dual-condition Fill inputs: a masked image and a source text style reference, both sharing a spatial reference with the target image. The masked image hides the original text and retains unedited content as a source canvas; the style reference supplies appearance cues that guide new character generation together with the target text. We introduce an Attention Transport framework in which Source Weight describes the attention allocated to a condition and the Spatial Aggregation Kernel describes its internal spatial attention distribution. Accurate transfer of source details depends on attention strength, the retention of details in condition features, and spatially accurate aggregation. For selected non-edit queries, we therefore preserve the total attention weight assigned to the masked image while redistributing its attention toward spatially corresponding neighborhoods, enabling selective visual transport. Multi-stage training, combined with a Glyph-aware Structural Loss on local target character structure, teaches the model to generate accurate text, preserve source text appearance, and retain unedited details. We also introduce TBench, a fine-grained text editing benchmark covering Alphabetic, Logographic, and Arabic numerals, to evaluate character updates, appearance preservation, and the preservation of unedited details. On TBench, EditText achieves state-of-the-art non-edit-region SSIM (0.9769) and full-image LPIPS (0.0460).

</details>


### [98] [Interpretable Orthogonal Latent Diffusion with Scale-Selective Uncertainty for Medical Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32135)
*Onkar Jadhav,Tim French*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Med-PODiff 是一种基于分块 POD 系数空间的扩散框架，用于脑部 MRI 超分辨率重建，并可将预测不确定性分解为粗、细尺度分量以定位幻觉风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务为脑部 MRI 超分辨率重建：输入低分辨率 MRI，输出高分辨率重建图像，目标是提升重建质量的同时识别扩散模型生成中难以从图像本身察觉的精细尺度幻觉。

Motivation: 已有扩散模型用于医学图像重建与超分辨时，采样可能包含看似合理的细尺度幻觉，且仅凭重建图像难以识别；同时像素空间或学习潜空间的扩散模型缺乏可解释的不确定性结构，无法区分不同尺度的误差来源。

Method: 在分块 POD 的紧凑可解释局部系数空间中进行生成式重建，利用 POD 结构将低秩解剖变化与细节残差变化分离；据此将预测不确定性分解为总、粗、细三部分，并定义逐像素幻觉风险分数 H_p，突出细细节不确定性主导粗结构不确定性的区域。

实验效果: 在脑部 MRI 上，重建质量优于代表性扩散基线且预测区间校准良好，总不确定性检测高误差区域 AUROC 达 0.954，H_p 定位细误差 AUROC 为 0.906，且粗/细不确定性分别优先预测对应尺度误差；受控注入与回顾性笛卡尔 k 空间欠采样实验进一步支持细细节定位与退化鲁棒性，被丢弃的 POD 尾部仅占保留细误差幅度的 3.8%，并提升下游分割一致性且推理时间显著低于像素空间扩散。

Abstract: Diffusion models are increasingly used for medical image reconstruction and super-resolution, but their samples may contain plausible fine-scale hallucinations that are difficult to identify from the reconstructed image alone. We propose Med-PODiff, a patch-wise proper orthogonal decomposition (POD) diffusion framework for brain MRI super-resolution that performs generative reconstruction in compact and interpretable local coefficient spaces. Unlike pixel-space or learned-latent diffusion models, Med-PODiff uses the POD structure to separate low-rank anatomical variation from fine-detail residual variation, enabling predictive uncertainty to be decomposed into total, coarse, and fine components. Building on this decomposition, we define a pixel-wise hallucination-risk score, $H_p$, that emphasizes regions where fine-detail uncertainty dominates coarse structural uncertainty. On brain MRI, Med-PODiff outperforms representative diffusion baselines in reconstruction quality while producing well-calibrated predictive intervals, with total uncertainty detecting high-error regions at AUROC 0.954. More importantly, the decomposed uncertainty is scale-selective: coarse and fine uncertainty preferentially predict errors at the corresponding scales, with $H_p$ localizing fine error at AUROC $0.906$. Controlled injections and retrospective Cartesian $k$-space undersampling further support fine-detail localization and degradation robustness. The discarded POD tail is only $3.8\%$ of the retained fine-error magnitude, and Med-PODiff improves downstream segmentation consistency while requiring substantially less inference time than pixel-space diffusion.

</details>


### [99] [VIDE : Training-Free Video Anomaly Detection via Visual Intrinsic Distribution Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32138)
*Kevin D. Nguetche,Atif Belal,Lilian Hollard,Eric Granger,Marco Pedersoli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VIDE是一个无需训练的框架，利用冻结的CLIP嵌入为每个测试视频自适应调整文本驱动的异常打分，从而缓解异常偏置并降低对提示选择的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 在基于嵌入的视频异常检测任务中，模型需为视频片段输出异常分数以识别异常片段；现有方法依赖文本描述构建正常性参考与异常方向，目标是提高对异常片段的排序与判别精度。

Motivation: 已有方法（如PRISM）通过沿异常方向度量对正常性参考的偏离来缓解“异常偏置”（正常片段与异常描述高度相似导致误报），但其正常性参考与异常方向均由文本导出且跨视频共享，忽略了场景间正常活动与异常外观的差异，且分数对提示选择敏感。

Method: VIDE先用初始异常分数较低的片段估计测试视频的正常场景内容，并用其部分校正文本参考；随后对各类别响应做重标定，对在多个类别上响应强烈的片段施加惩罚，并用高分片段精化异常方向；最后在多个提示视图上对分数取平均以降低提示敏感性，并融合两个冻结编码器的分数。

实验效果: 使用两个编码器时，VIDE在UCF-Crime、XD-Violence、MSAD上相对最佳PRISM结果AUC分别提升1.5、1.9、3.5个点，AP在XD-Violence与MSAD上分别提升6.5与9.3个点；其在三个基准上均为对比的无训练方法中AUC最高，并在XD-Violence与MSAD上为所有对比方法中报告的最高AUC。

Abstract: In embedding-based video anomaly detection (VAD), normal clips can have high similarity to anomalous descriptions, an anomaly bias that leads to false positives. Methods such as PRISM mitigate this bias by measuring deviations from a normality reference along anomaly directions. Both the normality reference and the anomaly directions are derived from text and shared across videos, although normal activity and the appearance of anomalies vary across scenes. The resulting scores are also sensitive to prompt selection. To address these limitations, we propose Visual Intrinsic Distribution Estimation (VIDE), a training-free framework that adapts text-derived scoring to each test video using frozen clip embeddings. VIDE estimates normal scene content from clips with low initial anomaly scores and uses this estimate to partially correct the textual reference. It then rescales category responses, penalizes clips with strong responses across categories, and refines the anomaly directions using high-scoring clips. VIDE averages scores across prompt views to reduce sensitivity to prompt selection and fuses scores from two frozen encoders. With both encoders, VIDE improves AUC over the best PRISM result by $1.5$, $1.9$, and $3.5$ points on UCF-Crime, XD-Violence, and MSAD, respectively, and AP by $6.5$ points on XD-Violence and $9.3$ points on MSAD. VIDE yields the highest AUC among the compared training-free methods on all three benchmarks and the highest reported AUC among all compared methods on XD-Violence and MSAD. Our code is included in the supplementary material and will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [100] [LiteSTAR: Low-Rank Channel Modulation for Temporally Consistent CVD Video Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32140)
*Peiyang Lin,Sichen Lin,Yipeng Liao,Liqun Lin,Xu Wang,Tiesong Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 LiteSTAR——一种仅 0.05M 参数、基于低秩通道自适应解码器 LiCaD 的轻量时空自适应视频重着色方法，并发布首个自我中心视角 CVD 视频基准 EgoCVDSet。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是面向色觉障碍（CVD）人群的视频重着色，需在设备端持续实时运行，输入为视频帧序列，输出为对 CVD 用户更具颜色区分度的重着色视频，目标是兼顾时序一致性与轻量化。

Motivation: 逐帧重着色会产生颜色闪烁，而现有方法依赖在像素或特征空间进行高开销的跨帧对齐；作者观察到闪烁与通道响应变化相关、且颜色敏感变化集中于低秩子空间，这提示可用低秩时序滤波替代显式对齐。

Method: 核心为即插即用的轻量通道自适应解码器 LiCaD：在低秩状态空间中进行时序滤波，并学习过程与观测不确定性，随后调制特征通道以实现时序一致性而无需显式空间对齐；LiteSTAR 再引入自适应图块路由以增强颜色区分度并避免过度增强。

实验效果: 定量与定性实验表明 LiteSTAR 仅用 0.05M 参数即取得更优性能，LiCaD 在不同任务与网络架构上表现良好泛化；但摘要未报告具体基准指标与对比数值。

Abstract: Color Vision Deficiency (CVD) affects a large population worldwide, yet practical recoloring must run continuously on-device. Frame-wise video recoloring produces color flickering, while existing methods rely on costly cross-frame alignment in pixel or feature space. We observe that color flickering correlates with variations in channel-wise responses, with color-sensitive variations concentrated in a low-rank subspace. Based on this observation, we propose Lightweight Spatio-Temporal Adaptive Recoloring (LiteSTAR), built around LiCaD, a plug-and-play Lightweight Channel-Adaptive Decoder. LiCaD performs temporal filtering in a low-rank state space with learned process and observation uncertainties. It then modulates feature channels to achieve temporal consistency without explicit spatial alignment. LiteSTAR further employs adaptive patch routing to improve color discrimination for CVD users while avoiding unnecessary over-enhancement. We also introduce EgoCVDSet, the first egocentric video benchmark for CVD recoloring. Extensive quantitative and qualitative experiments demonstrate that LiteSTAR achieves superior performance with only 0.05M parameters. LiCaD further demonstrates strong generalization across different tasks and network architectures. The code, models, and dataset will be released after review.

</details>


### [101] [CSC-Mamba: Bridging multimodal convolutional sparse coding and state space models for multi-contrast MRI super-resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32144)
*Iman Marivani,David Van Hamme,Jan Aelterman,Wilfried Philips*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 CSC-MAMBA，将深度展开的多模态卷积稀疏编码与 Vision Mamba 状态空间骨干统一，用于可解释且具全局建模能力的多对比度 MRI 超分辨率重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 多对比度 MRI 超分辨率的任务是从低分辨率（LR）输入重建高分辨率（HR）目标模态，并借助易于获取的参考对比度作为引导，从而加速临床扫描协议。输入为 LR 目标模态与参考对比度图像，输出为 HR 目标模态图像，目标是提升重建质量与效率。

Motivation: 现有方法存在两类互补缺陷：纯数据驱动网络依赖朴素拼接或黑箱交叉注意力等启发式融合机制，缺乏理论依据；基于模型的深度展开网络虽通过显式先验实现有原理支撑的跨对比度融合，但展开步骤仍局限于浅层卷积，缺乏可捕获全局上下文的现代深层模块。

Method: 提出 CSC-MAMBA，将深度展开的多模态卷积稀疏编码与 Vision Mamba 状态空间骨干相结合：一方面通过展开稀疏编码从引导模态中融合共同结构先验，实现可解释的模型引导式融合；另一方面利用 Mamba 骨干对对比度特异性表示进行全局细化，从而同时获得长程空间上下文与理论可解释性。

实验效果: 在 IXI 数据集上，CSC-MAMBA 的重建质量优于当前先进方法，PSNR 最高提升 0.62 dB，同时保持计算效率。

Abstract: Multi-contrast MRI super-resolution accelerates clinical protocols by reconstructing high-resolution (HR) target modalities from low-resolution (LR) inputs, guided by easily acquired reference contrasts. While purely data-driven deep neural networks achieve strong performance via rich representation capacity, they rely on heuristic fusion mechanisms (e.g., naive concatenation or black-box cross-attention) that lack theoretical grounding. Alternatively, model-based deep unfolding networks enforce principled cross-contrast fusion through explicit priors, yet their unrolled steps remain constrained to shallow convolutions, lacking modern deep blocks to capture global context. To bridge this gap, we propose CSC-MAMBA, unifying deep-unfolded multimodal convolutional sparse coding with the Vision Mamba state-space backbone. By fusing common structural priors from the guidance modality via unfolded sparse coding and globally refining contrast-specific representations with a Mamba back- bone, our approach benefits from interpretable, model-guided fusion and long-range spatial context. Experiments on the IXI dataset demonstrate that CSC-MAMBA outperforms state-of-the-art methods in reconstruction quality, achieving up to a 0.62 dB gain in PSNR while remaining computationally efficient.

</details>


### [102] [ARS-VLM: Visually Aligned Augmented Random Search for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32147)
*Xiangwei Wang,Jianyuan Lin,Peng Wang,Saman Halgamuge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ARS-VLM 是一种面向视觉语言模型的前向零阶后训练框架，通过视觉对齐的增广随机搜索缓解 patch-grid 过拟合与灾难性遗忘，在显著减少优化步数下达到与全参数 GRPO 相当的 OOD 推理精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 本文研究视觉语言模型（VLM）多模态推理的后训练问题：输入为图像-文本任务与奖励信号，输出为后训练后的 VLM 策略，目标是在避免反向传播、优化器状态和参考模型开销的前提下提升推理性能。

Motivation: 现有 GRPO 等一阶方法因反向传播、优化器状态与参考模型带来沉重内存与系统开销；而前向零阶方法（ES、top-k 的 ARS）虽可规避这些开销，却尚未用于 VLM 推理。作者发现直接套用标准 ES 或原始 ARS 会出现两类失效：(i) patch-grid 过拟合，拟合脆弱的网格特定视觉 token 伪影而忽略空间不变性；(ii) 灾难性遗忘，无界的高秩参数与策略漂移损害通用感知与 OOD 任务表现。

Method: ARS-VLM 包含三个关键步骤：(1) 从双线性配对的跨网格视图提取对比视觉基，将扰动集中于共享视觉信号并抑制 patch 位移伪影，以缓解 patch-grid 过拟合；(2) 以软子空间先验锚定浅层视觉融合层、放开深层推理层，在无参考模型下约束策略漂移；(3) 在配对视图上对扰动打分，于 top-k 选择中剔除视图脆弱方向。

实验效果: 在 Qwen3-VL-4B 与 Qwen3-VL-8B 上评测：8B 模型 20 步即达 61.0% 验证准确率，而 GRPO 需 150 步（优化步数减少 7.5 倍），并避免了原始 ARS 与 ES 的后期 OOD 崩溃；在七个 OOD 视觉推理基准上单视图微平均准确率为 75.41%，与全参数 GRPO 的 75.31% 相当，且仅使用前向后训练。

Abstract: First-order reinforcement learning, such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), is widely used to post-train Vision-Language Models (VLMs) for multimodal reasoning. However, it incurs heavy memory and system overhead from backpropagation, optimizer states, and a reference model. Forward-only zeroth-order (ZO) methods, such as Evolution Strategies (ES) and its top-k variant Augmented Random Search (ARS), avoid these overheads via reward-guided weight perturbations, yet remain unexplored for VLM reasoning. We find that directly applying standard ES or vanilla ARS to VLMs suffers from two fundamental failure modes: (i) patch-grid overfitting, fitting fragile grid-specific visual token artifacts while ignoring spatial invariants, and (ii) catastrophic forgetting, degrading general perception and out-of-distribution (OOD) tasks due to unbounded high-rank parameter and policy drift. We propose ARS-VLM, a visually aligned Augmented Random Search framework for forward-only VLM post-training. First, ARS-VLM extracts a contrastive visual basis from bilinearly paired cross-grid views, concentrating perturbations on shared visual signals while suppressing patch-shift artifacts. Second, ARS-VLM anchors shallow visual-fusion layers with a soft subspace prior while freeing deep reasoning layers, restraining policy drift without a reference model. Third, ARS-VLM scores perturbations on paired views to discard view-fragile directions during top-k selection. We evaluate ARS-VLM on Qwen3-VL-4B and Qwen3-VL-8B on multimodal reasoning tasks. On 8B, ARS-VLM reaches 61.0% validation accuracy in 20 steps versus 150 for GRPO (7.5x fewer optimization steps) and avoids the late OOD collapse of vanilla ARS and ES. Moreover, ARS-VLM achieves 75.41% single-view micro accuracy across seven OOD vision reasoning benchmarks, compared with 75.31% for full-parameter GRPO, using forward-only post-training.

</details>


### [103] [Let The Pixels Through: Rethinking Long Skip Connections for Scalable Pixel Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32151)
*Jiachen Lei,Jiahong Wu,XiangXiang Chu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过非对称长跳跃连接与 adaGLU 的像素扩散 Transformer（PiT）实现了从零训练、可平滑扩展至 1.6B 参数的强像素扩散模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 该文研究像素空间扩散模型的训练问题：以图像（及文本等多模态输入）为输入，目标是从头训练出架构简洁、超参数鲁棒且可规模化、能高质量生成图像的降噪网络。

Motivation: 现有像素扩散模型的训练依赖经验性（ad hoc）的训练配方与架构设计，且对超参数高度敏感；同时文献中 U-Net 长跳跃连接的作用被混淆，其特征拼接的线性组合方式在不同噪声水平下并不充分。

Method: 作者首先重新审视 U-Net 长跳跃连接，发现其优化收益与降噪收益并非必然绑定；其次指出跳跃特征与主干特征的常规线性组合难以适应不同噪声水平。据此提出两项修改：采用非对称长跳跃连接，将输入嵌入的细粒度信息传入解码层；在解码层引入类 SwiGLU 的 adaGLU 模块，在嵌入信息加回主干特征前进行自适应过滤。二者组合成一个简洁的像素扩散 Transformer。

实验效果: 从头训练时模型从小规模基线平滑扩展至 1.6B 参数，在 ImageNet-256 与 -512 上分别取得 1.62 与 1.70 FID；并原生支持文本、图像等多模态输入，在文生图任务上表现具竞争力。

Abstract: Training pixel diffusion models remains less straightforward, owing to the ad hoc training recipes and architecture designs, along with the sensitivity to hyper-parameter choices. We show that strong denoisers can be built by refining existing pixel-native designs from the literature. Specifically, we revisit the long skip connection in U-Net and find that the optimization benefit commonly attributed to it is not necessary for its denoising benefit. We further find that the conventional linear combination of features from the skip and main backbone is inadequate for regimes of various noise levels. Motivated by these observations, we introduce two core modifications. First, we adopt asymmetric long skips, which propagate fine-grained information from input embeddings to decoder layers. Second, we introduce adaGLU, a SwiGLU-like MLP block, in decoder layers to adaptively filter information from the embeddings before being added to main-branch features. The above designs give a simple pixel diffusion transformer architecture. When trained from scratch, it scales smoothly from small-scale baselines up to 1.6B parameters, achieving 1.62 and 1.70 FID on ImageNet-256 and -512, respectively. Besides, it natively supports multimodal inputs, such as text and images, yielding competitive performance in text-to-image generation. We release our codes at https://github.com/AMAP-ML/PiT

</details>


### [104] [RoboProbe: Benchmarking Physical Plausibility of Robot World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32152)
*Zhuoying Yang,Jiachen Ni,Yichen Guo,Xiaoshan Wu,Mengyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出五项物理规律专用指标及智能体评测框架 RoboProbe 与基准 RP-Bench，用于评估机器人世界模型生成视频的物理合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是评估生成视频的物理合理性，因为这类视频在真实执行前用于动作提议与可行性、安全性判断。输入为生成的机器人操作视频，输出为物理违规检测与评估结果，目标是支撑可靠的世界模型机器人规划。

Motivation: 当前视频生成器常产生物理上不合理的交互，而直接用视觉语言模型检测违规仍然困难，因为物理验证需要基于证据的时空推理，超出高层语义理解的能力范围。
