Across diverse backbones and benchmarks, COHERE4D substantially outperforms prior methods in human--scene alignment, metric scene and camera pose reconstruction, and global human motion estimation, adding only 4,ms/frame, enabling overall inference at up to 14 FPS.

</details>


### [171] [X-3DComposer: Feed-Forward 3D Scene Composition with Any Assets from Any Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32491)
*Zidian Qiu,Ancong Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: X-3DComposer 是一个前馈模型，将 3D 场景布局预测与资产生成分离，在一次约 1 秒的前向传播中联合预测所有物体的旋转、平移与逐轴尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是从一张或多张场景图像出发，为每个物体配好资产并确定其布局，从而构建可编辑的 3D 场景；输入为场景图像、物体掩码及任意来源的每个物体资产，输出为全部物体的六自由度姿态与尺寸，目标是得到高精度的场景级布局。

Motivation: 现有布局方法存在明显缺口：场景生成方法把布局与资产生成耦合在一起，无法独立替换布局；姿态估计器一次只处理一个物体，且依赖真实尺寸 CAD 模型、深度或迭代优化。

Method: 核心思想是把物体当作视图：将资产渲染图与场景图像一同送入由 VGGT-Ω 改造的多视图几何模型，由每个物体渲染图所预测的相机以闭式解直接推出其姿态；模型为前馈式，相机内参、相机位姿与深度均为可选输入，一次前向即可输出全部物体的旋转、平移与逐轴缩放。

实验效果: 在固定五种场景生成方法所生成网格、仅替换布局的设定下，X-3DComposer 的布局精度优于这些方法原生布局；与独立布局模型 SceneMaker 相比，在 IndoorMix（源自六个室内数据集的测试集）上场景级 Chamfer 距离降低 52%，在 3D-FUTURE 上降低 28%。

Abstract: Composing an editable 3D scene from images requires an asset for each object and a layout that places the assets. 3D generation and CAD retrieval now supply the assets, but layout still lacks a general solution: scene generation methods couple it with generation, and pose estimators handle one object at a time and need true-size CAD models, depth, or iterative refinement. We present X-3DComposer, a feed-forward model that predicts the layout separately from asset generation. Given one or more images of a scene, the object masks, and an asset for each object from any source, it jointly predicts the rotation, translation, and per-axis scale of all objects in a single forward pass of about one second. The key idea is to treat objects as views: renders of the assets are processed together with the scene images by a multi-view geometry model adapted from VGGT-$Ω$, and each object pose follows in closed form from the cameras predicted for its renders. Camera intrinsics, camera poses, and depth are optional inputs. When the meshes produced by five scene generation methods are kept fixed and only their layout is replaced, the layouts of X-3DComposer are more accurate than the native layouts of these methods. Compared with the stand-alone layout model SceneMaker, it reduces the scene-level Chamfer distance by 52% on IndoorMix, our test set from six indoor datasets, and by 28% on 3D-FUTURE.

</details>


### [172] [CalibTrack: Exploring Learned Heteroscedastic Covariance for Robust Multi-Camera Pedestrian Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32500)
*Jack Roberts,Aditya Iyer,Nora Ayanian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究学习到的异方差测量协方差对解析式多视角多目标跟踪的校准与鲁棒性影响，并提出检测端预测器 CalibTrack。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 解析式多视角多目标跟踪（MV-MOT）系统依赖测量协方差来引导关联、融合与状态估计；本文目标是用逐检测的协方差替代均匀/启发式协方差，并评估其对多相机行人跟踪的校准与鲁棒性作用。

Motivation: 现有协方差估计通常是启发式的，需针对具体部署场景手工调参，缺乏对学习式异方差协方差在解析跟踪框架内影响的原则性分析。

Method: 提出 CalibTrack——检测端的协方差预测器，将统一测量协方差替换为逐检测协方差；在 WildTrack 与 MultiviewX 数据集上接入多种解析跟踪核心（身份信息引导的 GM-PHD、JIPDA、剪枝 delta-GLMB 滤波器），并用 stable diffusion 模拟恶劣天气、高斯噪声、相机偏色与相机丢失来评估鲁棒性。

实验效果: 在恶劣条件下，CalibTrack 相较置信度缩放协方差提升了平均跟踪性能并给出更校准的预测；但与更强的启发式协方差方法相比，学习协方差的收益受限于观测质量与下游跟踪器选择。

Abstract: Analytical multi-view multi-object tracking (MV-MOT) systems rely on measurement covariance estimates to guide association, fusion, and state estimation. These covariance estimates are typically heuristic, requiring hand-tuning to the specific deployment scenario. We investigate how learned heteroscedastic measurement covariance affects calibration and robustness in analytical multi-camera tracking. To support this analysis, we introduce CalibTrack, a detector-side predictor that replaces uniform measurement covariance with per-detection covariance. We evaluate CalibTrack on the WildTrack and MultiviewX pedestrian tracking datasets with multiple analytical tracker cores, including an identity-informed GM-PHD, JIPDA, and pruned delta-GLMB filter. We additionally evaluate robustness by simulating adverse weather conditions with stable diffusion, Gaussian noise, camera browning, and camera dropout. CalibTrack improves average adverse tracking performance over confidence-scaled covariance, while producing better calibrated predictions, indicating the value of heteroscedastic covariance within analytical tracking. Comparisons with stronger heuristic covariance methods show that the benefits of learned covariance remain bounded by the quality of available observations and the downstream tracker choice. Together, these findings provide a principled exploration of learned covariance estimation and its potential for uncertainty-aware analytical tracking.

</details>


### [173] [Déjà-VAE: Training-Free Consistent Editing with Pseudo-Invertible Encoders and Latent Back-Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32501)
*Ron Libman,Yamit Ehrlich,Ohad Fried,Assaf Shocher*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Déjà-VAE：无需训练即实现精确自编码一致性与校准式编辑稳定化的统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 在潜扩散模型工作流中，需要保证潜在空间与像素空间往返、以及连续多次编辑时除用户明确要求的变化外信息不丢失；目标是在反复处理中抑制结构、纹理与颜色的累积漂移。

Motivation: 现有流程中自编码误差与编辑模型引入的非预期改动会在多次操作间累积，逐步破坏图像；而专用一致性方法仍需额外训练或微调，成本高且不保证精确性。

Method: 方法不训练不微调，包含两部分：(1) 将预训练VAE转为满射伪可逆网络，以固定增益平衡对原编码器与解码器的偏离，修改后的解码器D'是编码器E'的显式非线性右逆，构造上满足E'(D'(z))=z，故D'为自反广义逆，潜在往返保持输入（数值精度内）、重复重建幂等；(2) 用自适应小波反投影，在匹配低于校准阈值的低频小波系数约束下，求编辑输出在ℓ2意义下最近的潜变量，每轮仅校正一次，校准后无需额外网络评估。

实验效果: 摘要未报告具体数值；仅定性说明在多种模型与编辑基准上，漂移低于原始流水线与专用一致性方法。

Abstract: Consistency is essential to generative workflows: repeated processing should preserve information beyond the changes explicitly requested. In latent diffusion models, this requires stability both when moving between latent and pixel spaces and when applying successive edits. Autoencoding errors and unintended changes from the editing model can accumulate across these operations, progressively altering image structure, texture, and color. We introduce Déjà-VAE, a framework that combines exact autoencoding consistency with calibrated editing stabilization, without training or fine-tuning. Its autoencoding component converts a pretrained variational autoencoder into a surjective pseudo-invertible neural network, with a fixed gain balancing deviations from the pretrained encoder and decoder. The modified decoder $D'$ is an explicit nonlinear right inverse of the encoder $E'$, satisfying $E'(D'(z))=z$ by construction. Consequently, $D'$ is a reflexive generalized inverse: latent round trips preserve their input, up to numerical precision, and repeated image reconstruction is idempotent. To suppress editing drift, adaptive wavelet back-projection finds the closest latent to the editor's output in $\ell_2$, subject to matching the previous latent on wavelet coefficients whose changes fall below calibrated thresholds. This correction is applied once per turn, with no additional network evaluations after calibration. Together, these components support workflows that remain in latent space or interleave generation with pixel-space operations and constraints. Tests across models and editing benchmarks show less drift than stock pipelines and dedicated consistency methods.

</details>


### [174] [An Integrated Remote Sensing and Machine Learning Framework for Mapping Hazard Associated with Leached Metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32505)
*Salman Karim,Bahareh Nojabaei,Hosein Foroutan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究融合哨兵二号植被特征、环境协变量与地下水监测数据，用机器学习对弗吉尼亚州四个燃煤残渣场址开展场地校准式的沥滤金属相关危害空间筛查，但跨场址零样本泛化能力有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 研究任务是以美国弗吉尼亚州四个燃煤燃烧残渣（CCR）场址为对象，将地下水金属浓度换算为危害商（HQ）并合成综合危害指数（HI）作为目标变量，输入为Sentinel-2植被指数、生物物理参数、时空指标（2019–2025）及土壤、气候、水文、地形和土地覆盖数据，目标是构建可空间筛查金属沥滤危害的机器学习分类模型。

Motivation: 现有做法缺乏对CCR场址溶解金属污染的低成本空间筛查手段，而遥感能否作为地下水金属危害的有效替代信号、植被特征是否真正提供增量信息尚不明确，需要系统检验特征贡献与跨场址可迁移性。

Method: 将各组分地下水浓度转为HQ并合成为HI，构建多源特征集；用空间隔离测试块、特征集消融和留一场址交叉验证评估六种监督分类器，并做逐特征与距离变量的对比分析，以检验植被特征的必要性及模型跨场址泛化能力。

实验效果: 在33个金属—场址组合中有24个邻近监测井处高HQ伴随低植被指数（符号检验p=0.007），三个场址HI与CIRE、冠层叶绿素含量负相关（Spearman ρ最低−0.71）；随机森林场内HI分类精度0.768–0.924、高HI类召回0.965–0.999，34个组分—场址组合HQ分类精度0.775–0.989；环境协变量贡献普遍大于植被特征，移除植被预测因子不降低性能，单用至CCR单元距离在某场址表现最佳；跨场址零样本精度降至0.734–0.749，不优于多数类基线。

Abstract: This study integrates satellite remote sensing, groundwater monitoring, and machine learning to map leached-metal-associated hazards at four coal combustion residual (CCR) sites in Virginia, USA. Constituent-specific groundwater concentrations were expressed as Hazard Quotients (HQ) and combined into a composite Hazard Index (HI). Predictors included Sentinel-2 vegetation indices, biophysical parameters, and spatiotemporal metrics (2019-2025), alongside soil, climate, hydrology, topography, and land cover. Six supervised classifiers were evaluated using spatially separated test blocks, feature-set ablation, and leave-one-site-out testing. Higher HQ coincided with lower vegetation indices in 24 of 33 metal-site combinations near monitoring wells (sign test p = 0.007). Well HI was negatively correlated with CIRE and canopy chlorophyll content at three sites (Spearman rho as low as -0.71). Random Forest achieved within-site HI classification accuracies of 0.768-0.924 and higher-HI-class recall of 0.965-0.999. For 34 constituent-site combinations, HQ classification accuracies ranged from 0.775 to 0.989. Environmental covariates generally contributed more predictive information than vegetation features. Removing vegetation predictors did not reduce performance, and distance to the CCR unit alone performed best at one site. Zero-shot cross-site accuracy declined to 0.734-0.749, no better than the majority-class baseline. These findings demonstrate the potential of integrating satellite observations and environmental data with groundwater measurements for site-calibrated spatial hazard screening. The framework identifies indirect environmental associations rather than directly detecting dissolved metals, highlighting the importance of local calibration and independent validation.

</details>


### [175] [GeoVLDrive: Geometry-Grounded Vision-Language Reasoning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32510)
*JiaHui Huang,Songyan Zhang,Hanbing Li,Hanchao Leng,Kuiyuan Yang,Guang Chen,Hangjun Ye,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoVLDrive 是一个以三维几何为根基的 VLA 框架，通过门控交叉注意力融合 3D 基础模型多层特征、结构化潜空间思维链与场景打分的轨迹排序，实现 3D 时空推理与规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 面向自动驾驶规划任务，框架以视觉-语言骨干为基础，输入场景视觉与语言信息，输出未来轨迹规划，目标是让推理建立在 3D 场景结构及其时序演化之上。

Motivation: 现有 VLA 规划器虽继承了预训练视觉-语言模型的语义知识，但其推理很少真正锚定于 3D 场景结构及其随时间的变化，缺乏度量级空间与运动理解。

Method: 将 3D 基础模型的多层特征经门控交叉注意力注入视觉-语言骨干；用结构化潜空间思维链把未来场景几何、关键智能体运动与元动作组织为有序且分别监督的潜状态；动作专家据此与多层场景特征生成候选轨迹，并用场景锚定、相互作用的打分 token 进行排序；同时构建 NAVSIM 3DQA 用于训练与评测度量级 3D 空间与运动理解。

实验效果: 在 NAVSIM v1 上取得 91.8 PDMS、v2 上 90.9 EPDMS；在 NAVSIM 3DQA 上几何根基骨干总体得分 89.2%，比微调的 Qwen3-VL-2B 基线高 4.6 个百分点；在 LingoQA 上 Lingo-Judge 准确率为 68.6%。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) planners inherit semantic knowledge from pretrained vision-language models, but their reasoning is rarely grounded in 3D scene structure and its evolution over time. We introduce GeoVLDrive, a geometry-grounded VLA framework for 3D spatiotemporal reasoning and planning. Multi-layer features from a 3D foundation model augment the vision-language backbone through gated cross-attention. A structured latent chain of thought (CoT) organizes future scene geometry, key-agent motion, and meta-actions into ordered, separately supervised latent states. An action expert generates trajectory proposals from these states and multi-layer scene features, and ranks them using scene-grounded, interacting score tokens. We also introduce NAVSIM 3DQA to train and evaluate metric 3D spatial and motion understanding. GeoVLDrive achieves 91.8 PDMS on NAVSIM v1 and 90.9 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2. On NAVSIM 3DQA, the geometry-grounded backbone scores 89.2% overall, 4.6 percentage points above the fine-tuned Qwen3-VL-2B baseline. On LingoQA, the backbone achieves 68.6% Lingo-Judge accuracy.

</details>


### [176] [DiffAnchor: Anchor-Space Diffusion for Monocular 3D Hand Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32513)
*Changlong Jiang,Youci Tang,Haohong Kuang,Hang Xu,Jinghong Zheng,Ran Wang,Zhenghong Yu,Chen Feng,Xiaofeng Yue,Yang Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DiffAnchor 提出在锚点空间而非关节坐标上做少步扩散的单目 3D 手部姿态估计框架，以少量去噪步数和单一假设实现高精度与高推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 该任务面向具身人机交互与灵巧操作的单目 3D 手部姿态估计，输入单张图像，输出 3D 关节坐标，目标是在保证精度的同时兼顾推理效率。

Motivation: 现有扩散式方法直接对最终关节坐标进行迭代去噪，生成过程需同时探索合理姿态并恢复精确坐标，往往依赖额外去噪步数或多假设采样，导致推理成本显著上升。

Method: 核心思路是让随机探索不直接作用于最终输出：扩散仅搜索有信息量的中间锚点，再由锚点到关节的偏移预测与加权聚合完成精确定位。固定上下文锚点提供稳定全局参考，带噪动态锚点被迭代细化至有信息的关节区域；重要性引导的锚点选择与补充机制保留有效锚点、剔除无效锚点并探索覆盖不足区域。该设计降低了扩散的精度负担，因而仅需少量去噪步与单一假设。

实验效果: 在 HO-3D V2、NYU、ICVL 和 InterHand2.6M 上的实验表明，前三个基准达到最优性能，在具有挑战性的双手交互估计上具备竞争力，且推理效率与确定性回归基线相当。

Abstract: Accurate and efficient monocular 3D hand pose estimation is an important perception capability for embodied human interaction and dexterous manipulation. Recent diffusion-based methods model pose uncertainty by iteratively denoising 3D joint coordinates. However, directly diffusing the final joints requires the generative process to simultaneously explore plausible poses and recover precise coordinates, often relying on additional denoising steps or multiple hypotheses and consequently increasing inference cost. In this work, we propose DiffAnchor, a few-step anchor-space diffusion framework that diffuses anchors rather than joints. Our key insight is that stochastic exploration does not need to operate directly on the final output. Instead, diffusion searches for informative intermediate anchors that serve as local 3D regressors, while anchor-to-joint offset prediction and weighted aggregation complete precise joint localization. Specifically, fixed context anchors provide stable global references, whereas noisy dynamic anchors are iteratively refined toward informative joint regions. An importance-guided anchor selection and replenishment mechanism further retains useful anchors, removes ineffective ones, and explores insufficiently covered regions. This design reduces the precision burden of the diffusion process and enables accurate prediction using only a few denoising steps and a single hypothesis. Extensive experiments on HO-3D V2, NYU, ICVL, and InterHand2.6M demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on the first three benchmarks and competitive performance on challenging interacting-hand estimation, while maintaining inference efficiency comparable to deterministic regression baselines.

</details>


### [177] [HS-OVSeg: Multi-Granularity Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation in Remote Sensing via Heterogeneous Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32518)
*Wei Huang,Chenying Liu,Xudong Kang,Xiao Xiang Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 HS-OVSeg 统一训练框架，融合场景级、目标级与像素级异构监督，提升遥感开放词汇语义分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 遥感开放词汇语义分割（RS-OVSS）旨在以文本驱动的方式对遥感影像进行像素级语义映射，突破预定义类别限制。其输入为遥感影像与任意类别文本，输出为对应像素级分割掩码，目标是在无预设类别表的前提下实现灵活分割。

Motivation: 现有 RS-OVSS 受限于缺乏大规模、语义多样且具精确像素级标注的数据集；而现有遥感数据集虽含丰富的场景级与目标级标注，却缺少像素级监督，二者的互补价值尚未被利用。

Method: 提出 HS-OVSeg 统一训练框架：利用 SAM3，以场景级类别名、以及带边界框的目标级类别名为条件生成像素级伪标签；将其与真实语义掩码结合，通过 LoRA 微调 SAM3，实现场景、目标、像素三种标注粒度的联合训练，覆盖 20 个遥感数据集（3 个场景分类、4 个目标检测、13 个语义分割）。

实验效果: 在 20 个训练来源数据集及 5 个留出数据集上，HS-OVSeg 均显著优于现有 RS-OVSS 模型，展现出异构监督下的强多粒度分割能力；摘要未报告具体数值指标。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS) enables flexible, text-driven mapping of remote sensing (RS) imagery beyond predefined categories. Despite advances in segmentation foundation models, RS-OVSS remains constrained by the scarcity of large-scale, semantically diverse datasets with accurate pixel-level annotations. Meanwhile, existing RS datasets contain abundant scene- and object-level annotations that offer valuable semantic information but lack precise pixel-wise supervision. To exploit these complementary sources, we propose HS-OVSeg, a unified training framework that integrates heterogeneous scene-, object-, and pixel-level supervision for RS-OVSS. Specifically, we generate pixel-wise pseudo-labels using SAM3, conditioned on scene-level class names and object-level class names with bounding boxes. Combined with ground-truth semantic masks, these pseudo-labels enable joint training across all three annotation granularities through LoRA-based fine-tuning of SAM3. Our unified training spans 20 RS datasets, including 3 scene classification, 4 object detection, and 13 semantic segmentation datasets, with 1,000 images from the 7 classification and detection datasets manually annotated for pixel-level evaluation. HS-OVSeg substantially outperforms existing RS-OVSS models across both the 20 training-source and 5 held-out datasets, demonstrating strong multi-granularity segmentation capabilities under heterogeneous supervision. The code will be publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [178] [Rescuing Discarded Boundary Samples in Semi-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32532)
*Yuzhao Jiang,Meng Zhang,Jie Ma,Xiaohong Guan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出即插即用的边界细化与纠正方法 BRC，在保留置信度阈值伪标签选择规则的前提下回收被丢弃的边界样本并纠正其伪标签，从而缓解确认偏差并改善决策边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 半监督学习依赖伪标签范式，训练时需用置信度阈值过滤低置信样本以抑制确认偏差，输入为有标签数据与大量无标签数据，输出为分类（并可扩展至分割）模型，目标是提升伪标签利用效率与模型精度。

Motivation: 现有阈值过滤策略虽能降低偏差，但被丢弃的样本多位于类边界附近，导致宝贵的边界信息随之丢失；因此需要在不改变基座SSL伪标签选择机制的情况下挽救这些样本。

Method: BRC作为置信度阈值式SSL的附加模块：保持原有基于置信度的选择规则不变，仅作用于被拒绝样本集合；先识别其中仍具训练价值的样本，再以纠正后的软伪标签替换其原始伪标签，并将其重新纳入无监督损失。

实验效果: 在CIFAR-100、STL-10、TissueMNIST上验证了跨数据集与多种基座SSL方法的适用性，错误率最多降低4.97个百分点；在边界附近样本上Top-1准确率提升12.80个百分点；语义分割探索性扩展取得1.03–2.39 mIoU的稳定增益。

Abstract: The field of semi-supervised learning (SSL) has seen substantial progress, driven by ongoing refinements within the pseudo-label SSL paradigm. The main difficulty lies in reducing the adverse impact of confirmation bias. The predominant approach is to apply a confidence threshold during training to discard low-confidence samples. Nevertheless, while this mitigates the bias, the discarded samples are mostly situated near class boundaries, and consequently, this strategy also loses valuable boundary information. To address this problem, we propose Boundary Refinement and Correction (BRC), an add-on for confidence-thresholded SSL that rescues useful discarded samples without changing how the base SSL method selects pseudo-labels. BRC keeps the original confidence-based selection rule and operates only on the rejected set. It first identifies discarded samples that may still be useful for training, then replaces their original pseudo-labels with corrected soft pseudo-labels and returns them to the unsupervised loss. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-100, STL-10, and TissueMNIST demonstrate the flexibility and broad applicability of BRC across diverse datasets and base SSL methods, reducing error rates by up to 4.97 percentage points. On boundary-near samples, BRC further improves Top-1 accuracy by 12.80 percentage points, directly supporting its ability to refine the model's decision boundary. An exploratory extension to semantic segmentation also yields consistent gains of 1.03-2.39 mIoU, indicating that BRC generalizes beyond image classification.

</details>


### [179] [RSReasoner: Reformulating and Auditing Geospatial Reasoning Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32536)
*Xu Zhang,Xinpeng Zhang,Yunrui Zou,Hongliang Chen,Xiaotian Jiang,Yang Zheng,Kaitai Guo,Jimin Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文通过形式化重构与基准审计揭示现有地理空间推理分割基准存在属性混淆与感知捷径问题，并构建RSReasoner数据集与RSReasoner-Bench诊断基准加以改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 地理空间推理分割任务要求根据语言描述定位地理目标，且推理需超越直接视觉感知（语义推断）；输入为遥感影像与语言查询，输出为目标定位结果，目标是精确评估并提升模型的推理能力。

Motivation: 现有基准常把"推理"简化为类别省略（隐式类别），忽视属性混淆问题：既缺失显式类别推理，又引入感知捷径，无法精确评估推理能力。作者审计90,708条现有查询发现，纯"显式类别+推断属性"查询仅占0.61%，而31.98%混合了感知与推断两类属性。

Method: 首先按类别表达（显式/隐式）与属性性质（感知/推断）两个维度重构地理空间推理分割的分类框架，并据此审计现有基准；随后设计三阶段流程，利用OpenStreetMap属性并抑制几何与语义噪声，构建大规模数据集RSReasoner（106,610张图像、213,220条推理查询）与诊断基准RSReasoner-Bench（75,400条查询，覆盖四类典型类型及受控变体）。

实验效果: 实验表明RSReasoner-Bench能在多维度上精确评估模型能力并暴露捷径行为，且在RSReasoner上微调可同时提升推理性能与跨类型泛化能力（摘要未给出具体数值对比）。

Abstract: Geospatial reasoning segmentation aims to localize geographic targets from language requiring semantic inference beyond direct visual perception. However, existing benchmarks often operationalize reasoning as category omission while overlooking attribute mixing, which neglects explicit-category reasoning, introduces perceptual shortcuts, and fails to precisely evaluate reasoning capability. To diagnose and address these limitations, we first reformulate geospatial reasoning segmentation along category expression (explicit vs. implicit) and attribute nature (perceptual vs. inferential), and audit 90,708 queries across existing benchmarks to reveal that pure explicit-inferential queries account for only 0.61% whereas 31.98% mix both attribute types. We then develop a three-stage pipeline that harnesses OpenStreetMap attributes while mitigating geometric and semantic noise to construct RSReasoner, a large-scale dataset of 106,610 images with 213,220 reasoning queries, and RSReasoner-Bench, a diagnostic benchmark of 75,400 queries across four canonical types and controlled variants. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RSReasoner-Bench precisely evaluates model capability across multiple dimensions while exposing shortcut behaviors, and fine-tuning on RSReasoner boosts both reasoning and cross-type generalization. Our findings offer key insights for future geospatial reasoning research and benchmark design. Datasets and code will be released at https://github.com/Ricardo-XZ/RSReasoner.

</details>


### [180] [Advanced Synthetic Image Detection Using Hybrid Feature Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32538)
*Ameya Uppina,S Navaneetha Krishnan,Nikhil N Iyer,Joe Dhanith P R*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于深度学习特征分析的合成图像检测方法，通过选择性图像块（patch）的DCT评分选取高/低复杂度区域，结合SRM模块与ResNet50（含CBAM注意力）分类真实与AI生成图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是区分真实图像与GAN、MidJourney、DALL-E等生成模型产生的合成图像，输入为待判别图像，输出为真/伪二分类结果，目标是构建可靠的合成图像取证检测框架，以应对生成图像日益逼真带来的鉴别需求。

Motivation: 现有检测工作未充分利用生成模型难以复现的特征差异（如细微纹理与复杂物体结构），且直接对全图建模会稀释这些局部痕迹，因此需要针对性地选取具有判别力的图像区域进行细粒度特征分析。

Method: 采用选择性图像块分析：先用离散余弦变换（DCT）对图像块打分以衡量复杂度，分别提取最复杂与最不复杂的区域；将其分别送入SRM模块（捕捉高频噪声/纹理残差）和ResNet50模块处理；同时借助CBAM注意力与ResNet50结构检查复杂区域的形变特征，最终融合特征完成真伪分类。

实验效果: 摘要仅定性报告该方法在真实图像与合成图像分类上取得较高准确率，未给出具体基准数据集、对比方法或数值指标。

Abstract: In the realm of generative AI, models such as GANs, MidJourney, DALL-E, and others have been continuously evolving to generate increasingly lifelike images. This rapid advancement has created an urgent need for robust methods to distinguish between real and AI-generated images. This paper proposes a novel approach for the detection of synthetic images by employing deep learning techniques for feature analysis. The proposed architecture classifies images by identifying variations that generative models struggle to replicate, particularly fine-grained textures and complex object features. Deformations in complexes are examined with the help of the CBAM architecture along with ResNet50. We have performed a selective patch-based analysis using Discrete Cosine Transform (DCT) scoring, extracting the regions with the most and least complexity for further processing with an SRM Module and ResNet50 module. Experimental results show that our approach achieves high accuracy in classifying real-world images from synthetic ones, contributing to the development of more reliable detection frameworks.

</details>


### [181] [Smooth Essential-Manifold Residuals for View-Graph Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32540)
*Viktor Larsson,Johannes Schönberger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种新的本质矩阵流形距离残差表示，可将视图图标定问题转化为高斯-牛顿近似满秩、无需逐边SVD且收敛更快的非线性最小二乘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 视图图标定利用多组成对基础矩阵联合估计相机内参，正确内参将各基础矩阵转换为本质矩阵，从而把标定表述为最小化到本质矩阵流形的距离。输入为基础矩阵集合，输出为相机内参与标定结果，目标是高效、准确地求解该非线性最小二乘问题。

Motivation: 传统奇异值形式把本质矩阵的两个独立约束压缩为单一标量残差，导致逐边高斯-牛顿模型秩亏，收敛性能差，因此需要一种既不损失几何意义又能保证数值良态的残差表示。

Method: 作者推导出到2×2矩阵的等距约简，使本质矩阵非线性约束的每个分支变为线性子空间，从而得到一个光滑的二维残差：其平方范数恰等于原始流形距离，其高斯-牛顿近似在一般情况下满秩，且评估时无需逐边奇异值分解。

实验效果: 摘要未报告具体数值指标，但指出实验表明新残差保持几何目标的估计精度，同时以更少且计算更廉价的迭代收敛；结合鲁棒初始化与优化后，在接近廉价代数目标的运行时间下取得当前最优的视图图标定精度。

Abstract: View-graph calibration jointly estimates camera intrinsics from multiple pairwise fundamental matrices. The correct intrinsics transform each fundamental matrix into an essential matrix, casting the calibration problem as minimizing the distance to the essential manifold. We study how to represent this objective for nonlinear least squares. The conventional singular-value formulation collapses two independent essential-matrix constraints into one scalar residual. Its per-edge Gauss-Newton model is therefore rank deficient, which leads to poor convergence. We instead derive an isometric reduction to 2x2 matrices, where each branch of the nonlinear essential-matrix constraint becomes a linear subspace. This reduction yields a smooth two-dimensional residual whose squared norm is exactly the original manifold distance, whose Gauss-Newton approximation is generically full rank, and whose evaluation requires no per-edge singular value decomposition. Experiments show that the new residual retains the estimation accuracy of the geometric objective while converging in fewer, computationally cheaper iterations. Combined with robust initialization and optimization, it achieves state-of-the-art view-graph calibration accuracy at a runtime comparable to inexpensive algebraic objectives.

</details>


### [182] [Align or Fail: The Hidden Gap in Cross-Domain Panoptic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32542)
*Huy Hoang Nguyen,Oliver Zendel,Tobias Glueck,Minh Nhat Vu,Andreas Kugi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文以 ODISE 为测试平台，发现视觉基础主干升级会引入"分组-识别失配"，导致开放词汇全景分割跨域性能崩溃，并区分出可修复的视觉-CLIP接口缺口与词汇边界限制两类失效轴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 开放词汇全景分割（OVPS）中，视觉基础主干常被当作即插即用的升级手段以改进区域候选。本文以带 CLIP 识别分支的扩散式 OVPS 模型 ODISE 为对象，输入为图像与开放词汇文本，目标是提升源域与跨域分割性能。

Motivation: 现有工作默认更强主干必然带来下游收益，但主干改进往往难以转化为跨域性能提升，其失效机理未被解释，缺少对"主干升级—识别分支"之间兼容性问题的诊断。

Method: 将 ODISE 的扩散视觉提取器替换为 DINOv2、ConvNeXt 或 DINOv3，定位出致密区域特征与 CLIP 文本嵌入之间的"分组-识别失配"对齐缺口；结合分阶段诊断、线性探针与区域-文本对齐分析验证语义是否保留；再测试文本侧适配器、保形掩码保留（CMR）与仅 2.7M 参数的视觉侧 LoRA 等适配方案；并评估 OVSeg、CAT-Seg、MaskCLIP 以解释 ADE20K 的顽固性。

实验效果: DINOv3 变体在源域与近域提升明显，却在多个跨域目标上崩溃，而其他基线呈相反趋势；诊断表明 DINOv3 嵌入保留语义但几何上与 CLIP 不兼容。文本侧适配对词汇重叠更敏感，CMR 收益有限且依赖目标域，视觉侧 LoRA 可修复大部分视觉-to-CLIP 接口缺口，而 ADE20K 的失效归因于各 CLIP 识别系统共有的词汇边界限制。

Abstract: Vision foundation backbones are often treated as drop-in upgrades for improving region proposals in open-vocabulary panoptic segmentation (OVPS). Yet, improvements in backbones often fail to translate well into downstream cross-domain performance. Using ODISE, a diffusion-based OVPS model with a CLIP-based recognition branch, as a testbed, we replace its diffusion visual extractor with DINOv2, ConvNeXt, or DINOv3, and find that backbone upgrades can introduce a grouping-recognition mismatch: an alignment gap between dense-region features and CLIP text embeddings. Across source and near-domain, the newer DINOv3 variant improves source-domain performance but collapses on several cross-domain targets, whereas other baselines, interestingly, show the opposite. Stage-wise diagnostics, linear probes, and region-text alignment analyses indicate that DINOv3 embeddings retain semantic information but are geometrically incompatible with CLIP. We test adaptations, including text-side adapters, conformal mask retention (CMR), and a lean 2.7M-parameter visual-side LoRA, and find that text-side adaptation is more sensitive to vocabulary overlap. CMR provides only limited and target-specific gains, indicating that retention alone cannot resolve the full cross-domain failure. To understand why ADE20K resists improvement across all interventions, we evaluate OVSeg, CAT-Seg, and MaskCLIP. This separates two failure axes: a recoverable visual-to-CLIP interface gap that visual-side LoRA largely repairs, and a vocabulary-boundary limitation shared by the evaluated CLIP-based recognition systems that requires different remedies.

</details>


### [183] [SetCon: Setup-Conditioned IMU-Language Learning from 3D Human Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32548)
*Lennart Schiweck,Alaa Saleh,Cristóbal Curio*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SetCon，用"选择性不变性"替代完全不变性，通过估计设备佩戴方式并以 3D 人体运动为监督，显著提升 IMU-语言模型在真实数据集上的零样本活动识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是将 IMU 信号与文本对齐，实现跨穿戴设备的零样本人体活动识别。输入为已知身体部位上佩戴设备采集的 IMU 信号窗口，输出为对应活动类别；由于信号取决于设备安装方式（佩戴部位与固定方式的组合），目标是让模型在未见过的设备配置下仍能准确识别活动。

Motivation: 已有领先的 IMU-语言模型为使编码器对佩戴方式保持不变，强制学习完全不变表示，但同时丢弃了设备相对身体的朝向信息——例如可用于区分坐、站、卧的重力方向。这一关键信息损失限制了模型的判别能力，因此需要一种既能跨配置泛化、又保留姿态相关线索的方案。

Method: SetCon 以"选择性不变性"取代完全不变性：利用少量无标签窗口，在已知身体部位上估计设备的佩戴方式，并以此对编码器进行条件化。由于佩戴方式在佩戴期间近似固定，运动的人体本身可作为标定参照。标定与表示学习均以 3D 人体运动为监督，因为同一动作可在多种已知传感器配置下被仿真。测试时，类别由类名或"锚点"表示，锚点平均类名与活动描述的文本嵌入，无需任何带标签窗口；基于冻结编码器构建的语言模型还可回答动作的执行方式与顺序。

实验效果: 仅在由与 AnyMo 相同的 Nymeria 动作捕捉仿真的 IMU 数据上训练，SetCon 在 13 个真实数据集上取得 56.6%（类名）与 65.3%（锚点）的平均零样本准确率，而 AnyMo 分别为 41.8% 与 46.7%。

Abstract: Aligning inertial measurement unit (IMU) signals with text enables zero-shot activity recognition across wearable devices. A worn device's signal depends on its setup, the combination of body site and mounting. Leading IMU-language models make their encoders invariant to the mounting, which also discards the device's orientation relative to the body, such as the direction of gravity that separates sitting, standing and lying. We introduce SetCon, which replaces full invariance with selective invariance. It estimates each device's mounting on its known body site from a few unlabeled windows and conditions its encoder on this estimate. Because the mounting stays nearly fixed while the device is worn, the moving body serves as a calibration target. Both calibration and representation learning are supervised by 3D human motion, since one movement can be simulated under many known sensor setups. At test time, a class is represented by its name or by an anchor, which averages the text embeddings of the name and of activity descriptions and needs no labeled window. A language model built on the frozen encoder also answers how and in what order a movement is performed. Trained only on IMU simulated from the same Nymeria motion capture as AnyMo, SetCon reaches 56.6% mean zero-shot accuracy on 13 real datasets with class names and 65.3% with anchors, against 41.8% and 46.7% for AnyMo.

</details>


### [184] [MinPair: Measured Minimal Pairs for Fine-Grained Control in Text-to-Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32549)
*Lennart Schiweck,Cristóbal Curio*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MinPair：用规则化运动描述符让语言模型生成"最小对比"字幕对，并配合对比配对损失，使文本到运动扩散模型真正利用单属性词汇来控制动作执行方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 文本到运动生成任务以自然语言字幕为输入、生成3D人体运动序列，目标是不仅生成正确的动作类别，还要按字幕精确控制动作的执行方式（如高度等属性）。

Motivation: 现有方法擅长捕捉"做什么动作"，但难以控制"如何执行"：仅改动字幕中表示高度的词时，所测试的SOTA方法都无法同时满足膝、腰、头的高度要求。原因在于字幕中其他词往往也隐含高度等属性，训练损失与标准指标都不强制模型使用明确指出该属性的那个词——只有当该词是区分两条本应相同的字幕的唯一信息时，模型才必须依赖它。

Method: MinPair分三步：(1) 用基于规则的运动描述符度量每个真实运动片段的三维身体运动，并由语言模型据此写出带时间标注、逐身体部位描述运动方式的"最小配对"字幕；(2) 用同一描述符找出同一动作两片段在哪些属性上存在差异，构造最小对比对，即把一条片段的真实字幕与只改动一个属性取值（取另一片段的实测值）的改写副本配对；(3) 施加对比配对损失，要求扩散去噪器从真实字幕重建片段比从改写副本重建更准确。该方法无需合成运动数据，推理阶段保持不变。

实验效果: 在22个目标任务及每次只改一个属性的字幕编辑上评测：MinPair字幕本身即提升目标达成率并改善M2M、R@3、FID，但仅用其训练的模型仍忽略多数单词编辑；配对损失将模板式编辑的遵循率从11%提升至60%，而标准指标不再进一步提升。使用身体部位字幕时，MinPair达到的目标数超过训练数据多达99倍的SOTA方法。

Abstract: Text-to-motion generation has made great progress in capturing what action to perform, yet it remains limited in controlling how that action is executed. For example, when only the height word of a kick request changes, none of the tested state-of-the-art methods reaches all three of knee, waist and head height. The other words of a caption usually imply an attribute such as height, so neither the training loss nor the standard metrics require a model to use the word that states it. A model has to use this word only when it alone separates two otherwise identical captions. We introduce MinPair, which creates such caption pairs from real motion clips. It starts from rule-based motion descriptors that measure the 3D body motion of each clip. From these descriptors a language model writes MinPair captions, timed body-part captions that say how each body part moves. The same descriptors show in which attributes two clips of the same action differ. A measured minimal pair joins the true caption of one clip with an altered copy in which only one attribute takes the value measured in the other clip. A contrastive pair loss then requires a diffusion denoiser to reconstruct the clip more accurately from its true caption than from the altered copy. Training needs no synthetic motion and inference stays unchanged. Control is tested on 22 target tasks and on caption edits that each change one attribute. MinPair captions alone help motions reach their targets and improve the standard metrics M2M, R@3 and FID, yet a model trained on them still ignores most single-word edits. The pair loss raises the share of followed template edits from 11% to 60%, while the standard metrics do not improve further. With body-part captions, MinPair reaches more targets than state-of-the-art methods that were trained on up to 99 times more motion data.

</details>


### [185] [UnifiedAD: One-Shot Anomaly Detection across Modalities via Evidence Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32551)
*Shangze Li,Chuancheng Shi,Simiao Xie,Wenhua Wu,Xingyu Zhu,Fei Shen,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 UnifiedAD，用异常证据校准统一单模态与多模态异常检测，仅需每类一个正常参考且无需梯度更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 单模态与多模态异常检测的目标是识别偏离正常模式的样本，二者共享统一检测基础；本文任务是构建统一框架，输入为正常参考与查询特征，输出图像级异常判定与像素级定位结果。

Motivation: 现有方法依赖模态特定的建模与评分方式，在正常参考样本有限时难以获得可比较的异常证据，从而阻碍跨层、跨模态的证据聚合。

Method: 先在独立辅助数据集上由正常/异常样本得到神经元级判别先验，并结合目标参考在保常态变换下的神经元响应一致性，筛选可靠"异常神经元"并确定异常方向；再基于所选神经元对参考的空间响应，为每层、每模态构建低秩正常子空间，以投影残差量化查询特征对正常结构的偏离；最后利用正常参考及其变换的响应分布分别校准投影残差与异常方向响应，转成可比较的异常证据，实现跨层跨模态聚合。

实验效果: 摘要仅报告在多个异常检测基准上 UnifiedAD 在图像级检测与像素级定位上均取得强性能，未给出具体基准名称、对比方法与数值。

Abstract: Both single-modal and multi-modal anomaly detection aim to identify deviations from normal patterns, providing a common foundation for unified detection. However, existing methods rely on modality-specific modeling and scoring, making it difficult to obtain comparable anomaly evidence with limited normal references. To that end, we propose UnifiedAD, which unifies single-modal and multi-modal anomaly detection through anomaly evidence calibration, requiring only one normal reference per target category and no gradient updates. Specifically, we first derive neuron-level discriminative priors from normal and anomalous samples in an independent auxiliary dataset and combine them with neuron response consistency under normality-preserving transformations of the target reference to select reliable neurons, termed anomaly neurons, and identify anomaly directions. Next, we construct a low-rank normal subspace for each layer and modality from the selected neurons' spatial responses to the reference and quantify query feature deviations from normal structure using projection residuals. Finally, we separately calibrate projection residuals and anomaly-direction responses using the response distributions obtained from the normal reference and its transformations, converting them into comparable anomaly evidence for aggregation across layers and modalities. Experiments on multiple anomaly detection benchmarks demonstrate that UnifiedAD achieves strong performance in both image-level anomaly detection and pixel-level anomaly localization.

</details>


### [186] [PartAgent: Agentic 3D Part Segmentation via Expert Integration and Closed-Loop Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32554)
*Yang Cao,Zhe Zhu,Yiheng Zhang,Zhengqiang Zhang,Haoyuan Li,Le Wan,Dan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PartAgent 是一个免训练的智能体框架，通过整合 3D 与 2D 专家能力并闭环迭代优化，实现语义一致的 3D 部件分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是将 3D 物体分割为语义有意义的部件，需要同时推理几何结构与语义上下文；输入为 3D 物体及其多视图信息，输出为部件分割结果，目标是兼顾结构基础的区域与语义判别性。

Motivation: 现有能力分散：预训练 3D 专家只提供空间上可靠但缺乏语义的区域提案，2D 专家提供语义线索但缺乏空间定位，如何将二者互补整合并支持渐进式修正尚缺统一框架。

Method: 提出结构化-语义专家整合模块：通过图像到表面的对应关系，将 3D 专家预测区域与 2D 专家的多视图语义证据结合，构建多粒度组合式部件划分；并提出观测驱动的闭环划分优化模块，基于当前划分及其支撑证据选择检查、证据修复、组合或重选等动作，验证变更并利用更新状态与动作结果指导后续决策。

实验效果: 在 PartObjaverse-Tiny-WT 上达到 68.41 mIoU，超过最强对比方法 8.53 点；在 3DCoMPaT200、PartNeXt、PartNetE、PartObjaverse-Tiny 四个数据集上均取得最高 mIoU，平均 69.36，比对比方法中的最高均值高 7.28 点。

Abstract: Segmenting a 3D object into semantically meaningful parts requires joint reasoning about geometric structure and semantic context. Pretrained 3D experts provide spatially grounded region proposals, while 2D experts provide semantic cues for identifying object parts. The challenge is to integrate these complementary capabilities into a framework that can both produce meaningful part segmentations and progressively refine them. We present PartAgent, a training-free agentic framework for 3D part segmentation built upon two complementary modules: (i) Structural-Semantic Expert Integration combines regions predicted by the 3D expert with multiview semantic evidence from the 2D expert through image-to-surface correspondences, constructing compositional part partitions at multiple granularities. (ii) Observation-Driven Closed-Loop Partition Refinement observes the current partition and its supporting evidence to select targeted inspection, evidence repair, composition, or reselection actions. It verifies proposed changes and uses the updated state and action outcomes to guide subsequent decisions. On PartObjaverse-Tiny-WT, PartAgent achieves 68.41 mIoU, outperforming the strongest competing method by 8.53 points. Across 3DCoMPaT200, PartNeXt, PartNetE, and PartObjaverse-Tiny, PartAgent achieves the highest mIoU on each dataset, averaging 69.36 across the four datasets, 7.28 points above the highest mean among competing methods.

</details>


### [187] [Sol$^{3}$: Accelerating Video Diffusion for Free via Hierarchical Sparse Attention Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32562)
*Guoxin Fan,Weilun Feng,Chuanguang Yang,Mingqiang Wu,Xiaokun Liu,Jitao Lu,Zhulin An,Libo Huang,Boyu Diao,Di Gao,Yongjun Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Sol$^{3}$ 是一种免训练的分层稀疏注意力方法，通过高斯引导的块到令牌逐级路由，进一步细化精确计算的分配，从而加速扩散视频模型推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 面向扩散视频模型（含视频生成、音视频联合生成与世界模拟任务）的免训练推理加速，输入为注意力计算过程，目标是降低注意力开销同时保持生成质量。

Motivation: 现有 Sol-Attn 结合稀疏精确计算与低成本代表项近似，但块内注意力往往只集中于少数令牌；在精确保留这些关键交互的同时，块级决策仍要评估大量低响应成员，造成冗余精确计算开销。

Method: 提出分层稀疏注意力：1）高斯引导的分层路由，利用各层级统计阈值从块到令牌逐步确定精确计算范围，仅对 Mixed 块中需细化的成员子集做精确计算，其余由代表项近似；2）异构路由统计，将块中心相关性与块内 Key/Value 变化结合以识别难以近似的 Mixed 块，并在其中统一比较原始令牌相关性来选取需精确计算的成员。

实验效果: 在视频生成、音视频联合生成与世界模拟任务上达到 86% 注意力稀疏度与 3.07× 注意力加速，同时保持生成质量。

Abstract: Sol-Attn (Sparsifying online attention) combines sparse exact computation with low-cost representative approximation to accelerate training-free inference in diffusion video models. However, intra-block attention can focus on only a few tokens. While preserving these key interactions exactly, the block-level decision incurs the cost of evaluating many low-response members. We propose Sol$^{3}$, a training-free hierarchical sparse attention method that further refines how exact computations are allocated. First, we introduce Gaussian-guided hierarchical routing, which determines the scope of exact computation step by step, from blocks to tokens, using statistical thresholds at each level. Only a subset of members within Mixed blocks that require refinement is selected for exact computation, while representative terms approximate the remaining members. We further design heterogeneous routing statistics that combine block-center correlations with variations in Key and Value within blocks to identify blocks that are difficult to approximate. Among these Mixed blocks, we uniformly compare raw token relevance to select members requiring exact computation. Experiments on video generation, audio-video co-generation, and world simulation tasks demonstrate that Sol$^{3}$ achieves 86% attention sparsity and delivers a $3.07\times$ attention speedup while preserving quality. Our code is available at https://github.com/FofGofx/sol3.

</details>


### [188] [CoReQ: Condition-Aware Cross-Modal Repair and Quality-Guided Robust Collaborative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32588)
*Ziwei Guo,Lingzhi Li,Jin Wang,Yunjiang Xu,Yupeng Ouyang,Benyuan Yang,Yujun Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CoReQ框架，通过条件感知的双向跨模态修复与质量引导的多车加权融合，提升恶劣天气下LiDAR-4D Radar协同感知性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 多车协同感知利用不同视角观测缓解遮挡与感知范围受限问题，输入为各车LiDAR与4D Radar点云特征，输出为协同检测结果，目标是在雨雪等退化观测条件下保持可靠检测。

Motivation: 恶劣天气导致的缺失回波与虚假回波会降低单车特征质量；现有工作未解决如何依据当前观测条件自适应调整跨模态修复，以及修复后可靠性仍不一致的多车特征如何合理融合的问题。

Method: CBCR模块从点云占用率、密度与特征统计中提取车辆级观测条件，据此调节LiDAR与Radar特征的双向校正；TaQE利用EMA教师网络的分类与定位信息构建监督目标，预测修复后特征的空间质量；CoQF将质量信息与内容匹配，并结合双方观测条件进行多车特征加权融合。

实验效果: 在V2X-R与V2X-Radar-C上，多种观测条件下均具竞争力；V2X-R雪天条件下AP@0.7取得显著提升，并在所比较的V2X-Radar-C方法中取得最高AP。

Abstract: Multi-vehicle collaborative perception can use observations from different perspectives to alleviate the problems of occlusion and limited sensing range. However, missing echoes and false echoes caused by bad weather will reduce the quality of single-vehicle features and affect collaborative detection. LiDAR provides richer geometric information, and 4D Radar has strong weather adaptability. The complementarity of the two provides the possibility to improve perception under degraded observations. However, how to adjust cross-modal repair according to the current observation conditions and how to reasonably integrate multi-vehicle features whose reliability still differs after repair are still problems that need to be solved. To this end, we propose CoReQ, a condition-aware repair and quality-guided collaborative perception framework. For the former, the condition-guided bidirectional cross-modal repair module (CBCR) extracts vehicle-level observation conditions from point cloud occupancy, density and feature statistics, and adjusts the bidirectional correction of LiDAR and Radar features accordingly. For the latter, we combine local reliability assessment with collaborative weighting: first, the task-aware quality estimation module (TaQE) uses the classification and positioning information of the EMA teacher to construct a supervision target and predict the spatial quality of the repaired features; then, the condition and quality guided fusion module (CoQF) matches the quality information with the content, and the observation conditions of both parties are jointly used for multi-vehicle feature weighted fusion. Experiments on V2X-R and V2X-Radar-C show that the framework is competitive under a variety of observation conditions, achieving significant gains in AP@0.7 on V2X-R under snowy conditions, and achieving the highest AP among the compared V2X-Radar-C methods.

</details>


### [189] [FewRef-5: Fixed-Reference Test-Time Adaptation for Virtual Staining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32591)
*Wenxuan Wang,Liwen Wang,Mingmin Wu,Xingbo Dong,Zhe Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FewRef-5 是一种基于 StainPromptInversion 的参考辅助测试时自适应方法，利用 5 张真实 MAS 训练块的固定染色目标来校正虚拟染色输出，在提升目标集 FID 与源参考 SSIM 的同时牺牲了部分源 PSNR 与 MS-SSIM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 虚拟染色从常规 H&E 数字切片合成特殊染色图像，以扩展既有切片所承载的信息。该任务以 H&E 图像为输入、以目标特殊染色（此处为 MAS）图像为输出，目标是在生成器不重训练的前提下提升查询特异性的染色外观还原质量。

Motivation: 固定推理规则使得部署时真实目标染色参考无法被利用，从而限制了针对单个查询的染色外观校正能力。

Method: 在 StainPromptInversion 上构建参考辅助 TTA：从 5 张真实 MAS 训练块中提取组织掩膜下的光密度均值与标准差并平均为固定染色目标；通过按计划引入的统计匹配损失，将该目标与边缘/Gram 一致性惩罚及继承的重建目标一并纳入逐查询视觉提示优化；目标在查询间保持不变，避免对先前生成输出的递归依赖，预训练生成器保持冻结。

实验效果: 在 1,989 个查询的 H&E 到 MAS 记录输出上，相较 Edge+Gram 变体，目标集 FID 由 183.201 降至 166.904，源参考 SSIM 由 0.92787 升至 0.94996；但源 PSNR 与 MS-SSIM 下降，表明存在端点权衡而非全面更优的重建。

Abstract: Virtual staining synthesizes special-stain images from routine hematoxylin and eosin (H&E), extending the information available from an existing digital slide. However, a fixed inference rule can leave available real target-stain references unused, limiting query-specific correction of stain appearance at deployment. Test-time adaptation (TTA) provides a mechanism for this reference-guided, query-specific refinement without retraining the generator. We therefore propose FewRef-5, a reference-assisted TTA method for virtual staining built on StainPromptInversion. We extract tissue-masked optical-density means and standard deviations from five real Masson's trichrome (MAS) training patches and average them into a fixed stain target. A scheduled statistic-matching loss introduces this target into per-query visual-prompt optimization alongside our edge/Gram consistency penalties and the inherited reconstruction objectives. The target remains unchanged across queries, avoiding recursive dependence on earlier generated outputs while the pretrained generator stays fixed. On recorded H&E-to-MAS outputs for 1,989 queries, FewRef-5 reduces target-set FID from 183.201 to 166.904 and increases source-referenced SSIM from 0.92787 to 0.94996 relative to our Edge+Gram variant. Source PSNR and MS-SSIM decrease, showing an endpoint trade-off rather than uniformly better reconstruction.

</details>


### [190] [VenusOCR2.0: Towards Efficient and Effective Document Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32617)
*Lei Li,Zheng Wei,Yi Yuan,Qingbin Liu,Jiang Bian,Zang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VenusOCR2.0 是一个 1B 参数的紧凑视觉语言识别器，通过有界递归自我改进数据引擎与解耦并行草稿模块 PTPv2，实现高精度、高吞吐的文档解析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是面向文本、表格与公式的高精度、高吞吐文档解析，输入为文档图像、输出为结构化识别结果，目标是在紧凑模型规模下兼顾识别质量与推理效率。

Motivation: 现有大规模通用 VLM 在文档解析精度上仍有不足，而自训练易发生自我改进崩塌，同时自回归解码构成吞吐瓶颈，亟需可靠监督来源与无损加速方案。

Method: 构建有界递归自我改进数据引擎，模型仅作弱点探针，由多专家 medoid 共识、VLM 视觉校验与因子化合成提供监督，贯穿预训练、精选 SFT 与 RLVR；架构上采用动态分辨率 SigLIP2 编码器、2x2 空间 token 合并与 Qwen3-0.6B 解码器，并用紧凑原子 OTSL token 缩短表格序列；引入约 67M 的 PTPv2 解耦并行草稿模块，结合冻结多层验证器条件、独立学习掩码与块因果全位置下一 token 预测以消除自回归瓶颈。

实验效果: 在 OmniDocBenchv1.6 上总体得分 96.45、公式 CDM 达 SOTA 的 98.57，优于 Gemini-3-Pro（92.91）与 Qwen3-VL-235B（89.78）；单张 H20 上经 vLLM 部署，PTPv2 带来 2.96 倍单请求加速与最高 2.36 倍批量吞吐提升，并保证与标准自回归解码逐 token 无损等价。

Abstract: We present VenusOCR2.0, a compact 1B-parameter vision-language recognizer designed for both high-accuracy and high-throughput document parsing across text, tables, and formulas. To continuously improve recognition quality without self-training collapse, we build a bounded recursive self-improvement (RSI) data engine where the model acts strictly as a weakness probe, while external multi-expert medoid consensus, VLM visual verification, and factorized synthesis supply reliable supervision across pretraining, curated SFT, and RLVR. VenusOCR2.0 couples a dynamic-resolution SigLIP2 encoder and 2x2 spatial token merging with a Qwen3-0.6B decoder, adopting compact atomic OTSL tokens to shorten table sequences and reduce structural errors. To further eliminate the autoregressive decoding bottleneck, we introduce PTPv2, a lightweight ($\sim$67M) decoupled parallel draft module that combines frozen multi-layer verifier conditioning, a standalone learned mask, and block-causal all-position next-token prediction. On OmniDocBenchv1.6, VenusOCR2.0 achieves an overall score of 96.45 and a state-of-the-art 98.57 formula CDM, substantially outperforming massive general-purpose VLMs such as Gemini-3-Pro (92.91) and Qwen3-VL-235B (89.78). Deployed in vLLM on a single H20 GPU, PTPv2 delivers a 2.96x single-request speedup and up to 2.36x higher batched throughput while guaranteeing lossless, token-for-token equivalence with standard autoregressive decoding.

</details>


### [191] [ShapeQuest: Towards Granularity-Controlled Edit-Conditioned 3D Asset Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32621)
*Faris Abu-Ali,Sankalp Sinha,Didier Stricker,Muhammad Zeshan Afzal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 ShapeQuest 基准与 ShapeQuester 模型，用于指令条件下的三维资产检索（编辑定位任务）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 给定一个参考资产与类似"移除一个扶手"的指令，检索器需在库中找出完成该编辑且保留其余部分的变体。该任务在图像领域已较成熟，输入为参考资产+编辑指令，输出为匹配的编辑后三维资产，目标是实现编辑感知的检索。

Motivation: 编辑条件检索在图像中已有成熟设定，但针对单个三维资产的研究明显不足，缺乏对应基准与专门方法，因此需要构建带竞争性编辑变体的数据与模型。

Method: 构建 ShapeQuest 开源流水线，对带纹理网格做部件增删、材质与颜色修改，按受影响表面积比例划分五级编辑粒度，并在指令未指定部件时生成多个有效目标；用视觉语言模型从暴露编辑部位视角判别，并由人工审核。ShapeQuester 在编辑点云重建的辅助任务下联合学习检索，用部件掩码监督被改与保留区域，可配合冻结的预训练点编码器。

实验效果: ShapeQuest 含 50 类共 87,824 条查询与 50,919 个形状的候选库；冻结编码器下 ShapeQuester 测试集 operation-macro mAP@10 达 50.4，高于同输入融合基线 47.2 与最强基线 48.6；从头训练编码器时重建辅助在验证集带来 6.5 点提升，其中 3.3 点来自部件掩码。

Abstract: Given a reference asset and an instruction such as "remove one armrest," a retriever must find variants that make the edit and preserve the other parts. This search is well established for images but remains underexplored for individual 3D assets. To study this problem, we introduce ShapeQuest and ShapeQuester, a new benchmark and model for edit-conditioned 3D asset retrieval. ShapeQuest's open-source pipeline edits textured meshes through part additions, removals, and material and colour changes. It controls edit granularity across five levels, defined by the fraction of the object's surface area each edit affects. It constructs multiple valid targets when an instruction leaves the choice of part open. A vision-language model judges each edit from views chosen to expose the edited parts, and human raters audit its judgments. ShapeQuest has 87,824 queries in 50 categories and a 50,919-shape gallery with competing edits. To distinguish these variants, ShapeQuester learns retrieval alongside reconstruction of the edited point cloud, with part masks supervising changed and preserved regions. With a frozen pretrained point encoder, ShapeQuester reaches 50.4 operation-macro mAP@10 on the test split, against 47.2 for the fusion baseline with the same inputs and 48.6 for the strongest baseline. Reconstruction adds 6.5 points on the validation split when the point encoder is trained from scratch, including 3.3 from part masks.

</details>


### [192] [On the Capabilities and Limitations of Zero-Shot Open-Vocabulary Cloud Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32625)
*Jose Sosa,Thomas Gillet,Rim Sleimi,Albert Abello*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在零样本设置下评测十二种开放词汇语义分割（OVSS）方法，在六个公开遥感数据集上考察二分类云检测与多类别云分割的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是对地观测（EO）影像的云检测与云分割：输入为遥感影像与文本提示，输出为云/非云二分类标签或厚云、薄云、云阴影、背景等多类别掩膜，目标是评估视觉-语言基础模型在无需任务特定训练时的可泛化分割能力。

Motivation: 尽管视觉-语言基础模型已推动无需任务特定训练的开放词汇语义分割发展，但其在云检测上的能力仍缺乏系统探索，尤其缺少跨模型、跨数据集的零样本对比基准，以及对文本提示敏感性的分析。

Method: 构建零样本评测框架：不进行微调或目标域适配，在六个公开EO数据集上对十二种OVSS方法统一评测，同时考察二分类云检测与厚云/薄云/云阴影/背景的多类别分割；并设计受控的提示敏感性分析，用不同文本描述（含外观导向提示）检验性能变化。

实验效果: SegEarth-OV3 在六个数据集中的四个上取得最佳二分类云检测性能；多类别分割明显更难，尤其是细粒度云类别区分；提示敏感性分析显示模型对文本描述的响应具有依赖性——外观导向提示对部分方法有益、对另一些则有损。

Abstract: Recent advances in vision-language foundation models have enabled open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS) without task-specific training, offering new opportunities for Earth Observation (EO) applications. However, their capabilities for cloud detection remain insufficiently explored. In this work, we evaluate twelve OVSS approaches across six publicly available EO datasets in a zero-shot setting, i.e., without fine-tuning or adaptation to the target data. We investigate both binary cloud detection and multiclass segmentation, distinguishing between thick clouds, thin clouds, cloud shadows, and background. Our results show differences in performance across models and datasets, with SegEarth-OV3 achieving the highest binary cloud detection performance on four of the six datasets. While several approaches demonstrate promising performance in binary cloud detection, multiclass segmentation remains more challenging, particularly when distinguishing between fine-grained cloud categories. Furthermore, our controlled prompt sensitivity analysis reveals model-dependent responses to alternative textual descriptions, with appearance-oriented prompts benefiting certain approaches while degrading the performance of others. Overall, our findings demonstrate both the potential and current limitations of zero-shot OVSS for cloud detection, establishing a comparative benchmark for future research on generalisable cloud segmentation. Code will be available at: https://github.com/josesosajs/zero-shot-cloudovss.

</details>


### [193] [Completion-Aware Compression for Progressive 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32628)
*Reagan Koo,Yeong-Gyu Kim,Eun-Seok Ryu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种渐进式3DGS编解码器，用单一码流在任意字节预算下提供可解码的部分重建，并以"完成感知"的属性分配提升率失真性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）可高质量渲染新视角，但场景表示体积庞大、传输代价高。本文任务是构建一个渐进式3DGS码流：输入为原始3DGS表示，输出为支持部分3D重建并按字节到达逐步增加高斯的单一压缩码流。

Motivation: 剪枝与压缩虽能减小文件体积，但渐进传输要求在场景全部到达前即可用；现有渐进表示虽能给出部分场景，其分层更新受预定义的编码边界限制，缺乏在任意接收字节预算下的细粒度可解码性。

Method: 提出单一紧凑码流，将新解码的高斯随字节增加逐步并入重建，支持任意接收字节预算下的细粒度更新，且无需额外独立编码或后续属性精修；核心是"完成感知分配"，在属性保真度与高斯提前可用的图像收益之间做权衡分配。

实验效果: 在完整文件大小与接收字节匹配条件下，完成感知分配相较仅属性分配将1/8 PSNR提升0.48–1.43 dB；编解码器将原始3DGS压缩62–88倍并保持接近参考的Full PSNR；在评估的50 Mbps传输模型下，约0.2 s即可获得部分重建。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables high-quality novel-view rendering, but its large scene representations are costly to transmit. Pruning and compression reduce file sizes, yet progressive delivery also requires usable reconstructions before the full scene arrives. Existing progressive representations provide partial scenes, but level-based updates are limited by predefined coding boundaries. This paper presents a progressive 3DGS codec that balances attribute fidelity with the image benefit of making Gaussians available earlier. Its single compact stream supports partial 3D reconstruction and adds newly decoded Gaussians as more bytes arrive. The stream supports fine-grained updates at arbitrary received-byte budgets, without separate encodings or later attribute refinement. At matched complete-file sizes and received bytes, completion-aware allocation improves 1/8 PSNR over attribute-only allocation by 0.48-1.43 dB. The proposed codec compresses original 3DGS representations by 62-88 times while retaining near-reference Full PSNR. Under the evaluated 50-Mbps delivery model, partial reconstruction becomes available in approximately 0.2 s.

</details>


### [194] [HazeFlux: Few-Step Generative Priors for Real-World Image Dehazing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32632)
*Yunong Liu,Danna Xue,Javier Vazquez-Corral*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出两阶段框架，将少步流匹配生成先验适配到真实单图去雾任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是真实世界单图去雾：输入一张含雾图像，输出清晰的去雾图像。由于成对真实数据稀缺，实际场景中存在严重域偏移，目标是让模型在真实雾霾条件下也能泛化良好。

Motivation: 现有工作依赖合成雾数据训练，在真实雾霾上泛化差；虽有预训练生成编辑模型可提供强先验以弥合该差距，但直接迁移存在两个问题：标准生成模型偏向分布对齐而牺牲像素级保真度，且在压缩潜空间中操作会丢失细粒度结构细节。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1) 用LoRA微调潜空间少步流匹配骨干，除流匹配目标外额外施加像素级监督，实现直接的雾图到清晰图转换；(2) 在VAE解码器中引入结构保持的跳跃连接，绕开潜空间压缩伪影以恢复精确空间边缘。

实验效果: 在四个真实世界基准上，该方法在真实条件下的去雾性能显著优于现有去雾方法（摘要未给出具体数值指标）。

Abstract: Real-world single-image dehazing suffers from severe domain shifts, as paired real data is scarce and models trained on synthetic haze generalize poorly to real-world haze. While pre-trained generative editing models offer powerful image priors to bridge this generalization gap, applying them to image dehazing poses two major challenges: standard generative models prioritize distribution alignment over strict pixel-level fidelity, and operating in compressed latent spaces inherently discards fine-grained structural details. In this paper, we propose a novel two-stage framework that adapts the generative prior of a few-step flow-matching backbone to real-world dehazing. In the first stage, we fine-tune the latent backbone using Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), supervised not only on a flow-matching objective, but also on pixel-level supervision, thus enabling direct hazy-to-clear translation. In the second stage, we introduce structure-preserving skip connections directly to the VAE decoder, bypassing latent compression artifacts to recover precise spatial edges. Experiments on four realworld benchmarks demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms existing dehazing methods in real conditions.

</details>


### [195] [ICLWorld: Benchmarking In-Context Learning of Agents from Digital to Physical Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32649)
*Qingle Liu,Jieming Li,Pei-lin Li,Shenghan Zheng,Ruize Yang,Sunqi Fan,Deyao Hong,Huan-ang Gao,Meng-Hao Guo,Shuojin Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出在线基准 ICLWorld 与统一执行框架 UIverse，从数字世界到物理世界系统评测前沿智能体的上下文学习（ICL）能力，揭示电脑使用与机器人使用之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是评测智能体在推理阶段从上下文习得新规则并适配用户特定需求的能力。作者构建覆盖电脑使用与机器人使用的在线基准，输入为文本或视频形式的规则演示，输出为智能体在 160 个任务上的成功率及三级泛化表现。

Motivation: 现有基准多为离线、依赖文本或静态图像、存在训练数据污染风险，且机器人评测仅限于方法层面的消融实验，缺少对前沿智能体 ICL 的统一、在线、跨数字与物理世界的系统评估。

Method: 构建在线基准 ICLWorld 与统一执行框架 UIverse，将每个环境封装为用户界面；建立 30 个环境、630 段演示视频（10 小时以上）、160 个带定制且常反直觉规则的任务，每任务提供三个规则版本（文本或视频），并在重放演示、新实例、未见环境三个层级测试泛化。

实验效果: 六个前沿模型中，Claude Opus 5.5 与 GPT-6 Astra 在电脑使用任务上成功率为 82% 和 76%，机器人使用仅 27% 和 26%，无规则对照成功率降至最多 8%；GPT-6 Astra 在全部三个规则版本下仅完成 38% 的电脑使用任务（为单一规则时的一半），机器人规则问答正确率 98% 但任务完成率仅 20%，显示数字与物理世界、以及识别规则与执行规则之间的双重差距。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) lets agents acquire new rules from context and adapt to user-specific requirements at inference time. The same frontier agents now operate both computers and robots, yet their ICL remains insufficiently assessed: existing benchmarks are largely offline, rely on text or static images, and risk training-data contamination, while robot evaluations are limited to method-level ablations. We present ICLWorld, an online benchmark that evaluates the ICL of agents from digital to physical worlds, covering computer use and robot use, together with UIverse, a unified harness that turns every environment into a user interface. We build 30 environments, record 630 demonstration videos (10+ hours), and design 160 tasks with customized, often counter-intuitive rules that cannot be satisfied without learning from the context. Each task comes with three rule versions, given as text or video, and we test generalization at three levels, from replaying the demonstration to new instances and unseen environments. Across six frontier models, the strongest agents, Claude Opus 5.5 and GPT-6 Astra, succeed on 82% and 76% of computer-use runs but only 27% and 26% of robot-use runs, and a no-rule control reduces success to at most 8%. GPT-6 Astra succeeds under all three rule versions on only 38% of computer-use tasks, half its rate under a single rule, and answers 98% of robot rule questions correctly yet completes only 20% of these tasks. Frontier agents learn rules comparably well from video and from text and transfer them across environments, whereas weaker agents rely on text and lose most of their video-based success once the demonstrated situation is resampled. These findings reveal substantial gaps between the digital and the physical world and between identifying a rule and acting on it.

</details>


### [196] [LaWanle: Why Can't Leading Driving Models Make a Simple Turn?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32650)
*Chengzhi Gao,Junzhe Gao,Yongzhi Liu,Weichao Zhuang,Weilong Hu,Taonian Liang,Guodong Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 端到端自动驾驶模型在NAVSIM上接近人类水平，却在真实感闭环仿真器中无法可靠完成简单的90度转弯；作者构建LaWanle基准定位失效原因，并用闭环DAgger微调小部分参数将转弯通过率从19%提升到76%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 论文考察端到端驾驶策略在真实感闭环仿真器中执行90度转弯（含多个起点、驶入交叉口）的可靠性，输入为闭环仿真中的驾驶场景，输出为模型在该转弯上的通过/失败表现及改进后的横向路径策略，目标是诊断并修复此类失效。

Motivation: 现有端到端模型在NAVSIM等指标上已接近人类驾驶员，因此其性能被认为基本达标；但作者发现这些模型在闭环真实感仿真中连简单转弯都无法稳定完成，说明现有评测掩盖了实际闭环鲁棒性缺陷，需要专门基准来揭示失效机制。

Method: 构建LaWanle基准，从三个闭环仿真器中采集转弯场景，每段转弯设置多个起点以检验同一模型的表现差异；通过扰动起点与交叉口入口处的航向角做敏感性分析，并用轻微航向修正验证可救回的比例；随后在横向路径上对开源车道保持模型进行闭环DAgger后训练（仅训练5%参数），并进一步在恢复数据上微调。

实验效果: 同一模型对同一转弯的通过与否取决于起点，且两次行驶在进入交叉口前几乎一致；入口处仅旋转数度即可使原本通过的转弯失败，而轻微航向修正可救回约一半失败样例；闭环DAgger仅训练5%参数即使转弯通过率由19%升至76%，增益可迁移到另外两个基准并跻身领先模型，但基于恢复数据的额外微调在三个基准上均未优于等时长训练的对照组，且所有变体仍远低于人类驾驶员。

Abstract: We were surprised to find that leading end-to-end driving models across major paradigms, despite scoring close to human drivers on NAVSIM, cannot reliably make a simple 90-degree turn in photorealistic closed-loop simulators. To investigate why, we build LaWanle, a benchmark of turns drawn from three such simulators. Driving each turn from several starts, we find that the same model passes it from one start and fails it from another, and that the two drives look alike until they enter the junction. Rotating the car by a few degrees at the junction entry can fail a turn it otherwise passes. Conversely, a slight heading correction to the plans can rescue about half of the failed turns we tested. Such errors arise when a model drives by itself, so we post-train an open-source lane-keeping model with closed-loop DAgger on its lateral path. Training 5% of its parameters is enough to raise its turn pass rate from 19% to 76%. The gain transfers to the two other benchmarks, where the policy also scores among the leading models. Further fine-tuning on recovery data repairs the deviations it is trained on but, against a control trained equally long, improves turns on none of the three benchmarks, and every variant stays far below a human driver.

</details>


### [197] [Attack-Inference Aligned Universal Adversarial Attacks on Video Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32658)
*Ruiqi Shen,Chang Liu,Henghui Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AUV 提出一种对齐 VOS 两阶段推理范式的通用对抗扰动框架，通过破坏共享记忆空间实现对多种提示模态的即时攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 视频目标分割（VOS）依赖"目标初始化 + 基于记忆的传播"两阶段推理范式，本论文的输入为视频及目标提示，输出为对抗扰动，目标是在该范式下构造高效、持久的攻击。

Motivation: 现有针对 VOS 的对抗攻击忽视了两阶段结构，未针对初始化与传播分别设计，因而难以充分利用共享记忆空间这一关键脆弱环节，也未实现跨提示模态的通用攻击。

Method: AUV 优化通用对抗扰动（UAP），采用分别适配初始化阶段与传播阶段的解耦训练策略；通过扰乱两阶段共享的记忆空间，实现与提示无关（prompt-agnostic）的攻击，兼容掩码、点、框与语言等多种模态提示。

实验效果: 在 DAVIS17-val 上，AUV 将基于掩码的 SAM3 的 J&F 降至 8.4（相同训练设置下此前 SOTA 为 55.4）；同时表现出最快起效、最持久的时间动态特性，并支持视频中任意时刻的攻击。

Abstract: Modern video object segmentation (VOS) relies on a two-stage inference paradigm: target initialization and memory-based propagation. However, existing adversarial attacks on VOS often overlook this structure. To bridge this gap, we propose AUV, an adversarial framework that Aligns with the Universal two-stage paradigm of VOS models. Specifically, AUV optimizes a universal adversarial perturbation (UAP) through decoupled training strategies tailored for both initialization and propagation stages. By disrupting the shared memory space, it achieves prompt-agnostic attacks across different modalities, including masks, points, boxes, and language. Extensive experiments show that AUV establishes state-of-the-art VOS attack performance, such as degrading mask-based SAM3 to only 8.4 J&F on DAVIS17-val (compared to 55.4 for the previous SOTA under the same training setup). It also exhibits superior temporal dynamics with the fastest onset, the most enduring persistence, and the support of any-time mid-video attacks. The AUV optimization framework is applicable to different VOS models, revealing vulnerabilities in real-world streaming VOS. Code is available at https://github.com/FudanCVL/AUV.

</details>


### [198] [Text Is Just Another Shot in Vision-Language Models: Simple Probes on Frozen Encoders Do It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32672)
*Reda Bensaid,Ismail Ben Ayed,François Leduc-Primeau,Vincent Gripon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出TASP：在冻结的视觉语言特征上做带文本��验与正则的凸逻辑回归，并指出少样本适配的进展主要来自表征质量与文本权重的合理使用，而非复杂适配机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 本文研究视觉语言模型（基于CLIP类模型）的少样本适配：输入为少量有标注图像与类名，输出为图像分类结果，目标是在冻结编码器条件下高效提升下游分类精度。

Motivation: 现有方法普遍遵循"零样本文本分类器为锚点、标注图像仅作修正"的共同原则，并不断叠加复杂度；作者认为这一原则反映的是CLIP视觉特征的性质而非任务本身的性质，因而需要重新审视适配机制的必要性。

Method: 给出一个贝叶斯解释：类名等价于 κ=σ²/τ² 张标注图像（类内视觉离散度与类名错配度之比），该模型的贝叶斯分类器相当于把文本计为 κ 个额外样本。据此提出TASP——在冻结特征上的简单凸逻辑回归，将类名嵌入同时作为加权训练样本与正则先验，从而把少样本适配化归为良正则化的凸探针学习。

实验效果: 在标准11数据集基准上，TASP配冻结PE-Core-L特征达到1 shot 87.1%、16 shots 91.4%，领先现有少样本视觉语言适配方法4.6至11.0个百分点；匹配特征下的对照实验表明现有冻结编码器适配方法并未实质优于该探针，且文本权重比其融入方式更重要，自监督视觉特征与视觉语言表征结合对较弱编码器收益最大。

Abstract: Few-shot adaptation of vision-language models has produced increasingly elaborate methods built on CLIP and on one shared principle: the zero-shot text classifier is the anchor, and labeled images only correct it. We argue that this principle reflects CLIP's visual features rather than a property of the task. We provide a simple Bayesian interpretation, in which a class name is worth exactly kappa = sigma^2 / tau^2 labeled images, the ratio between the visual spread of a class and the misalignment of its name. The Bayes classifier of this model counts text as kappa more samples. With modern frozen encoders, few-shot adaptation then reduces to a well-regularised convex probe. Specifically, we introduce TASP, a simple convex logistic regression on frozen features that incorporates class-name embeddings as weighted training examples and as a regularization prior. On the standard 11-dataset benchmark, TASP with frozen PE-Core-L features achieves 87.1% accuracy at 1 shot and 91.4% at 16 shots, outperforming state-of-the-art few-shot vision-language adaptation methods by 4.6 to 11.0 percentage points. While stronger representations explain much of this improvement, controlled comparisons on matched features show that existing frozen-encoder adaptation methods do not materially improve on this probe. We further find that the weight assigned to text matters more than how it is incorporated, and that combining self-supervised visual features with vision-language representations is most beneficial for weaker visual encoders. Together, these findings suggest that progress in few-shot vision-language adaptation depends more on representation quality and the appropriate use of text supervision than on increasingly complex adaptation mechanisms.

</details>


### [199] [TokenMatte: Unlocking T2V Diffusion Priors for Pixel-Native Video Matting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32675)
*Zanyi Wang,Dengyang Jiang,Fengfei Yu,Shilong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TokenMatte 将预训练视频 DiT 改造为单次前向、无需辅助输入的像素原生 RGB 到 alpha 预测器，直接在像素空间监督并以 token 级线性读出实现全分辨率抠像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 高分辨率视频抠像需从视频序列恢复连续的 alpha 值，准确还原细发丝、软边界与运动模糊，但稠密 alpha 监督数据稀缺。目标是在仅有公开抠像数据集的条件下，端到端输出全分辨率 alpha 序列。

Motivation: 现有生成式视频抠像方法多经由 RGB 预训练的 VAE 潜空间输出 alpha，会削弱细节并带来额外解码开销；同时稠密 alpha 监督稀缺，限制了精度。因此需要消除目标侧 alpha 编码与输出侧 VAE 解码，并充分利用预训练时空先验。

Method: 以预训练视频 DiT 为骨干，采用单一共享线性读出，将每个时空 token 直接映射为其对应的 t×p×p alpha 像素块，在同一局部视频块内同时建模空间细节与时间变化；平铺这些块即得全分辨率 alpha 序列，无需卷积解码器或可学习上采样。alpha 预测在像素空间直接监督，真值 alpha 不进入 VAE，从而省去目标编码与解码环节，且推理时不需要辅助输入或额外数据整理。

实验效果: 在与潜空间输出基线匹配的设置下，像素原生接口提升了抠像精度并带来 1.51× 推理加速；在 VideoMatte、YouTubeMatte 与 V-HIM60 上取得无辅助输入条件下的新最优结果。

Abstract: High-resolution video matting requires accurate recovery of continuous alpha values, including fine hair, soft boundaries, and motion blur, yet dense alpha supervision remains scarce. Pretrained text-to-video diffusion transformers (DiTs) offer rich spatio-temporal priors, whereas existing generative video matting approaches typically route alpha predictions through RGB-trained VAE latents, which can attenuate fine details and introduce additional decoding overhead. We present TokenMatte, which adapts a pretrained video DiT into a single-pass, auxiliary-free RGB-to-alpha predictor with a pixel-native output interface. At its core is a single shared linear readout that maps each spatio-temporal token directly to its corresponding ($t \times p \times p$) block of alpha pixels, predicting spatial details and temporal variations within the same local video block. Tiling these blocks produces the full-resolution alpha sequence without a convolutional decoder or learned upsampling. The predicted alpha is supervised directly in pixel space, while the ground-truth alpha never enters the VAE, eliminating target-side alpha encoding and output-side VAE decoding. Leveraging the pretrained video prior, TokenMatte is trained using only existing public matting datasets, without additional data curation or auxiliary inputs at inference. Compared with a matched latent-output baseline, our pixel-native interface improves matting accuracy while achieving a 1.51$\times$ inference speedup. TokenMatte establishes a new auxiliary-free state of the art on VideoMatte, YouTubeMatte, and V-HIM60.

</details>


### [200] [Are We Rewarding the Wrong Module? Module-Specific Policy Optimization for Multimodal Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32687)
*HuiSeong Cho,Jun Yeong Park,Yu Rang Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出模块特定策略优化（MSPO），将感知信号与结果信号分离到视觉编码器与投影器/LLM，缓解模块级信用纠缠，提升多模态推理表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是提升多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的多模态推理能力，沿用基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）与GRPO框架：输入为视觉与文本问题，输出为推理答案，目标是在数学与通用推理基准上获得更高正确率。

Motivation: 现有方法存在“模块级信用纠缠”：视觉编码器、投影器与LLM共用同一优势信号联合优化，即使感知正确性与最终答案正确性并不一致，也无法区分责任归属，导致感知模块无法获得针对性学习信号。

Method: MSPO按模块拆分学习信号。先用视觉信息辅助问题构成的感知清单（perception checklist）生成回答并获得感知准确率；对其阈值化得到感知符号决定奖励方向，再由潜变量感知准确率上的Beta分布给出后验置信度以缩放奖励幅度，构成置信感知奖励CaPR；随后对CaPR做组内相对归一化并用主任务成功率缩放，得到视觉优势用于更新视觉编码器，而投影器与LLM仍保留基于结果的GRPO更新。

实验效果: 在七个数学与通用推理基准上，MSPO在3B与7B规模下平均均优于现有RL调优模型；摘要未报告具体数值。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) based on Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has been widely adopted to enhance the multimodal reasoning of multimodal large language models (MLLMs). However, existing methods share a common limitation of Module-Level Credit Entanglement, in which the vision encoder, projector, and LLM modules are jointly optimized using the same advantage signals even when perceptual correctness and final-answer correctness differ. To address this limitation, we propose Module-Specific Policy Optimization (MSPO), a framework that separates perceptual and outcome-based learning signals across model modules. Specifically, MSPO uses responses to perception checklists comprising auxiliary questions about visual information to derive an advantage for training the vision encoder. From these responses, MSPO computes the Confidence-aware Perception Reward (CaPR), using a perception sign obtained by thresholding checklist accuracy to determine reward direction and posterior confidence from a Beta distribution over latent perception accuracy to scale reward magnitude. We then derive the vision advantage through group-relative normalization of CaPR and scale it using main-task success rates. This advantage guides updates to the vision encoder, while the projector and LLM retain outcome-based GRPO updates. Extensive experiments on seven mathematical and general reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that MSPO outperforms existing RL-tuned models on average at both the 3B and 7B scales.

</details>


### [201] [RestPose: Canonicalization for Open Object Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32696)
*Sandeep Inuganti,Weihang Li,Benjamin Busam,Didier Stricker,Jason Rambach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RestPose方法，从单张图像自动生成物体网格并通过几何与物理分析导出规范坐标系，实现无需参考模型与规范框架约定的开放物体姿态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 物体姿态估计任务要求从单张图像输出相对于某规范坐标系的物体姿态；学习类方法需先由参考模型定义该规范框架并据此生成训练用真值标注，目标是提升跨物体、跨类别的泛化能力。

Motivation: 现有方法中规范框架由人为任意选定的建模约定决定，训练与推断都被这一任意约定绑定，且依赖参考模型，从而限制了泛化性；论文据此主张规范框架本可从物体本身推导。

Method: RestPose在训练阶段不使用参考模型或规范框架约定：先从单张输入图像生成物体网格，再通过对物体的几何与物理分析将其规范化（canonicalize），随后利用基础模型（foundation model）的稠密匹配估计姿态；由此将"任意约定"替换为"由物体推导的规范框架"。

实验效果: 在HouseCat6D、NOCS和Omni6DPose类别级数据集上，无需任务特定训练即比监督式SOTA方法在绝对度量上提升18.3个点、在尺度无关度量上提升13.8个点。

Abstract: In object pose estimation methods, predicted poses are expressed with respect to a canonical frame, defined by a reference model of that object. In learning-based methods, an arbitrarily chosen modeling convention defines this canonical frame, which is then used to generate the ground-truth pose-annotations for training. Object pose estimation methods are therefore bound to such arbitrary conventions, restricting generalization. In this work, we propose that such a canonical frame can instead be derived for any object, and present RestPose, an approach towards open object pose estimation that does not require reference models or canonical frame conventions during training. From a single input image, we generate an object mesh and canonicalize it through a geometric and physical analysis of the object, then use foundation model-based dense matching to estimate the pose. We choose category-level benchmarks for our experiments, which contain instances across various object categories and enable us to measure the effectiveness of our canonicalization. Across HouseCat6D, NOCS, and Omni6DPose datasets, we improve on the supervised state-of-the-art methods by 18.3 points in absolute metrics and 13.8 points in scale-agnostic metrics, without requiring task-specific training. Code will be published.

</details>


### [202] [Knocked Off Course: Tracing Uncommanded Motion from Perception to Action in Embodied VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32705)
*Dan Zlotnikov,Nadav Alali,Ohad Ben-Shahar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文系统评测跟踪型VLA模型在非指令性自运动（风、不平地面、路面颠簸等扰动）下的鲁棒性，定位表示与动作两个环节的缺陷，并通过2%量级的针对性微调显著提升目标丢失后的恢复成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: Vision-Language-Action（VLA）模型以第一视角视频与文本目标描述为输入，输出具身跟踪平台的动作指令；目标是让平台按指令运动并稳定跟踪目标。实际平台受风、不平地形、路面颠簸等扰动影响，无法精确执行指令，因此需要研究模型如何应对这类"非指令性自运动"。

Motivation: 现有工作默认平台会精确执行动作指令，未考察环境扰动引起的非指令性自运动如何影响VLA的感知与决策，也缺乏对"像素→动作"链路上具体失效环节的定位，因而无法指导针对性的改进。

Method: 首先在3个数据集上对3个跟踪VLA进行扰动基准评测；随后逐层追踪自运动从像素到输出动作的传递，识别两个缺口：（1）表示层面，帧间显式关系可可靠恢复运动，但对历史帧的激进压缩削弱了小运动读出；（2）动作层面，大旋转虽进入决策状态，但解码出的转向方向仅保留镜像旋转变换效应的几个百分点。基于该分析，对Uni-NaVid设计一系列分析驱动的变体，仅用相当于原训练集1.7%的数据进行微调。

实验效果: 三个模型对各扰动的敏感度不同，但均对目标突然丢失敏感；微调变体在目标丢失后提升5–22个点且不损害无扰动成功率，平滑丢失情形下最佳变体相对提升72%，弥补了76%的与无扰动性能的差距；并表明改进部分经由文本中介的动作依赖实现，无需新架构、大规模数据或从头训练。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) enable simultaneous processing of egocentric video and textual target descriptions to produce action commands for embodied tracking platforms. However, these platforms rarely move exactly as commanded, in part due to unexpected environmental disturbances such as wind, unleveled terrain, and road bumps. Here we study how such models handle uncommanded egomotion. First, we explore performance across diverse disturbances by benchmarking three tracking VLAs on three datasets. We find that models differ in which environmental disturbances hurt them most, but all are sensitive to sudden target loss. Tracing egomotion from pixels to emitted actions, we identify two gaps. In representation, motion is reliably recovered through explicit relations between frames, but aggressive compression of past frames weakens small-motion readouts. In action, large rotations do reach the decision state, yet the decoded direction carries only a few percent of a mirrored rotation's effect on the turn. Targeting both gaps, we fine-tune one model, Uni-NaVid, on data amounting to just 1.7% of its original training set in a ladder of analysis-motivated variants. These gain 5-22 points after target-loss without degrading clean success; after a smooth loss, the best one improves success by 72% (relative), recovering 76% of its gap to clean. We also show that targeted fine-tuning improves recovery partly via a text-mediated action dependency, without new architecture, large-scale data collection or training from scratch.

</details>


### [203] [SplatSeg3D: Efficient Zero-Shot 3D Segmentation via Point Cloud Surfelization and Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32716)
*Sandeep Inuganti,Mahdi Chamseddine,Marcel Rogge,Martin R. Oswald,Didier Stricker,Jason Rambach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SplatSeg3D 通过将裸彩色点云表面化并渲染为 2D 高斯泼溅视图，实现无需图像、相机位姿、类别监督与实例掩码的开放词汇 3D 分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是开放词汇 3D 语义/实例分割：输入为仅含颜色的 3D 点云，输出为带开放词汇语义（及新类实例）标注的 3D 分割结果，目标是摆脱对配准图像与语义监督的依赖。

Motivation: 现有开放词汇 3D 方法依赖与点云配准的 2D 基础模型图像，而这些图像常不可得；直接做 3D 点云分割的方法又需要语义类别监督，难以零样本部署。该空白促使作者从点云自身合成视图以桥接 2D 基础模型。

Method: 先将点云表面化为有向 2D 高斯（surfel），再通过泼溅渲染场景视图，并用最大化场景覆盖、最少视图数的视图选择策略控制数量；随后对渲染视图施加文本提示分割，为每个 3D 点获得多视角语义线索并融合为 3D 分割；整个过程无需配套图像、位姿、类别监督或实例掩码提案。

实验效果: 在 Scannet、Scannet++、Replica、S3DIS 四个基准上评估语义分割与新类实例分割，性能优于或持平当前最优，且在运行时间与计算开销上更高效；摘要未给出具体数值。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary 3D segmentation methods rely on 2D foundation models applied to registered images captured alongside the 3D point clouds. However, such images may not be available in many cases, while direct 3D point cloud segmentation methods require semantic class supervision and are challenging to deploy in a zero-shot manner. To address these issues, we present SplatSeg3D, which segments a bare colored point cloud in an open-vocabulary manner without requiring the accompanying images, camera poses, class supervision, or instance mask proposals. To achieve this, we first surfelize the input point cloud and obtain oriented 2D Gaussians. We then render views of the scene by splatting, with a view selection strategy that maximizes scene coverage with as few views as possible. Next, we apply text-prompted segmentation on the rendered views, giving each 3D point a set of multi-view semantic cues, fused to produce a 3D segmentation of the scene. We evaluate SplatSeg3D on semantic segmentation and novel-class instance segmentation on 4 standard benchmarks: Scannet, Scannet++, Replica, and S3DIS. Our pipeline not only performs better or on-par with the state-of-the-art, but we also achieve these results efficiently across runtime and compute requirements. Code will be published.

</details>


### [204] [Diving Deeper into Dense Keypoint Localization in Object Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32728)
*Jiahong Chen,Jinghao Wang,Yueneng Yang,Banglei Guan,Zi Wang,Dewen Hu,Qifeng Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GHLAPose 提出混合格雷-二进制编码与局部感知细化的全局到局部框架，用于高精度刚体 6D 位姿估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 刚体目标 6D 位姿估计是计算机视觉的基础且具挑战性的任务，需从单张 RGB 图像预测目标的旋转与平移；论文在 LM、LM-O、YCB-V、TUD-L 等标准数据集上以位姿精度指标进行评估。

Motivation: 现有稠密语义关键点方法采用二进制编码实现高效全局关键点定位，但二进制编码的不连续性阻碍高分辨率下的有效训练，且其固有的离散化与量化误差带来显著定位不确定性。

Method: 提出 GHLAPose 全局到局部框架：先用混合格雷-二进制（HGB）编码缓解不连续性，获得精确的初始全局关键点定位；再由局部感知细化（LAR）模块估计不确定性并细化关键点位置，以补偿编码误差；二者协同聚合全局与局部特征，形成精确的全局到局部策略。

实验效果: 在 LM、LM-O、YCB-V、TUD-L 上超越现有最优方法，细粒度指标提升明显：LM-O 的 ADD(-S) 0.02d/0.05d 分别领先次优方法 1.4% 和 1.9%，YCB-V 的 ADD(-S) 0.1d 领先 1%。

Abstract: 6D pose estimation of rigid objects is a fundamental and challenging task in computer vision. Recent dense semantic keypoint-based methods employ binary encoding for efficient global keypoint localization, delivering strong performance in object pose estimation. Nevertheless, the discontinuous nature of binary encoding hinders effective training at high resolutions, while its inherent discrete and quantization errors lead to significant localization uncertainty. We propose GHLAPose, a global-to-local framework that synergizes hybrid Gray-binary (HGB) encoding with local-aware refinement (LAR). Specifically, HGB encoding first alleviates discontinuity to achieve accurate initial global keypoint localization. To further address intrinsic encoding errors, the LAR module estimates uncertainty and refines keypoint localization. This synergy enables a precise global-to-local strategy by aggregating global and local features. Extensive experiments on LM, LM-O, YCB-V, and TUD-L datasets demonstrate that GHLAPose surpasses state-of-the-art methods, particularly on fine-grained metrics. On LM-O, it outperforms the second-best method by 1.4% and 1.9% on ADD(-S) 0.02d and 0.05d respectively, and on YCB-V by 1% on ADD(-S) 0.1d. The code will be available at GHLAPose.

</details>


### [205] [AnyPrune: Optimization-Free Pruning for Any Feedforward 3D Gaussian Splatting Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32735)
*Nicola Leonetti,Federico Stella,Aoxiang Fan,Saqib Javed,Pascal Fua,Luigi Di Stefano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnyPrune 是一个无需优化的 transformer 剪枝模块，可叠加在任意前馈 3DGS 管线上，通过学习管线特有的冗余模式高效去除多余高斯点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 前馈 3DGS 管线能在单次前向传播中由输入图像重建可实时渲染的 3D 高斯表示，但所得表示包含远超渲染所需的高斯数量（多视角输入时尤甚），导致显存与存储开销激增。目标是在保持渲染质量的前提下压缩该表示。

Motivation: 现有剪枝方法要么依赖代价高昂的再优化，违背单次前向传播的初衷；要么采用简单策略，未能充分利用可用信息。因此需要一种既能免优化、又能充分挖掘信息的高效剪枝方案。
