We study both traps with verified failure-driven repair (VFR): typed minimal-pair failure mining, verification by three LLM judges that answer without seeing the stored labels, answer-first corrective targets, and LoRA fine-tuning with replay. VFR repairs 82.8% of held-out failures (base model: 10.0%), and on independently labeled, unconditioned pairs it raises overall accuracy from 87.4% to 92.4% through spatial relations, whose accuracy rises from 66.7% to 96.8% even where the wording points to the wrong answer. Yet on negation, of which it repairs 88.9% of held-out failures, its accuracy on unconditioned pairs falls from 88.7% to 79.7%: it breaks negations about absent objects, rare in its training set. Without verification, accuracy on unconditioned pairs falls to 80.8%, below the base model's 87.4%, through a negation shortcut our development benchmarks miss; without failure selection, it stays at 92.0% (one seed) while repair falls to 73.4%. Training also lowers two-image relation accuracy (74.3% to 49.6%; replay data alone: 48.4%); multi-image replay partly and unevenly reverses this. Paraphrasing the spatial questions keeps most of the gain (87.3% vs. the base model's 62.0%), though rewording lowers spatial repair on held-out failures from 92.9% to 76.8%. On What'sUp's independent photos of objects under others, caption choice stays perfect while accuracy on our yes/no pairs falls from 84.5% to 26.4%.

</details>


### [240] [Decision-Native Multimodal Clinical Modeling via Anatomical Grounding and Confidence Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32993)
*Juntao Jiang,Wenqi Zhong,Guangyu Guo,Dexia Chen,Yilong Hu,Yunhao Bai,Zhenyu Yi,Jianwei Xu,Tony C. W. Mok,Muxuan Gao,Ling Zhang,Yong Liu,Jiangning Zhang,Zhongwei Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Radev 是一种面向三维 CT 结构化临床决策的多模态模型，通过解剖引导的视觉压缩、候选直接预测与反事实置信度监督，同时提升决策准确率与置信度校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 该任务基于体素级 CT 数据输出结构化的临床判断（如病变存在与否、多项选择题答案、序数严重程度），要求预测既具备解剖学依据，又给出经过校准的置信度估计。

Motivation: 现有 3D 医学多模态模型存在未校准的自回归解码，以及对无关解剖区域的捷径依赖，导致置信度不可靠、决策缺乏解剖学依据。

Method: 采用冻结 3D 编码器提取可复用的器官级特征，由查询动态选择并在预测的解剖边界内关注 2D 切片特征，实现全局上下文与细粒度信息的桥接；紧凑视觉 token 使语言主干单次前向即可输出候选概率，免去自回归解码；并引入反事实校准训练（CCT），在完整与干预后的器官表征下对比预测，使移除目标器官证据导致决策质量下降时置信度随之降低，同时惩罚移除无关器官时的过度置信度下降。

实验效果: 摘要称在构建的 CT-Verdict 基准（涵盖二分类征象存在性、多项选择题与序数严重程度评估，含分布内与外部队列）上，Radev 在决策准确率与置信度校准上均显著优于现有最优方法，并在分布偏移下保持稳健泛化；摘要未报告具体数值。

Abstract: Structured clinical decisions from volumetric computed tomography (CT) require anatomically grounded predictions and calibrated confidence estimates. However, existing 3D medical multimodal models often suffer from uncalibrated autoregressive decoding and shortcut reliance on irrelevant anatomical regions. We introduce Radev, a decision-native multimodal model that integrates anatomy-guided visual compression, direct candidate prediction, and counterfactual confidence supervision. To bridge global context with fine-grained slice details, a frozen 3D encoder extracts reusable organ-level features, which are dynamically selected by the query to attend to 2D slice features within predicted anatomical bounds. The resulting compact visual tokens enable the language backbone to estimate candidate probabilities in a single forward pass without autoregressive decoding. We further propose Counterfactual-Calibrated Training (CCT) to compare predictions under intact and intervened organ representations. CCT encourages confidence to decrease when removing target-organ evidence degrades decision quality, while penalizing excessive confidence drops under unrelated-organ removal. To facilitate rigorous evaluation, we construct CT-Verdict, a comprehensive benchmark spanning binary finding presence, multiple-choice questions, and ordinal severity assessment across both in-distribution and external cohorts. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Radev significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both decision accuracy and confidence calibration, while generalizing robustly under distribution shifts. Project page at https://juntaojianggavin.github.io/projects/Radev/

</details>


### [241] [PAVE: Preference-Aligned Visual Egocentric Learning for the Blind and Low-Vision Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32996)
*Nan Li,Shaofeng Liang,Shiyun Zhao,Keni Shi,Zhencheng Huang,Bowen Tian,Jiemin Wu,Wenshuo Chen,Kaishen Yuan,Runwei Guan,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出偏好对齐的PA-EgoQA数据集与PAVE框架，使第一人称视频问答输出更贴合盲人及低视力用户的实际辅助需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 该任务是以第一人称（自我中心）视频问答的方式，让盲人和低视力（BLV）用户用自然语言查询身边环境；输入为自拍视频与用户查询，输出为可直接用于行动指导的回答，目标是生成具备空间方位、可操作细节且简洁的实用回答。

Motivation: 现有基准与模型仅以与参考答案的语义一致性为优化目标，把视觉辅助简化为事实匹配；而事实正确但缺乏空间方位、可操作细节或简洁性的回答，对用户当下的即时任务实际无用。

Method: 基于EgoBlind的真实BLV自拍视频与真实用户查询，按实证BLV沟通准则重构参考答案，构建偏好对齐数据集PA-EgoQA；提出PAVE统一学习与推理框架，将结构化答案策略微调与确定性、以问题为条件的路由相结合，在推理时依据查询表层形式自适应决定回答表述；并用Filter-GRPO对齐，联合分层式正确性-质量奖励与基于对比的候选组过滤，从事实正确性对比与正确回答间的细粒度质量差异中提取学习信号。

实验效果: 在三类视觉-语言骨干模型上的实验一致提升了事实准确性，并在辅助回答质量上取得显著增益（摘要未报告具体基准数值）。

Abstract: Egocentric video question answering enables blind and low-vision (BLV) users to query their immediate surroundings through natural language. However, existing benchmarks and models primarily optimize for semantic agreement with reference answers, reducing visual assistance to a purely factual matching task. In real-world scenarios, a factually correct response remains practically unhelpful if it lacks the spatial orientation, actionable detail, or conciseness required by the immediate task of the user. Drawing upon real BLV egocentric videos and authentic user queries from EgoBlind, we construct the PA-EgoQA, a preference-aligned dataset that reconstructs reference answers according to empirical BLV communication criteria. Based on this dataset, we introduce Preference-Aligned Visual Egocentric Learning (PAVE), a unified learning and inference framework that couples structured answer-policy fine-tuning with deterministic question-conditioned routing to adapt response formulation directly to the surface form of the query at inference time. We further align the model via Filter-GRPO, which pairs a hierarchical correctness-quality reward with contrast-based candidate-group filtering to extract learning signals from both factual accuracy contrasts and fine-grained quality variations among correct answers. Extensive experiments across three vision-language backbones demonstrate consistent improvements in factual accuracy alongside substantial gains in assistive response quality. These results establish a concrete paradigm for translating human-centered response requirements into egocentric supervision, generation policies, and post-training objectives. Code will be provided at https://github.com/shawnliang420/PAVE.

</details>


### [242] [First-Hit Photoconsistency for Neural Surface Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32998)
*Gabriel Lenain,Edmond Boyer,Jean Ponce*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出仅依赖多视角图像块光度一致性与几何可见性、无需体渲染和参数化外观模型的神经隐式表面重建方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务为多视角表面重建：输入多视角图像及其位姿，输出神经隐式表示的场景表面几何，目标是获得准确的三维表面而非仅合成图像。

Motivation: 现有神经隐式与高斯泼溅方法联合优化几何与外观，带来解耦歧义：参数化外观模型可补偿几何误差，从而在表面不准确时仍能高精度复现输入图像，导致几何质量被掩盖。

Method: 对每条参考光线使用球追踪求首个表面交点，由交点位置与法向定义局部图像块，在考虑几何可见性的相邻视角中直接从输入图像采样颜色并计算光度一致性；网络采用1-Lipschitz MLP以保证球追踪效率与稳定；仅使用单一光度一致性损失，不含前景掩码与辅助几何正则。

实验效果: 在BlendedMVS（BMVS）上取得所比较方法中最低的平均Chamfer距离，并在DTU上达到与领先重建方法相当的几何精度。

Abstract: Recent neural implicit and Gaussian splatting approaches have achieved impressive results in multi-view surface reconstruction. However, jointly optimizing geometry and appearance introduces ambiguities. A parametric appearance model can compensate for geometric errors, reproducing the input images accurately without an accurate surface. We demonstrate accurate neural implicit surface reconstruction using only multi-view patch photoconsistency with geometry-based visibility, without volumetric rendering or parametric appearance models. For each reference ray, sphere tracing computes the first surface intersection. Its position and normal define a local patch whose photoconsistency is evaluated across visible neighboring views using colors sampled directly from the input images. For efficient sphere tracing, we use a 1-Lipschitz multi-layer perceptron. We use a single photoconsistency loss, without foreground masks or auxiliary geometric regularization. Experiments show that our method achieves the lowest mean Chamfer distance on BlendedMVS (BMVS) among compared methods and matches the geometric accuracy of leading reconstruction methods on DTU.

</details>


### [243] [One, No One and One Hundred Thousand CTs: Testing Anatomical Control in Text-to-CT Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33003)
*Francesca Pia Panaccione,Eugenio Lomurno,Matteo Matteucci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CtrlCT 工具包，以 Credibility 与 Control 两项属性统一评测 Text-to-CT 生成器，并用 Report Card 汇总。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是由放射报告（文本）生成对应胸部 CT，借助深度学习与公开的 CT-报告配对数据集实现。论文目标是提供可比较、可检验报告可控性的标准化评测方法。

Motivation: 现有 Text-to-CT 生成器的评测在预处理与指标上各不相同，难以横向比较；其协议几乎从不检验编辑报告是否真的按预期改变 CT，甚至在同一随机种子下也不检验，因而"报告控制解剖结构"这一假设缺乏证据支撑。

Method: 提出 CtrlCT 工具包，检验生成器的两项属性：Credibility 将生成 CT 作为群体与真实 CT 及报告比较；Control 采用蜕变测试（metamorphic），无需参考 CT——固定种子下编辑某一征象应产生所要求的改变、被否定的征象应保持缺失、远处解剖的位移不应超过换新种子的影响，且改变需跨种子成立；最后以 Report Card 汇总各项指标与洞见。在 940 例胸部 CT 及 687 条胸腔积液与心脏增大编辑上对五个公开生成器做基准，并留出第六个用于测试该工具包。

实验效果: 在 940 例胸部 CT 与 687 条编辑上，最可信的生成器对新增胸腔积液几乎无响应，而响应最强的生成器产出的 CT 真实性和多样性较差；现有文献中的分析未能揭示这一权衡。工具包、编辑、种子及 30,525 张合成 CT 将在录用后公开。

Abstract: Recent advances in deep learning and the public availability of computed tomography (CT) datasets with paired reports have enabled many Text-to-CT generators. However, their evaluations almost always differ in preprocessing and metrics, which hinders comparison. Moreover, their protocols almost never test how editing the report changes the CT, not even at the same seed, so the report is often assumed to control the anatomy without evidence of how much and how consistently it does. We therefore propose CtrlCT, a toolkit that tests two properties of a generator, Credibility and Control. Credibility tests compare generated CTs, as a population, with real CTs and reports; Control tests are metamorphic and need no reference CT: at a fixed seed, editing one finding should change it as requested, keep negated findings absent and shift distant anatomy no more than a new seed, and the change should hold across seeds. A Report Card summarises all measures and insights for each generator. We benchmark five public generators on 940 chest CTs and 687 edits of pleural effusion and cardiomegaly, holding out a sixth to test the toolkit. Among them, the most credible generators barely respond to an added effusion, while the most responsive produce less realistic and less diverse CTs. The analyses proposed in the literature do not reveal this. We release CtrlCT, its edits, seeds and 30,525 synthetic CTs after acceptance.

</details>


### [244] [Train What You Run: Probabilistic Convolutions for Learning Token Compaction in State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33009)
*Piotr Kubaty,Mikhail Saladkou,Jacek Tabor,Łukasz Struski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProbConv 通过在保留位置处计算激活前的精确期望卷积，使局部卷积序列模型中的可微 token 压缩得以正确训练，并在强压缩下取得最佳效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 视觉 token 压缩通过仅将选中的图像块或视频 token 传给后续层来降低推理成本，本文目标是学习在压缩后新邻域结构下应保留哪些 token。

Motivation: 在含局部卷积的序列模型中，每次删除 token 都会改变其邻居关系，因此学习保留哪些 token 需要一种能反映新邻域的训练信号，而现有做法缺乏对此的显式建模。

Method: ProbConv 在独立保留决策假设下，对保留位置计算激活前的精确期望卷积，该算子对保留概率可微；当决策取二值时，ProbConv 与阶跃门控可复现压缩后的 Mamba 块。

实验效果: 在图像、视觉-语言与视频骨干网络上，ProbConv 在强压缩下表现最佳，且随删除 token 增多相对匹配训练基线的优势扩大；在 Cobra 上于相同推理预算下优于已发表视觉-语言剪枝方法的适配版本，并在视频帧选择与语音帧丢弃任务上优于匹配的零掩码训练。摘要未报告具体数值。

Abstract: Visual token compaction reduces inference cost by passing only selected image patches or video tokens to later layers. In sequence models with local convolutions, each removal changes which tokens are neighbors. Learning which tokens to keep therefore requires a training signal that accounts for the new neighborhoods. We introduce ProbConv, which computes the exact expected convolution before activation at a kept position under independent keep decisions. The operator is differentiable in the keep probabilities. With binary decisions, ProbConv and step gating reproduce the compacted Mamba block. Across image, vision--language and video backbones, ProbConv is strongest under strong compaction: its advantage over matched training controls grows as more tokens are removed. In Cobra, it also outperforms adaptations of published vision--language pruning methods at matched inference budgets. ProbConv also improves over matched zero-mask training in video frame selection and speech frame dropping. Our code is available at: https://github.com/gmum/probconv

</details>


### [245] [Embodied AI Fails the Mirror Test: How a Simple Mirror Breaks Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33010)
*Xuanchen Liu,Hejun Zhang,Boxuan Dong,Ying Wu,Heshuai Li,Wen Gao,Yonghong Tian,Weihao Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 受动物镜像自我识别测试启发，论文提出 RoboDojo-Mirror 基准，系统检验含反射场景下机器人操作策略的失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是评估机器人操作策略能否在含镜面反射的场景中可靠地操作真实物体。基准基于 RoboDojo 构建，保持任务目标、成功判据与策略检查点不变，输入为无镜面的基线、单镜面、双镜面与镜面桌面等条件，输出为各策略在这些条件下的成功率及失败行为分析。

Motivation: 现有机器人操作基准与策略评估通常假设无镜面反射的理想视觉环境，未考察反射这一常见物理现象对策略的影响，也缺乏统一的对照实验设计来区分"镜面作为障碍物"与"镜面作为视觉干扰"两种效应。

Method: 构建 RoboDojo-Mirror 基准，在 RoboDojo 上设置无镜面基线及单镜面、双镜面、镜面桌面三类镜面条件，并在五类策略（VLA 模型、世界动作模型、智能体策略）上评测；同时引入几何匹配的非反射面板作为对照，以隔离碰撞效应；再通过轨迹与接触分析及针对性诊断（近反射抓取、推动进展、主相机遮挡下的真实目标交互）识别失效机理；最后用 π0.5 与 FastWAM 进行真机验证。

实验效果: 所有镜面条件均降低总体成功率，三种条件平均下降 10.8–56.7 个百分点，镜面桌面条件达 62.5 个百分点；由于 50–88% 的镜面相关失败未记录到机器人与面板接触，碰撞并非主因，轨迹与接触分析指向对反射物体的"幻影瞄准"与对真实物体的空间定位错误。真机实验中 π0.5 与 FastWAM 在直立镜面与镜面桌面条件下成功率同样下降。

Abstract: Can a simple mirror expose the limitations of today's robotic manipulation policies? Inspired by the mirror self-recognition test in animal cognition, we investigate whether robot policies can reliably manipulate physical objects in scenes containing their reflections. We introduce RoboDojo-Mirror, a benchmark built on RoboDojo that preserves task objectives, success criteria, and policy checkpoints while comparing a mirror-free baseline with single-mirror, dual-mirror, and mirrored-tabletop conditions. Across five policies spanning Vision-Language-Action models, World Action Models, and agentic policies, every mirror condition reduces aggregate success rates: averaged over the three conditions, the drop ranges from 10.8 to 56.7 percentage points across policies, and it reaches 62.5 points on the mirrored tabletop. Because a mirror is also a physical obstacle, we compare it against geometrically matched non-reflective panels; 50--88\% of mirror-specific failures occur without recorded robot--panel contact, so direct collisions alone do not explain the degradation. Trajectory and contact analyses instead reveal recurring behaviors consistent with phantom targeting of reflected objects and spatial mislocalization of real ones. Targeted diagnostics further characterize grasping near reflections, policy-dependent pushing progress, and physical-target interaction under main-camera occlusion. Real-robot experiments with $π_{0.5}$ and FastWAM also show lower overall success rates under the upright-mirror and mirrored-tabletop conditions. These findings reveal a shared vulnerability to reflective environments across diverse robot policies and motivate reflection-aware evaluation of embodied intelligence. Code is available at https://github.com/iPPLab/robodojo-mirror.

</details>


### [246] [MESSI: Multi-Expert Soccer Skill Integration for Physics-Based Character Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33011)
*Xiaogang Peng,Jiaqi Zou,Lei Zhong,Sirui Xu,Huaizu Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MESSI 通过冻结的学生策略上加一个共享的低秩适配器，实现多个物理仿真足球技能之间的平滑切换，同时保持原有技能策略不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是让物理仿真中的足球角色掌握多种运动技能，并能流畅地从一个技能过渡到另一个技能（如带球后射门）。目标是使技能串行执行时不因前置行为留下的陌生状态而失效。

Motivation: 单独训练表现良好的技能在顺序执行时可能失败，因为前置行为会把角色和球带入训练分布外的状态；而针对每种技能过渡分别重训各自的专家代价高昂。

Method: 先用运动先验与任务奖励训练八个具备任务能力的专家，再将其蒸馏为一个统一的、以技能为条件的 Transformer 学生策略。随后在冻结的学生策略上，通过在线强化学习训练一个共享的、上下文条件的低秩适配器：它在技能切换时激活，帮助目标技能应对陌生进入状态；同时学习一个退出机制决定何时把控制权交还原学生策略。原生技能执行时禁用适配器，从而在结构上保证原有策略不变。

实验效果: 在单项技能、技能过渡和连续技能链的实验中，MESSI 相较直接切换取得显著提升，带球到射门的成功率从 19.8% 提高到 61.8%。

Abstract: Physics-based soccer characters must perform diverse motor skills and transition smoothly between them, such as dribbling into a shot. However, skills that perform well in isolation may fail when executed sequentially, as preceding behaviors can leave the character and ball in unfamiliar states. We present MESSI (Multi-Expert Soccer Skill Integration), a framework that enables multiple soccer skill transitions through shared adaptation while preserving the original skill policy outside transitions. MESSI first trains eight task-competent experts using motion priors and task rewards, then distills them into a unified, skill-conditioned Transformer. Rather than retraining individual experts for different transitions, we train a shared, context-conditioned low-rank adapter on the frozen student through online reinforcement learning. Activated upon skill switching, the adapter helps the target skill handle unfamiliar entry states, while a learned exit mechanism determines when to return control to the original student. By disabling the adapter during native skill execution, MESSI preserves the original student policy by construction. Experiments across individual skills, skill transitions, and continuous skill chains demonstrate substantial improvements over direct switching, including an increase in dribbling-to-shooting success from 19.8% to 61.8%.

</details>


### [247] [RFW-W: Scaling Physical Asset Universes for Generative Embodied Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33013)
*Jiawei Weng,Rui Zhang,Saining Zhang,Changjian Pan,Yuanbo Lyu,Jiarui Guo,Yongzhi Li,Guolin Liu,Jialu Liu,Jiankun Wang,Yi Yang,Peishuo Li,Zhiheng Li,Jiaying Wan,Junhao Chen,Weiqing Xiao,Jingrun Du,Wenyi Li,Weiyi Lin,Zenan Meng,Hu Wei,Lin Qu,Zongzheng Zhang,Hao Zhao,Chaojian Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RFW-W 是一个统一的大规模物理资产体系，涵盖刚体、铰接体与可变形物体，并通过通用接口支持任务条件的场景组装与具身策略训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 针对具身仿真规模化需求，本文构建并整合多类可执行物理表示的资产库，输出可供检索与场景装配的资产与分类体系，目标是生成物理落地、任务条件的具身场景。

Motivation: 现有工作缺乏同时覆盖刚体、铰接体与可变形物体、且具备可执行物理表示与统一接口的大规模资产基础，难以支撑规模化具身仿真与场景组装。

Method: 构建含1,010,386条资产记录的资产宇宙：RFW-R（524,611个、1,212类）结合类别引导生成、碰撞几何、组合物理属性与交互标注；RFW-A（402,410个、531类）采用可复用的智能体编写程序化生成器，显式建模部件与关节；RFW-S（83,365个联合验证、485类）采用结构感知仿真表示、标定与独立测试；并以统一接口保留各资产类型特有物理约束，支持检索与场景组装。

实验效果: RFW-R 在资产覆盖度与场景满足能力上优于现有数据集；在五个 RoboTwin 任务、每任务每种资产源100条演示下，RFW-R 训练的策略宏成功率在干净设置为71.2%、随机化为41.8%，分别超出最强基线15.8和10.6个百分点。

Abstract: Scaling embodied simulation requires diverse objects with executable physical representations. We present RFW-W, a physical asset universe connecting rigid, articulated, and deformable asset sources to task-conditioned scene composition. Its reported inventories comprise 1,010,386 asset records: 524,611 rigid assets in 1,212 categories (RFW-R), 402,410 articulated assets in 531 categories (RFW-A), and 83,365 jointly verified deformables in 485 categories (RFW-S). These taxonomies contribute 2,228 dataset--category entries. RFW-R couples category-guided generation with collision geometry, composed physical properties, and interaction annotations; RFW-A uses reusable agent-authored procedural generators with explicit part and joint structure; RFW-S uses structure-aware simulation representations, calibration, and independent testing. A common interface preserves physical requirements specific to each asset type for retrieval and scene assembly. Quantitative evaluations demonstrate that RFW-R provides broader asset coverage and stronger scene-fulfillment capabilities than existing datasets. Across five RoboTwin tasks, using 100 demonstrations per task for each asset source, policies trained on RFW-R achieve macro success rates of 71.2% in clean settings and 41.8% under randomization, exceeding the strongest baseline by 15.8 and 10.6 percentage points, respectively. RFW-W provides a scalable and unified asset foundation for generating physically grounded, task-conditioned embodied worlds.

</details>


### [248] [Training Lightweight Gaze Models without Target Gaze Annotation: What Makes a Good Teacher?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33014)
*Scott J. Lewis,Di Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文通过受控比较11种监督条件，研究模型生成标签能否替代人工标注、以及能否在训练学生模型前预判教师质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务为注视目标估计（gaze target estimation），面向需要兼顾准确与高效的交互机器人场景。输入为视频数据与注视点标签来源，输出为轻量学生模型，目标是厘清何种监督信号适合训练学生模型。

Motivation: 模型生成标签虽可规模化获取，但"何为好教师"缺乏判据，且无法在训练学生前进行评估；现有工作也未系统区分教师能力与标签可靠性。

Method: 固定学生模型、训练协议与361k训练样本，仅改变注视点标签来源，在三个视频数据集、两种学生规模、三个随机种子上做11种监督条件的受控对比，并以人工标注评估所有学生；同时加入复制坐标基线与"提示中给出他人面部中心"的探测实验来检验捷径行为。

实验效果: 未在目标数据集注视标注上训练的Gaze-LLE所训学生在ViT-Tiny上与人工标注训练相当、在ViT-Small上更优（平均距离误差0.187 vs 0.196），且ViT-Tiny学生在其笔记本CPU上比教师快32倍；InternVL3与Qwen3-VL在提示含他人面部中心时分别有93.4%和81.1%的样本直接返回该坐标，复制坐标基线即可匹敌InternVL3；验证集标签误差可预判学生测试误差（Spearman ρ=0.91），仅10段标注片段在95%的抽样中识别出两名 specialist 教师。

Abstract: Model-generated labels offer a scalable route to training lightweight vision models, but what makes a model a good teacher, and can we tell before training a student? We study this in gaze target estimation, where interactive robots need models that are both accurate and efficient. In a controlled comparison of eleven supervision conditions, we vary only the source of gaze-point labels, keeping the student, training protocol and 361k training samples fixed, across three video datasets, two student sizes and three seeds; every student is evaluated against human annotation. Three findings emerge. First, imperfect model-generated labels can replace human annotation: Gaze-LLE, never trained on the target datasets' gaze annotation, trains students that match annotation-trained ones on ViT-Tiny and are significantly more accurate on ViT-Small (average distance error 0.187 vs 0.196), and the ViT-Tiny student runs 32$\times$ faster than its teacher on a laptop CPU. Second, apparent competence can conceal unreliable supervision: given another person's face centre in the prompt, InternVL3 and Qwen3-VL return it on 93.4% and 81.1% of test samples, and a copy-the-coordinate baseline matches InternVL3's labels and students. Without that coordinate, both models fall far behind the specialist. Third, good teachers can be identified before training: validation label error ranks student test error (Spearman's $ρ= 0.91$), and ten annotated clips single out the two specialist teachers in 95% of draws. These findings give practical principles for assessing supervision, exposing shortcuts and selecting teachers for lightweight models.

</details>


### [249] [RePix-VFI: Re-rendering Observed Pixels for Video Frame Interpolation with Generative Priors from One-Step Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33016)
*Dayeon Kim,Jeonghyeok Do,Munchurl Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RePix-VFI 将单步草稿条件视频扩散与关键帧像素重渲染结合，在多帧视频插帧中兼顾运动准确性与观测纹理保真，并显著降低采样开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 多帧视频插帧（VFI）需从稀疏关键帧重建稠密视频，输出中间帧，要求在大时间间隔下同时保证中间运动准确与外观忠实。

Motivation: 时序间隔增大时，光流类方法虽保留观测纹理但会在运动歧义下错置纹理；迭代式潜空间扩散模型虽能合成合理内容，却采样代价高，且经 VAE 重建时可能改变细节。

Method: 先用预训练视频扩散模型以单次前向对整窗光流渲染草稿进行精修；随后对每个目标帧，用光流估计器将其前后两个关键帧与生成预测对齐，得到对应关系；再在 VAE 之外依据该对应关系重渲染原始关键帧 RGB 像素（generative readback），最后用轻量外观融合头将重渲染结果与生成外观融合。

实验效果: 在 SNU-FILM ×8 与 ×16 上取得最高 PSNR 及最低 LPIPS、FloLPIPS、tOF，PSNR 分别比最佳扩散基线高 1.71 dB 和 1.48 dB，且比最先进多帧扩散 VFI 模型快 32–42 倍。

Abstract: Multi-frame video frame interpolation (VFI) aims to reconstruct dense video from sparse keyframes, requiring accurate intermediate motion and faithful appearance. As temporal gaps grow, flow-based methods can retain observed textures but misplace them under ambiguous motion, whereas iterative latent-diffusion models can synthesize plausible content but incur substantial sampling costs and may alter details during reconstruction with a variational autoencoder (VAE). We present RePix-VFI, named for Re-rendering observed Pixels, a multi-frame VFI framework that couples draft-conditioned one-step video diffusion with reconstruction from observed keyframes. A pretrained video diffusion model refines a flow-rendered draft of the whole window in a single forward pass. For each target frame, our generative readback matches its two bounding keyframes to the generated prediction using an optical-flow estimator. The resulting correspondences guide re-rendering of original keyframe RGB pixels outside the VAE. A lightweight appearance fusion head combines these renderings with generated appearance. Experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate significant improvements in reconstruction and perceptual quality. On SNU-FILM at $\times$8 and $\times$16, RePix-VFI achieves the highest PSNR and the lowest LPIPS, FloLPIPS, and tOF among the compared methods, exceeding the best evaluated diffusion-based baseline by 1.71 and 1.48dB in PSNR, respectively. It also runs 32--42$\times$ faster than the state-of-the-art multi-frame VFI diffusion model.

</details>


### [250] [E-MAPS: Event-Based Promptable Video Segmentation with Memory-as-Prompt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33017)
*Adonay Demewez Gebremedhin,Mohamad Alansari,Wessam Shehieb,Muzammal Naseer,Hasan AlMarzouqi,Sajid Javed,Naoufel Werghi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出事件相机驱动的可提示视频分割任务ePVS及E-MAPS框架，通过事件到Token对齐与记忆感知提示实现高效的事件流目标传播与交互式修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 论文形式化事件相机可提示视频分割（ePVS）任务：以事件流视频为输入，在任意时间窗口给出提示来初始化、修正或引入目标，输出全序列的目标掩码，目标是在快速运动和不利光照下保持稳健的分割与传播。

Motivation: RGB方法在快速运动与恶劣光照下脆弱，事件相机虽在这些场景保留信号，但运动缓慢时证据不足；现有可提示事件方法仅处理单一时间窗口，无法支持跨窗口的状态传播与交互修正，缺乏统一的事件域可提示视频分割范式与基准。

Method: 将预训练可提示分割器迁移到事件流：Event-to-Token（E2T）对齐循环事件特征与预训练表征并保留提示条件先验；Memory-Aware Prompting（MAP）把目标条件历史转为稀疏提示，使记忆与用户输入共享同一解码通路；并构建Event-View Masklets（EViM，206K对齐RGB-事件对、620K掩码）用于两阶段表征迁移与原生事件视频学习。

实验效果: 在三个训练不相交基准上，E-MAPS在单次提示与交互式ePVS中均领先且更高效，并在零样本目标跟踪基准上表现强劲（摘要未给出具体数值）。

Abstract: RGB-based video object segmentation (VOS) remains brittle under fast motion and adverse illumination. Event cameras preserve signal under blur and challenging exposure, but provide little evidence when motion slows. Interaction can restore object state for subsequent propagation, yet promptable event methods merely address single windows. We formalize event-based Promptable Video Segmentation (ePVS), where prompts at arbitrary windows initialize, correct, or introduce objects and predictions. We present Event Memory-As-Prompt Segmentation (E-MAPS), which transfers a pretrained promptable segmenter to event streams and learns propagation and correction. Event-to-Token (E2T) aligns recurrent event features with pretrained representations while preserving prompt-conditioning priors. Memory-Aware Prompting (MAP) converts object-conditioned history into sparse prompts, allowing memory and user inputs to share one decoder pathway efficiently. We construct Event-View Masklets (EViM) from 206K aligned RGB-event pairs and 620K masks for two-stage representation transfer and native event-video learning, and establish three training-disjoint benchmarks. E-MAPS leads prompt-once and interactive ePVS across all three benchmarks while being more efficient. It further demonstrates strong performance on zero-shot object tracking benchmarks. Project page: https://github.com/adonaydem/emaps-wacv

</details>


### [251] [GRAPE-Bench: When Vision Degrades, Does Video Reasoning Hold?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33020)
*Ce Zhang,Junyi He,Tianshi Ming,Jinxi He,Jianshu Zhang,Xiyuan Yang,Jianuo Zhu,Yicheng Tao,Xiyu Luo,Minghao Fu,Jiahui Zhu,Kehan Guo,Yunzhong Xiao,Jingyang Lin,Wei Chow,Lingdong Kong,Katia Sycara,Yaqi Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRAPE-Bench 是一个联合评估视觉退化条件与问题难度的视频推理基准，并提出合成数据微调、特权同策略蒸馏与偏好引导三种改进/控制手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 论文研究视频推理模型在视觉退化条件下的可靠性，面向自动驾驶等安全关键场景。基准输入为退化/恶劣天气下的视频及逐视频的三级递进问题，输出为模型在退化条件与难度层级上的推理表现评估。

Motivation: 已有基准多以人工模拟退化或高难度问题来考察该问题，未回答模型能否应对自然退化视频，以及能否完成对安全驾驶至关重要的较简单判断；同时整体准确率掩盖了不同难度层级的弱点。

Method: 构建包含日常生活与驾驶视频、覆盖十二种损坏类型的合成子集，并补充夜间及雨雪雾真实驾驶片段；每个视频配三道递进难度问题（从具安全含义的基础判断到高难推理压力测试）。此外引入合成数据微调、干净条件特权在线策略蒸馏，以及偏好引导以连续调节冻结模型在安全相关判断与高难推理间的权衡。

实验效果: 在开源与前沿模型上观察到合成与真实条件下的排名反转，以及被整体准确率掩盖的分层弱点；合成数据微调将 Qwen3.5-9B 真实场景准确率从 76.7% 提升至 81.1%，干净特权在线策略蒸馏在另一推理设置下达到 80.4%；偏好引导可在整体准确率几乎不变的情况下连续控制安全判断与高难推理的权衡。

Abstract: Despite strong performance on standard benchmarks, video reasoning models remain vulnerable to visual degradation, raising concerns about their reliability in safety-critical scenarios. Existing benchmarks address this concern in part by simulating visual degradation or probing model reasoning with challenging questions, leaving open how well models handle naturally degraded video and whether they can make the simpler judgments that matter for safe driving. To this end, we introduce GRAPE-Bench, which jointly evaluates video reasoning across visual degradation conditions and question difficulty. It combines a synthetic set spanning twelve corruption types across daily-life and driving videos with real-world footage of driving at night or in rain, snow, or fog. Each video is paired with three progressively challenging questions, from fundamental judgments with potential safety implications to demanding reasoning stress tests. Across open-weight and frontier models, we observe ranking reversals between synthetic and real-world conditions and level-specific weaknesses obscured by overall accuracy. Synthetic-data fine-tuning improves Qwen3.5-9B's real-world accuracy from 76.7% to 81.1%, while clean-privileged on-policy distillation reaches 80.4% in a separate reasoning-enabled setting. Through preference steering, we further demonstrate continuous control over a frozen model's trade-off between safety-relevant judgments and challenging reasoning, with little change in overall accuracy. Together, GRAPE-Bench provides a unified testbed for evaluating, improving, and controlling video reasoning under degraded visual conditions.

</details>


### [252] [SHARPnP: Self-Supervised and Plug-and-Play Hyperspectral Super-Resolution from Real Noisy Unaligned Camera Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33032)
*Mohamad Jouni,Brayan Monroy,Jorge Bacca,Mauro Dalla Mura*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SHARPnP 是一个可解释、无需干净参考的框架，将配准与重建耦合，实现 RGB 引导的噪声、未对齐测量下的高光谱超分辨。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是由单次曝光但空间分辨率低、噪声大的快照高光谱图像，结合细节丰富的低成本 RGB 图像引导，重建高空间分辨率的高光谱图像；输入为未对齐、含噪的 RGB-HSI 对，输出为高分辨率高光谱图像，目标是提升重建精度与跨传感器泛化性。

Motivation: 现有监督融合方法依赖较大的干净高分辨率高光谱数据集，对传感器噪声敏感，且难以泛化到新传感器；同时 RGB 与 NIR 之间存在空间变化的跨相机错位，使配准与融合难以协同。

Method: 提出 SHARPnP：通过固定即插即用融合模块反传的跨视角损失微调预训练配准模型，实现配准与重建耦合；利用时间与重损坏配对在含噪输入上做无参考自监督；测试时校准跨相机辐射度并精化对齐；融合采用逐序三波段去噪的即插即用策略。

实验效果: 在 REAL 基准上，干净输入下 4×、8×、16× 及高斯噪声（σ=5/255、10/255）4× 条件下均优于监督融合基线；在原生 RGB-HSI 快照测量上无需高分辨率参考即重建全部 51 个 VIS-NIR 波段，超出常规 31 波段 VIS 设置。

Abstract: Affordable snapshot hyperspectral cameras capture spectral images in a single exposure but suffer from limited spatial resolution and sensor noise. Low-cost RGB cameras provide fine visible details to guide NIR reconstruction, but introduce spatially varying cross-camera misalignment. Supervised fusion methods require relatively large datasets of clean, high-resolution hyperspectral images, are sensitive to sensor noise, and generalize poorly across new sensors. We introduce SHARPnP, an interpretable, flexible framework coupling registration and reconstruction for RGB-guided hyperspectral super-resolution from noisy, unaligned measurements without clean references. We adapt a pretrained registration model via a cross-view loss backpropagated through a fixed plug-and-play fusion module. Temporal and recorrupted pairs enable reference-free self-supervision on noisy inputs. At test time, we calibrate cross-camera radiometry and refine alignment. Plug-and-play fusion employs sequential three-band denoising. On the REAL benchmark, SHARPnP outperforms supervised fusion baselines at $4\times$, $8\times$, and $16\times$ on clean inputs and at $4\times$ under Gaussian noise ($σ=5/255, 10/255$). On native RGB-HSI snapshot measurements, it reconstructs all 51 VIS-NIR bands, beyond the conventional 31-band VIS setting, without high-resolution references.

</details>


### [253] [Linear Probes Can Guide: Training-Free Control of Representation Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33037)
*Pengwei Wang,José Morano,Botond Fazekas,Hrvoje Bogunović*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 LiSM 框架，利用预训练视觉基础模型高度线性可读的表征空间，将语义引导转化为贝叶斯线性逆问题，实现无需训练期条件化的轻量生成控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 面向基于表征空间的生成模型（如 Representation Autoencoders, RAE），目标是无需训练期条件化地对生成样本实施全局与局部语义控制（类别、属性、前景、关键点）。输入为探测器的语义得分约束，输出为受控生成结果。

Motivation: 现有工作仅将预训练视觉基础模型当作重建型 tokenizer 用于加速训练和提升保真度，忽视了其组织良好、线性可读的表征空间；而事后引导通常受限于噪声下得分估计的不确定性，且训练期条件化代价高。

Method: 依托期望线性性，证明对理想去噪器预测施加干净的线性探测器可得到任意噪声水平下探测得分的精确后验均值，该情形称为 Semantic Mean-Space，从而把语义引导重构为贝叶斯线性逆问题。在此基础上提出 LiSM 统一框架，开发并改编多种求解器，按“探测空间不确定性估计方式”与“修正如何通过表征传播”进行刻画；并提出 Fit-Path 求解器以同时实现强全局与局部控制。

实验效果: 摘要未报告具体数值结果或基准。定性结果显示，在类别、属性、前景、关键点四种控制设置下，LiSM 方法（尤其 Fit-Path）提供了有效且轻量的控制，优于噪声感知引导与像素空间引导。

Abstract: Representation-space generative models, such as Representation Autoencoders (RAEs), leverage pretrained vision foundation models (VFMs) primarily to accelerate training and improve sample fidelity. Yet VFMs are more than reconstruction tokenizers, as they feature rich representation spaces that are highly organized and linearly readable. We observe that this removes a key obstacle of post-hoc guidance: by linearity of expectation, a clean probe applied to an ideal denoiser's prediction yields the exact posterior mean of the probe scores at any noise level. This regime, which we call Semantic Mean-Space, turns semantic guidance into a Bayesian linear inverse problem, bypassing the need for training-time conditioning. With this foundation, we propose LiSM, a unified framework for Linear Guidance in Semantic Mean-Space. To validate the framework, we developed and adapted different solvers, and characterized them based on how they estimate probe-space uncertainty and propagate the correction through the representation. In addition, we propose Fit-Path, a solver designed to achieve strong global and local control. Our results in four control setups (class, attributes, foreground, and keypoints) show that LiSM methods, and Fit-Path in particular, provide effective, lightweight control across global and local tasks, outperforming noise-aware and pixel-space guidance.

</details>


### [254] [Spectrify: Framework for Hyperspectral Image Generation Using Computer Graphics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33042)
*Yasin Mamedov,Daniil Vladimirov,Sofiia Dorogova,Bakhtiyar Khalfin,Egor Ershov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Spectrify 通过基于材质类别的实测反射率谱与 Mitsuba 3 全光谱路径追踪，将 3D 场景数据集转换为高光谱图像，并发布 4,500 张渲染 HSI、代码及材质标注反射率库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是缓解高光谱图像（HSI）获取慢、成本高、最大真实数据集仅约 1,000 张的数据稀缺问题；输入为 3D 场景数据集与材质反射率谱库，输出为物理正确的合成 HSI（可投影为相机 RAW），目标是服务于高光谱重建与相机流水线等底层视觉方法的训练。

Motivation: 高层视觉已用 3D 渲染图像替代稀缺真实数据，但底层任务需要物理正确的光谱，因而依赖逐材质实测光谱的光谱渲染；现有光谱渲染仅覆盖少数场景，且多采用简化的成像模型，无法提供规模化、物理准确的训练数据。

Method: 构建 Spectrify 框架及按材质类别标注、由近距离高光谱数据集与分光光度计测量汇编的反射率谱库；使光谱随材质走：每个物体依其材质类别赋予基于物理的光学模型与实测反射率谱或光学常数，每盏灯赋予对应类型的发射谱，天空使用建模日光光谱；再由 Mitsuba 3 按波长对每个场景的完整光传输进行路径追踪。

实验效果: 仅用合成 HSI 训练的高光谱重建网络在多数测试集上对未见真实数据集的泛化优于用最大真实 HSI 数据集训练的网络；按材质类别而非仅按颜色赋谱在每个测试集上都提升迁移效果；由其 HSI 投影得到的 RAW 图像用于颜色恒常性训练时与现有合成 RAW 生成器表现相当。

Abstract: Hyperspectral images (HSIs) record the full spectrum of light at every pixel, so projecting an HSI through a camera's spectral sensitivities gives that camera's RAW image, and one HSI can supply training data for hyperspectral reconstruction and camera-pipeline methods. Real HSIs, however, are slow and expensive to capture, and the largest datasets hold only about 1,000 images. High-level vision commonly replaces scarce real data with images rendered from 3D scenes. Low-level tasks, however, need physically correct spectra, which requires spectral rendering with a measured spectrum for every material in a scene, and existing spectral renderings cover only a handful of scenes, mostly with a simplified image formation model. We present Spectrify, a framework that converts 3D scene datasets into HSIs, together with a library of reflectance spectra labeled by material and compiled from close-range hyperspectral datasets and spectrophotometer measurements. In Spectrify, spectra follow the material: each object receives a physically based optical model and measured reflectance spectra or optical constants from its material category, each lamp an emission spectrum of its type and the sky a modeled daylight spectrum, and Mitsuba 3 path-traces the full light transport of each scene per wavelength. We show that a hyperspectral reconstruction network trained only on our synthetic HSIs generalizes to unseen real datasets better than networks trained on the largest real HSI datasets on most test sets, and that assigning spectra by material category instead of by color alone improves this transfer on every test set. RAW images projected from our HSIs are also useful training data for color constancy, performing on par with existing synthetic RAW generators. We release the 4,500 rendered HSIs, the code of Spectrify and its material-labeled reflectance library.

</details>


### [255] [SUPRA: Synthesizing Amyloid PET from MRI and Plasma Biomarkers with an Explicit Regional SUVR Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33061)
*Hanwen Li,Fadi Deek,Zhi Wei,Ruixiang Tang,Feng Liu,Bharat Biswal,Michal Schnaider Beeri,Auriel Willette,Chenxi Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SUPRA 用血浆生物标志物提供淀粉样蛋白信息、用 MRI 提供图像细节，生成接近真实水平的合成淀粉样 PET（SUVR）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 该任务旨在由常规结构 MRI 与易获取的血浆标志物（如 p-tau217）合成淀粉样蛋白 PET 的 SUVR 图像，以替代昂贵且普及度低的淀粉样 PET 检查，输出为绝对 SUVR 尺度的合成图像。

Motivation: 仅凭 MRI 合成淀粉样 PET 属病态问题，因为脑解剖结构几乎不携带淀粉样负荷信息；而现有已发表方法在该设定下性能有限，因此需要引入额外的淀粉样相关信息来源。

Method: 将任务在 MRI 与血浆标志物间分工：先用回归模型将血浆面板映射到区域 SUVR 水平，并以其绘制到受试者 MRI 分割上作为显式先验；再用 3D 网络在 MRI 引导下做残差校正，最终输出绝对 SUVR，且模型可仅用 p-tau217 运行。

实验效果: 在 ADNI（111 对测试）上 PSNR 达 23.4 dB，优于四个已发表模型的至多 20.7 dB；复合皮层、早期蓄积区与纹状体的 SUVR 秩相关为 0.56–0.67（对比方法至多 0.24）。第二队列（143 对，认知未受损、其他血浆平台）为 22.8 dB 与 0.32–0.61（对比至多 21.4 dB 与 0.20）；淀粉样阳性的 AUC 为 0.91 与 0.82（对比至多 0.63）；消融显示血浆提供淀粉样信息、网络提供图像质量。

Abstract: Amyloid positron emission tomography (PET) quantifies brain amyloid-$β$ as a standardized uptake value ratio (SUVR), but is costly and not widely available. Structural magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is routine, and plasma biomarkers such as phosphorylated tau 217 (p-tau217) are becoming practical. Synthesizing amyloid PET from MRI alone is ill-posed, because brain anatomy reveals little about amyloid burden. We present SUPRA (SUVR prior-guided amyloid synthesis), which divides this task between MRI and plasma biomarkers. A regression maps the plasma panel to regional SUVR levels, painted onto the subject's MRI segmentation as an explicit prior. A 3D network then adds a residual correction guided by MRI. The output is in absolute SUVR, and the model also runs on p-tau217 alone. On the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI) cohort (111 test pairs), SUPRA reaches a peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) of 23.4 dB, against at most 20.7 dB for four published models, and rank correlations of 0.56 to 0.67 with true SUVR in the composite cortex, an early-accumulation region and the striatum, against at most 0.24. In a second, cognitively unimpaired cohort with other plasma platforms (143 test pairs), it reaches 22.8 dB and 0.32 to 0.61, against at most 21.4 dB and 0.20. Its area under the curve for amyloid positivity is 0.91 and 0.82, against at most 0.63. Ablations show that plasma supplies the amyloid information and the network the image quality. Combining MRI with a blood test yields synthetic amyloid PET that is realistic and tracks burden across regions.

</details>


### [256] [The Radar-based Activities of Daily Living (R-ADL) Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33088)
*Dominique Regli,Brian C. Williams*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发布 R-ADL 数据集，提供超过八小时、八类日常活动、五个采集位置的雷达标注数据，并给出数据集构建指南与基线结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是利用雷达感知对居家日常活动（ADLs）进行识别，以支持居家人员（尤其是老年人）的安全与健康监测；输入为雷达传感信号，输出为活动类别，目标是在保护隐私的前提下提升活动识别性能。

Motivation: 现有雷达活动识别工作受限于大规模数据集匮乏，且已有数据在规模、房间配置多样性（多局限于单一位置）和活动层级（多聚焦原子动作而非高层ADL）三方面存在不足。

Method: 构建 R-ADL 数据集：提供超过八小时、八类活动的标注记录，在两室两卫公寓的五个不同位置采集；通过对学术研究、健康调查工具和临床试验中ADL优先级的分析确定具有临床意义的活动；并系统给出实验设计、数据采集与标注策略、从原始数据生成数据集的后期处理流程，以及在标准机器学习架构上的基线实验。

实验效果: 摘要未报告具体实验数值结果，仅说明在标准机器学习架构上给出了基线结果，并强调该数据集在数据规模、采集位置多样性和活动层级上的扩展。

Abstract: Monitoring Activities of Daily Living (ADLs) supports safety and well-being for people living at home, especially older adults, and radar sensing is gaining increasing attention in the field of activity recognition for its ability to ensure privacy in the home while also surpassing technical limitations of other sensing modalities. However, radar-based activity recognition remains constrained by the limited availability of large-scale datasets. We introduce the Radar-based Activities of Daily Living (R-ADL) dataset to addresses the gaps identified in existing work: 1) it expands the scale of available data by providing over eight hours of annotated recordings in which a person performs eight distinct activities; 2) it overcomes room configuration limitations by recording from five different locations in a two-bedroom, two-bathroom apartment; and 3) it extends beyond primitive actions to focus on high-level ADLs. In addition to the dataset, this paper introduces practical guidance for future ADL dataset construction. We conducted a detailed analysis of how ADLs are prioritized across academic research, health survey tools, and clinical trials to identify clinically significant ADLs. Furthermore, we provide our experimental design, data collection and annotation strategies, post-processing pipeline for forming the R-ADL dataset from raw data, and baseline results on standard machine learning architectures. The R-ADL dataset serves as a valuable resource for advancing ADL recognition systems as we continue building larger and more diverse datasets.

</details>


### [257] [EventDiffusion: Learning Native Event Stream Distribution from Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33096)
*Karthik Sundar Senthilkumar,Christopher Thirgood,Simon Hadfield*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EventDiffusion 是一个分层生成框架，直接从真实录制中学习原生异步事件流的条件分布，并配套提出事件流真实感度量 CEFS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 事件相机以高时间分辨率异步记录亮度变化，但真实录制稀缺，因此需要由稀疏 RGB 观测生成事件流，以支撑数据驱动研究。输入为 RGB 帧，输出为密集异步事件流（活动量、像素位置与时间戳）。

Motivation: 稀疏 RGB 观测无法唯一确定中间事件：相同帧可能对应活动、极性和时序不同的多条事件流；现有模拟器依赖预设的传感器动态与估计的亮度轨迹，其假设限制了生成事件流的多样性与保真度。

Method: 将生成分解为三个概率过程：Event Activity Diffusion（EAD）学习随机离散事件计数；Event Spatial Allocation（ESA）在保持基数不变的前提下把计数分配到各像素；Event Timing Diffusion（ETD）学习连续异步时间戳分布。该分解绕开对亮度轨迹和固定传感器模型的依赖，直接建模真实事件流的条件分布。另提出 CEFS 真实感指标，结合事件活动与极性、多尺度时空结构和时间保真度。

实验效果: 摘要报告：在所有数据集上，EventDiffusion 的准确率为 SOTA 方法的两倍，并在不同相机、任意分辨率下表现出鲁棒性。

Abstract: Event cameras capture asynchronous brightness changes at high temporal resolution, but scarce recordings constrain data-driven research. Sparse RGB observations cannot uniquely determine intervening events since identical frames may correspond to streams with different activity, polarities, and timing. Existing simulators largely rely on prescribed sensor dynamics and estimated brightness trajectories, whose assumptions limit the diversity and fidelity of plausible event streams. We introduce EventDiffusion, a hierarchical generative framework that directly learns the conditional distribution of native asynchronous event streams from real recordings. EventDiffusion decomposes generation into three probabilistic processes: Event Activity Diffusion (EAD) learns stochastic discrete event counts; Event Spatial Allocation (ESA) distributes these counts to individual pixels while preserving cardinality; and Event Timing Diffusion (ETD) learns continuous asynchronous timestamp distributions. We also introduce CEFS, an event-stream realism metric combining event activity and polarity with multiscale space-time structure and temporal fidelity. Across all datasets, EventDiffusion achieves double the accuracy of SOTA methods and demonstrates robustness across different cameras at any resolution.

</details>


### [258] [RenGen: Generating Images with as Many Tokens as the Scene Needs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33109)
*Savya Khosla,Jay Mahajan,Akin Tuna Sakalli,Derek Hoiem,David Forsyth,Alex Schwing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RenGen 通过在预训练区域编码器的隐空间中用数量可变的区域 token 生成图像，以场景复杂度而非图像质量换取更少 token，从而在质量与计算之间推进帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是图像生成：给定少量或较多 token，从零生成简单场景或复杂场景图像，目标是在简单场景上节省时间与算力、同时不损失真实感。

Motivation: 现有潜变量生成器将每张图表示为固定网格的 patch token，导致简单图像与复杂图像生成开销相同；而此前按内容自适应改变 token 数量的方法在 token 数减少时会损失图像质量。

Method: RenGen 改为在预训练区域编码器的隐空间中生成，每个 token 对应图像的一个区域，由解码器渲染回像素；由于区域形状与位置可变，需同时编码"内容"与"空间范围"。具体做法是在池化前向编码器特征加入位置嵌入以编码空间范围，并在生成时将每个 token 锚定到单个随机采样点，由生成器据此推断区域的形状、大小与内容；采样时锚点数量即决定场景复杂度与计算量的权衡。

实验效果: 在 ImageNet 256×256 上，平均 88 个 token 时 gFID 达 1.34，为可变 token 数方法中最优；以 2.5× 更低延迟接近 SOTA patch 方法 RAEv2 的质量；gFID 在不同场景复杂度下基本持平，而延迟在最简单与最繁忙场景间相差 2.4×；自编码器以 2.9× 更少 token 取得接近 RAEv2 的重建（rFID 0.32 vs. 0.30）。

Abstract: We present RenGen, a latent diffusion model that generates simple scenes from a few tokens and busy scenes from many, saving time and compute on simple scenes without losing realism. Most latent generators represent every image as a fixed grid of patch tokens, so a simple image costs as much to generate as a complex one. Prior methods that vary the token count through content-adaptive tokenization lose image quality as the count shrinks. RenGen instead trades scene complexity, rather than image quality, for fewer tokens. It does so by generating in the latent space of a pretrained region encoder, where each token represents one region of the image, and a decoder renders these region tokens back into pixels. Unlike patch tokens, region tokens cover areas of varying shape and position, so each token must capture both what its region contains and where it lies on the image canvas. We encode this spatial extent by adding positional embeddings to the encoder features before pooling, and we generate it by anchoring each token to a single randomly sampled point, from which the generator infers the region's shape, size, and content. At sampling time, the number of anchor points sets the trade-off between scene complexity and compute. On ImageNet $256{\times}256$, RenGen reaches a gFID of 1.34 with 88 tokens per image on average, the best among methods that vary their token count. It approaches the quality of RAEv2, a state-of-the-art patch-based model, at $2.5{\times}$ lower latency, advancing the Pareto frontier of generation quality versus compute. RenGen's gFID stays flat across scene complexity while latency varies by $2.4{\times}$ between simplest and busiest scenes, and its autoencoder reconstructs nearly as well as RAEv2 (rFID 0.32 vs. 0.30) with $2.9{\times}$ fewer tokens.

</details>


### [259] [BriConstrain: Geometrically Constrained Decoding for Autoregressive Brick Assembly Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33135)
*Nadav Magar,Amir Hertz,Ariel Shamir,Or Patashnik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BriConstrain 提出一种受约束解码方法，通过在采样前收紧预训练语言模型的下一词概率分布，将 LEGO® 装配生成引导至无碰撞且有效连接的放置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是由文本生成 LEGO® 装配（含零件与连接关系），输入为文本指令与逐步预测的零件放置，输出为可实际拼装、零件不相互碰撞且均附着于已有结构的装配序列。目标是提升生成构建的物理有效性。

Motivation: 现有“拒绝采样—重采样”无效放置的做法会丢弃已解码的 token，且无法保证最终构建有效；而由于放置有效性依赖非预先确定的构建状态，无法编译为固定语法，只能动态校验；同时有效放置空间组合爆炸、不可穷举枚举，使得采样前校验极具挑战。

Method: 关键思路是约束“零件如何连接”而非“哪些零件相连”，从而缩小搜索空间。具体步骤：给定模型对当前待放置零件的预测及其想要连接的已放置父零件，约束模型对二者连接方式的选择；借助预计算的几何 oracle 在连接方式空间中枚举候选并过滤掉碰撞选项；最后对剩余有效候选偏置模型的下一词分布后再采样。

实验效果: 在两种 LEGO® 格式与模型上评测：BrickNet（显式标注连通性）无碰撞构建率由 31% 提升至 91%，作者提出的隐式连通 LDraw 格式（ICLF）由 15% 提升至 65%；两种格式上均优于拒绝采样，且解码开销约为其 60%。

Abstract: Language models can generate LEGO$^\circledR$ assemblies from text, but their builds often contain parts that collide or attach to nothing. The usual approach of rejecting and resampling invalid placements discards decoded tokens without guaranteeing a valid build. We present BriConstrain, a constrained decoding method that restricts a pretrained language model's next-token probabilities before sampling, steering it towards valid placements. Because the validity of a brick placement depends on the non-predetermined build state, it cannot be compiled into a fixed grammar and must be checked dynamically. However, checking the validity of a brick before sampling is challenging as the space of valid placements is combinatorially large and cannot be enumerated exhaustively. Our key idea is to narrow the search space by constraining how parts attach rather than which parts attach to each other. Concretely, given the model's predictions of the currently placed part, and the previously placed parent it wants to connect to, we constrain the model's choice of how they attach. This allows us to span the space of part attachments, using a precomputed geometric oracle, and to effectively filter out colliding options. With valid attachment candidates in hand, we bias the model's next-token distribution towards the remaining valid candidates. We evaluate our method on two LEGO$^\circledR$ formats and models: BrickNet, which annotates connectivity explicitly, and our proposed Implicit Connectivity LDraw Format (ICLF), which uses numeric values for placements and leaves connectivity implicit. Our method raises collision-free builds from 31% to 91% on BrickNet and from 15% to 65% on ICLF, and outperforms rejection sampling on both formats at about 60% of its decoding cost.

</details>


### [260] [Physicl Scenes: An Artist-created 3D Dataset for Learning Perception and Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33137)
*Yueh-Cheng Liu,Snehal Jauhri,Matthias Nießner,Angela Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文发布Physicl Scenes——一个由艺术家创作与策展的6,499个3D场景数据集，覆盖室内、公共空间与室外场景，并提供几何、PBR材质及物体级/部件级语义标注，用于视觉与具身学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 该工作面向视觉与具身AI的联合监督训练数据构建问题，输入为艺术家构建的3D场景（含几何、物理材质与环境布局），输出为带物体级和部件级语义标注的数据集，目标是为逆渲染、3D重建、场景理解与移动操作等任务提供训练与评测基础。

Motivation: 现有替代性合成数据集在几何细节、物理材质、环境构图的真实性以及物体/部件级语义标注的联合完备性上不足，难以同时支撑视觉任务与具身操作任务的训练与细粒度评测，因此需要更高质量、带联合监督的场景数据。

Method: 论文构建并策展了6,499个高质量3D场景，融合精细几何、基于物理的材质与精心构图的环境，并附物体级与部件级语义标注以形成联合监督；随后在逆渲染、3D重建、场景理解与移动操作四类任务上以该数据训练当前最优方法，并利用部件标注构建移动操作基准，评测视觉-语言-动作模型在真实布局场景中的物理抓取放置及抓取位置评分。

实验效果: 摘要未报告具体数值结果；仅定性指出在上述四类任务中，使用该场景数据训练当前最优方法相比其他合成数据集取得性能提升，并新增了带抓取位置评分的移动操作基准。

Abstract: We present Physicl Scenes, a high-quality artist-created and curated dataset of 6,499 3D scenes spanning household rooms, public interiors, and outdoor spaces. The dataset combines detailed geometry, physically based materials, and carefully composed environments with object- and part-level semantic annotations, providing rich, joint supervision for visual and embodied learning. We demonstrate its value as training data across inverse rendering, 3D reconstruction, scene understanding, and mobile manipulation. Across these tasks, training state-of-the-art methods with our scenes improves performance over alternative synthetic datasets. In addition, we introduce a mobile manipulation benchmark, evaluating vision-language-action models performing physical pick and place in diverse 3D scenes with realistic layouts, and using our part annotations to also score where each object is grasped. Our dataset will be released publicly to facilitate research in visual and embodied AI.

</details>


### [261] [Lost on Campus: Evaluating Embodied Spatial Reasoning of Vision-Language Models in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33145)
*Zehan Zheng,Yanyuan Chen,Deming Li,Yutao Tang,Jianwen Xie,Alan Yuille,Jieneng Chen,Cheng Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Lost on Campus 基准，基于 3D 高斯泼溅重建的大规模真实户外校园环境，用统一"推理–动作"框架评估视觉语言模型的具身空间推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是评估视觉语言模型（VLMs）的具身空间推理（ESR）能力，这是其在真实具身任务中落地的关键。输入为多模态指令与大规模真实户外 3D 场景，输出为诊断性问答答案与闭环导航行为，目标是在真实连续环境中忠实衡量空间推理水平。

Motivation: 现有基准多在静态图像或合成室内环境中评估空间推理，任务中语义捷径即可奏效，无法真实反映"in the wild"场景下的空间推理能力，导致评估失真、难以诊断模型瓶颈。

Method: 构建 Lost on Campus 基准：用 3D Gaussian Splatting 重建大规模真实户外 3D 环境；设计统一的推理–动作评估框架，将孤立推理的诊断式 QA 与多模态指令下的闭环交互导航（主动推理）相结合；并按六个能力维度做细粒度诊断——动作落地、空间前瞻、度量感知、目标导向规划、自我–客体中心定位、时空一致性。

实验效果: 实验发现：(i) 相比室内场景，真实户外环境对空间推理要求显著更高，现有模型表现较差；(ii) VLMs 仍存在细粒度视觉对齐困难，空间前瞻与自我–客体中心定位失败是主要瓶颈；(iii) 提升多模态交互能力与长时程空间推理对具身智能发展至关重要。摘要未报告具体数值指标。

Abstract: Embodied Spatial Reasoning (ESR) is central to deploying Vision-Language Models (VLMs) in real-world embodied tasks, yet remains inadequately evaluated. Existing benchmarks primarily assess spatial reasoning in static images or synthetic indoor environments, where semantic shortcuts often suffice, making it difficult to faithfully evaluate spatial reasoning capabilities in the wild. In light of this, we present Lost on Campus, a benchmark for evaluating ESR in large-scale real-world outdoor 3D environments reconstructed by 3D Gaussian Splatting. Our benchmark introduces a unified reasoning-action evaluation framework that seamlessly integrates diagnostic QA for isolated reasoning with closed-loop interactive navigation for active reasoning under multimodal instructions. To enable fine-grained diagnosis, we evaluate six capability aspects of ESR: action grounding, spatial foresight, metric awareness, goal-directed planning, ego-allocentric localization, and spatio-temporal consistency. Extensive experiments reveal that: (i) Compared to indoor settings, real-world outdoor environments impose substantially higher demands on spatial reasoning, where existing models exhibit poor performance; (ii) VLMs still struggle with fine-grained visual alignment, with failures in spatial foresight and ego-allocentric localization emerging as primary bottlenecks; (iii) Improving multimodal interaction capabilities and long-term spatial reasoning is crucial for advancing embodied intelligence. These findings highlight that faithful evaluation of ESR demands benchmarks tightly coupling perception, reasoning and action within realistic and continuous environments.

</details>


### [262] [One Take, Many Cameras: Audio-Grounded Shot Synthesis for Dialogue Re-Cinematography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33147)
*Anthony Chen,Naomi Ken Korem,Matan Ben-Yosef,Tavi Halperin,Urska Jelercic,Ofir Bibi,Daniel Cohen-Or,Or Patashnik,Maneesh Agrawala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"对话再摄影"任务，借助语音的视角不变性，从单段广角对话视频合成新的镜头视角，并用说话人感知音频门控消除多人场景中的语音泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 对话场景通常需要在多个视角（特写、中景、过肩镜头）之间切换，但获取多机位素材成本高、往往不可得。本文研究"对话再摄影"：输入一段单视角广角对话视频，输出由目标帧指定视角的新增镜头，目标是合成唇动与语音一致的额外机位画面。

Motivation: 现有视频生成器仅依赖视觉线索合成新镜头，当源视频几乎看不到嘴唇时，生成的唇动无法与语音匹配；而多人场景中预训练音视频模型又会用同一语音驱动所有可见人脸，导致静默听者仿说说话人台词的"语音泄漏"。

Method: 关键洞察是语音具有视角不变性，可约束任意目标视角下的唇动。据此改造预训练音视频扩散模型，以指定视角的目标帧、含表演的源视频和驱动唇动的语音音频为条件生成新镜头；再引入免训练的说话人感知音频门控模块，使音频只驱动目标镜头中真正说话的人脸，从而抑制语音泄漏。

实验效果: 在覆盖多样视角与景别的电影级配对视角基准上，相对最强基线 Wan 3.0，感知误差降低59%、关键点误差降低56%、语音泄漏降低97%，并在真实场景中保持稳健。

Abstract: Creating a dialogue scene typically requires cutting between multiple views (e.g., close-ups, medium shots, and over-the-shoulder shots) of the actors. Yet capturing such coverage is costly and in many settings unavailable. We study dialogue re-cinematography: synthesizing additional shots from a single wide-angle dialogue video. Existing video generators can synthesize new shots, but rely on visual cues alone, so when the source video barely shows the lips, the generated lip motion fails to match the speech. Our key observation is that the speech audio is view-invariant: it constrains lip motion in any target view. Building on this insight, we adapt a pretrained audio-visual diffusion model to generate the new shot from a target frame that specifies the view, a source video that contains the performance, and the speech audio that drives lip motion. In multi-person scenes, however, pretrained audio-visual models drive every visible face with the same speech, causing speech leakage, where silent listeners mouth the speaker's words. We therefore introduce speaker-aware audio gating, a training-free module that lets the audio drive only the faces that speak in the target shot. On a cinematic paired-view benchmark spanning diverse viewpoints and framings, our method lowers perceptual error by 59%, landmark error by 56%, and speech leakage by 97% relative to the strongest baseline, Wan 3.0. Our method also remains robust on in-the-wild scenes, highlighting its potential for film post-production.

</details>


### [263] [BioLoom: Scaling Biomedical Vision-Language Models by Learning from the Structure of Scientific Articles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33151)
*Yuwei Yang,Xiaoxiao Sun,Alejandro Lozano,Yuan-Sen Ting,Yuhui Yuan,Liang Zheng,Serena Yeung-Levy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出利用科学文献自身结构（图、子图、图注与正文）构造多层级图文监督，构建 Loom52M 数据集并训练出 BioLoom 系列生物医学视觉-语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是训练生物医学视觉-语言模型（VLM）的对比式图文预训练，输入为科学文献中的图表面板与其文本描述，输出为可做零样本分类与图文检索的模型，目标是在现有数据规模之外从数据中提取更丰富的表征。

Motivation: 现有工作主要依赖不断扩大图文数据规模，且将图、子图、图注与正文视为相互独立的图文对，忽略了它们在文献内部的结构关系，导致监督信号浪费、区分度不足。

Method: 将文献结构关系写入对比监督：保留精确匹配的正对，对相关对（如某面板与同级面板图注）赋予较弱正监督；对同内容类型的跨文章负样本加权以增强细粒度判别；并用视觉内容、原始图注及提及段落富化面板描述。以“面板—富化描述”“整图—原始图注”配对构建 52.4M 规模的 Loom52M 数据集，据此训练 BioLoom（DFN-2B）。

实验效果: 在 43 个数据集、10 个领域的 67 项零样本分类任务上，BioLoom（DFN-2B）取得 56.82% 的领域均衡平均准确率，高于 MedPMC-CLIP 的 48.77%；在 BioLoomBench 与 BioMedFlickr 上双向平均 Recall@1 分别为 63.20% 和 10.59%，对应最强基线 BiomedCLIP 与 MedPMC-CLIP 为 46.37% 和 7.55%。

Abstract: The development of biomedical vision-language models (VLMs) with contrastive image-text pretraining has been driven by scaling to increasingly large and diverse image-text datasets. Beyond scaling, further progress can come from learning richer representations from existing data. We hypothesize that the structure of scientific literature itself provides an additional source of supervision: figures, panels, captions, and accompanying article text encode rich relationships across modalities. Yet, treating these as independent image-text pairs overlooks their relationships. To address this limitation, we incorporate these structural relationships into contrastive image-text supervision. We preserve exact image-text matches while introducing weaker positive supervision for related pairs, such as a panel and the caption of a sibling panel. We upweight cross-article negatives of the same content type to encourage finer discrimination. We also enrich panel descriptions using visual content, original figure captions, and mentioning paragraphs. Pairing panels with these descriptions and whole figures with their original captions yields Loom52M, a dataset of 52.4 million image-text pairs spanning scientific charts and biomedical images. Using Loom52M, BioLoom (DFN-2B) achieves 56.82% domain-balanced mean accuracy across 67 zero-shot classification tasks from 43 datasets in 10 domains, compared with 48.77% for MedPMC-CLIP. On BioLoomBench with concise captions and BioMedFlickr, it achieves bidirectional mean Recall@1 of 63.20% and 10.59%, versus 46.37% and 7.55% for the respective strongest evaluated baselines, BiomedCLIP and MedPMC-CLIP. Code, datasets, benchmarks, and models are available at https://bioloom-clip.github.io/.

</details>


### [264] [PointX: Multi-View Consistent Intrinsics by Querying Surface Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33157)
*Miguel Farinha,Sam Sartor,Iliyan Georgiev,Giuseppe Vecchio,Ronald Clark,Michael Fischer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PointX 提出一种前馈模型，将内蕴属性预测从逐像素改为逐 3D 表面点，从而实现跨视角天然一致的分解、重光照与编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是从图像中恢复场景的 albedo、粗糙度、金属度与法向等内蕴属性以及光照辐照度；输入是一组未标定（unposed）的多视图图像（也可为单图），输出是表面点的内蕴属性及对应的逐视图映射，目标是获得跨视角一致且可用于重光照与编辑的分解结果。

Motivation: 内蕴分解本身具有歧义性，而现有逐像素预测方法会对同一表面点在不同视角给出不同分解结果，导致视角间不一致，难以支持一致的重光照与编辑。

Method: PointX 采用简单的前馈架构：多视图 Transformer 编码一组未位姿图像；对任意查询的三维表面点，将该点在各可见视图中对应的 token 进行池化（pooling），并仅解码一次得到该点的内蕴属性。因此点云或网格的每个点只获得单一预测，按构造即跨视角一致；将每个像素视为所见表面点的查询，同一模型还能输出单图或多次视图的逐视图映射。

实验效果: 摘要未报告具体数值指标，仅给出定性结论：PointX 的一致性显著优于现有方法，单图精度具竞争力，且随视图数量增加而提升；逐视图映射在估计几何与对应精度范围内跨视角一致。

Abstract: The appearance of a scene is determined by the albedo, roughness, metallicity and normal of every point on its surface, and by the light that reaches it, its irradiance. Recovering these intrinsics from images is ambiguous, and methods that predict them per pixel give the same surface point a different decomposition in every view. We introduce PointX, a simple feed-forward model that predicts intrinsics per 3D surface point instead. A multi-view transformer encodes a set of unposed images, and for any queried surface point, PointX pools the point's tokens across the views in which it appears and decodes the point's intrinsics once. Each point of a 3D reconstruction of the scene, such as a point cloud or a mesh, thus receives a single prediction, which is exactly consistent across views by construction and enables view-consistent relighting and editing. The same model also predicts per-view maps, from one image or many, by querying the point seen at every pixel. These maps agree across views up to the precision of the estimated geometry and correspondences. PointX is significantly more consistent than existing methods, while remaining competitive in accuracy from a single image and improving as views are added. Project page: https://pointx-paper.github.io/

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [265] [A User-Item Aware Encoding Framework for Short Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32187)
*Weihui Deng,He Liu,Bin Wang,Xiaocheng Li,Deliang Fu,Zhen Wang*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 提出 UIAE（用户-物品感知编码）多码率阶梯组编码方法，按用户群体自适应优化短视频编码以最大化 QoE，并已上线处理千万级日转码任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 针对海量用户生成短视频的转码场景，输入为视频内容与用户-物品关系及上下文消费模式，输出为按人群划分的多码率阶梯编码策略，目标是在异构内容与消费关系下最大化终端观看者的体验质量（QoE）。

Motivation: PGC 时代的传统编码方法难以应对短视频的两大特性：日上传量达数亿级、以及内容质量与消费场景高度异构的用户-消费者关系，因此需要一种能按用户群体差异化调整的编码方案。

Method: UIAE 包含三个组件：1）通过规则式或 DNN 模型建立用户-物品关系；2）基于上下文消费模式，为各子人群构建最大化 QoE 的局部优化模型；3）通过层次化模型整合推导全局最优编码策略，从而以分群局部优化加层次聚合的方式替代单一全局编码。

实验效果: 摘要未报告具体基准、对比方法或量化指标；仅定性说明 UIAE 能为不同上下文画像的用户群自适应调整编码结果并保证最优 QoE，且已部署至线上流水线，日均处理数千万个视频转码任务。

Abstract: Traditional encoding methods from the Professional Generated Content (PGC) era face significant challenges due to two unique characteristics of user-generated short videos: massive daily uploads (reaching hundreds of millions) and heterogeneous content-consumer relationships (varying video quality and diverse consumer contexts). To address these challenges, we propose UIAE (User-Item Aware Encoding), a novel multiple bitrate ladder group encoding method comprising three key components: 1) Establishing user-item relationships via rule-based or DNN-based models; 2) Developing local optimization models maximizing Quality of Experience (QoE) for sub-populations based on contextual consumption patterns; 3) Deriving globally optimal encoding strategies through hierarchical model integration. Experimental results demonstrate that the UIAE framework adaptively adjusts encoding outcomes for distinct user groups with varying context profiles, ensuring optimal QoE for end viewers. We have deployed the UIAE method into the online pipeline, handling tens of millions of video transcoding tasks daily.

</details>


### [266] [Residual Guidance for Real-Time Interactive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32820)
*NaHyeon Park,Kyeong-Hwan Moon,Youjin Jeon,Hyunjung Shim*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 论文提出免训练的残差引导（RG）方法，通过缓存外的校正信号缓解"上下文惯性"，使实时流式视频生成在切换提示词后能及时响应新提示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务为实时交互式视频生成：用户在生成过程中修改提示词以引导演化中的视频，流式模型逐块（chunk）生成视频。目标是让模型在提示切换后输出与新风提示一致的内容，同时保持视觉连续性。

Motivation: 现有流式模型在提示切换后仍延续旧提示。作者将失败归因于"上下文惯性"：前一块作为强先验压制新提示；即使缓存仅保留前一块惯性依旧存在，而直接丢弃上下文又会破坏视觉连续性，使编辑缓存类方法受限。

Method: 不编辑缓存，而是在缓存外度量校正项：将上一块重新加噪后，在新提示下、不使用缓存地做一次去噪，得到残差；再将该残差加到下一块早期去噪步（此时内容基本定型、新提示受压制最强）。方法无需训练、无需逐模型调参，每块仅增加一次前向。

实验效果: 摘要未给出具体基准或数值。定性/对比性结论为：在单次切换到长序列多次切换的设置下，RG 在包括自带切换机制的现有流式模型上显著提升提示切换能力，并大体保持视觉质量。

Abstract: Real-time interactive video generation allows users to change the prompt during generation to steer the evolving video. However, existing streaming models, which generate videos chunk by chunk, often keep following the previous prompt after a switch. We trace this failure to context inertia, where the previous chunk acts as a strong prior that suppresses the new prompt. Furthermore, we find that this inertia persists even when the cache retains only the previous chunk, yet discarding the context breaks visual continuity, which limits approaches that edit the cache. Rather than editing the cache, we propose Residual Guidance (RG), a simple, training-free method that counteracts context inertia with a correction measured outside the cache. Specifically, RG denoises the re-noised previous chunk once under the new prompt without the cache and adds the resulting residual to the early denoising steps of the next chunk, where its content is largely decided and the new prompt is most suppressed. Across settings ranging from a single switch to long multi-switch generation, RG substantially improves prompt switching capability on existing streaming models, including those with dedicated switching mechanisms, while largely retaining visual quality. As it requires no training or per-model tuning and adds only one forward pass per chunk, RG can be easily integrated into existing real-time video generation pipelines.

</details>


### [267] [MSRGF: Multi-Source Reconstruction with Reference-Guided Fusion for Incomplete Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32860)
*Yan Zhuang,Minhao Liu,Yanru Zhang,Li Ma,Weilong Chen,Xingyu Wu,Jiawen Deng,Fuji Ren*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: MSRGF通过保留各源独立的模态重建估计并以参考引导方式融合，提升不完整多模态情感分析性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 不完整多模态情感分析需在文本、声学、视觉特征部分缺失时预测情感。输入为可观测模态特征，输出为情感预测结果，目标是缓解缺失信息带来的性能下降。

Motivation: 现有重建方法用可用模态估计缺失特征后直接融合，但不同可用模态对同一模态会给出不同估计，直接融合未能充分利用这些估计之间的关系，导致信息利用不充分。

Method: 提出MSRGF，包含三部分：源级重建（SWR）用模态特定查询与共享生成器，从每个可用模态分别为各模态生成独立估计；参考引导融合（RGF）依据估计间关系与可观测特征构建加权参考，用差异选择性精化，并在可用时并入观测特征；全局上下文调制（GCM）用全局上下文调整模态摘要以进行情感预测。该方法通过保留多源估计并建模其关系，解决直接融合信息利用不足的问题。

实验效果: 在三个数据集上优于现有方法；MOSEI上随机模态内缺失平均准确率提升1.9%，随机整模态缺失提升0.6%，固定整模态缺失提升1.3%。

Abstract: Incomplete multimodal sentiment analysis (MSA) predicts sentiment from textual, acoustic, and visual features when some observations are missing. Existing reconstruction methods estimate missing features from available modalities and combine the resulting information for sentiment prediction. However, different available modalities can produce distinct estimates of the same modality, while direct fusion often leaves the relationships among these estimates underused. We propose MSRGF (Multi-Source Reconstruction with Reference-Guided Fusion), which retains separate estimates and exploits their relationships during fusion. Specifically, MSRGF comprises three components: (1) Source-Wise Reconstruction (SWR), which uses modality-specific queries and a shared generator to produce a separate estimate of each modality from every available modality; (2) Reference-Guided Fusion (RGF), which uses relationships among estimates and available observations to construct a weighted reference, selectively refines it using their differences, and incorporates observed features when available; and (3) Global Context Modulation (GCM), which uses global context to adjust modality summaries for sentiment prediction. Extensive experiments on three datasets show that MSRGF outperforms existing methods, achieving average accuracy improvements of 1.9% on MOSEI under random within-modality missingness, 0.6% under random whole-modality missingness and 1.3% under fixed whole-modality missingness.

</details>
